**[00:04:57.040] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000059)  
Thank you, Comoughs.

**[00:04:59.933] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000060)  
Sa ating mga magigiting na miyembro ng Kagawaran ng Tanggulang Pambansa.

**[00:05:05.317] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000061)  
Tangsandatahang lakas ng Pilipinas.

**[00:05:07.223] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000062)  
sa pumumuno ng ating kalihim, Secretary Gilberto C. Chidoro,

**[00:05:16.049] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000063)  
AFP Chief of Staff General Antonio Nafarete,

**[00:05:20.842] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000064)  
at mga pinuno ng ating mga kawanihan at attached agencies,

**[00:05:26.917] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000065)  
maligayang pagdating po dito sa Kongreso at isang magandang umaga po sa ating lahat.

**[00:05:34.080] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000066)  
It is my privilege to welcome the Department of National Defense

**[00:05:38.400] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000067)  
to the Committee on Appropriations as we begin deliberations on its proposed Fiscal Year 2027 budget.

**[00:05:47.120] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000068)  
For Fiscal Year 2027, the Department of National Defense is proposing a budget of $324.63 billion,

**[00:05:57.520] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000069)  
reflecting the government's continuing commitment to our national security,

**[00:06:03.280] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000070)  
territorial integrity, and the protection of our people.

**[00:06:08.240] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000071)  
This is complemented by $132.59 billion,

**[00:06:14.880] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000072)  
of which $50 billion is allocated for the revised AFP modernization program

**[00:06:22.319] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000073)  
to support the acquisition and the upgrading of equipment and systems necessary

**[00:06:28.400] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000074)  
to keep our armed forces capable and responsive to the security challenges of our time.

**[00:06:36.080] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000076)  
The remaining $82.59 billion is allocated to the equally important pensions

**[00:06:42.880] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000077)  
of our veterans and AFP retirees.

**[00:06:47.199] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000078)  
These are significant investments, but national defense is not measured by numbers alone.

**[00:06:54.880] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000079)  
It is ultimately about the men and women who stand ready to serve our country.

**[00:07:01.599] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000080)  
Our maritime domain remains contested.

**[00:07:06.639] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000081)  
And the men and women who hold our position there have done so with a steadiness

**[00:07:14.240] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000082)  
this country has to pause to acknowledge.

**[00:07:19.280] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000083)  
Through the leadership of Secretary Chodoro,

**[00:07:22.880] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000084)  
our partnerships with like-minded nations have deepened,

**[00:07:27.199] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000085)  
and our forces have carried the weight professionally and well.

**[00:07:35.759] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000086)  
Our soldiers continue to bear internal security,

**[00:07:40.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000087)  
and in every community struck by earthquake, by flood, by storm,

**[00:07:48.400] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000088)  
it is the men in uniform that arrive first.

**[00:07:52.960] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000089)  
I say that not as a matter of record, but because I've seen that firsthand.

**[00:08:00.240] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000090)  
In the middle of the series of earthquakes and monsoon rains

**[00:08:03.520] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000091)  
that struck our communities in the 2nd District of Sultan Kudarat,

**[00:08:09.199] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000092)  
that is what this budget is for.

**[00:08:12.639] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000093)  
Not platforms, not line items, but the fact that when this country is at its most frightened,

**[00:08:21.039] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000094)  
it sends the men and women of the Department of National Defense forward.

**[00:08:27.039] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000095)  
And they go.

**[00:08:29.039] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000096)  
And when they do, we must give and arm them with the capabilities to do so.

**[00:08:36.399] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000097)  
It means seeing the modernization of the armed forces through.

**[00:08:41.120] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000098)  
Republic Act No. 10349, or the Revised AFP Modernization Act of 2012,

**[00:08:47.760] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000099)  
approaches the end of its term, and the work it began is not finished.

**[00:08:54.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000100)  
Congress and this representation will support a modernization program carried to completion

**[00:09:01.600] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000101)  
rather than left at its most expensive and at least useful stage, which is halfway.

**[00:09:10.959] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000103)  
That law and how the ongoing modernization program is financed

**[00:09:16.480] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000104)  
are among the most consequential questions of national security

**[00:09:20.800] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000105)  
that should be at the forefront of national policy discussions.

**[00:09:25.760] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000106)  
At an age where threats to our sovereignty have morphed from conventional military threats

**[00:09:33.920] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000107)  
to hybrid tactics, cyber intrusion, disinformation,

**[00:09:41.680] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000108)  
gray zone activities,

**[00:09:43.440] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000109)  
we need to support investments in our technical capacities

**[00:09:47.920] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000110)  
and in those that safeguard our defense establishment in all its forms.

**[00:09:54.399] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000111)  
Let us therefore ensure that this fiscal year 2027 national budget

**[00:10:00.560] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000112)  
supports a stronger, more modern armed forces,

**[00:10:05.360] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000113)  
a more empowered Office of Civil Defense,

**[00:10:15.360] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000117)  
Thank you.

**[00:10:18.560] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000118)  
May we now invite Secretary Teodoro to proceed with the presentation of the budget of the Department of National Defense.

**[00:10:25.410] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000119)

**[00:10:29.290] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000120)

**[00:10:52.090] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000128)

**[00:11:05.710] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000129)

**[00:11:08.390] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000130)

**[00:11:12.650] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000131)

**[00:11:20.490] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000132)

**[00:11:28.790] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000133)

**[00:11:33.930] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000134)

**[00:11:39.830] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000135)

**[00:11:42.650] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000137)

**[00:11:46.490] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000138)

**[00:11:47.090] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000139)

**[00:11:53.750] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000140)  
So I'm very,

**[00:11:54.590] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000141)

**[00:12:04.550] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000142)

**[00:12:05.210] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000143)

**[00:12:12.350] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000145)

We, we, uh, uh, of course,

**[00:12:27.310] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000148)  
the mandate is already known under Executive Order 292 and other laws of the department.

I propose,

**[00:12:39.550] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000150)

**[00:12:41.870] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000151)  
that we go to the major thrusts,

**[00:12:45.450] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000152)

**[00:12:53.310] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000153)  
So, for the major thrusts,

**[00:12:55.510] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000154)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:12:59.010] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000155)  
For sovereignty and territorial integrity,

**[00:13:05.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000156)  
which is, uh, a [unclear] mission area,

**[00:13:08.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000157)  
which is a new mission area for us,

**[00:13:11.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000158)  
given the fact that now we realize that we have

**[00:13:14.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000159)  
2.4 million square kilometers of maritime domain that we must, uh... uh, make, uh, have overwatch over.

**[00:13:28.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000160)  
And secondly to try to preempt any efforts to remove from the jurisdiction of the Philippines the exploration and exploitation of the same.

**[00:13:46.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000161)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:13:47.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000162)  
Although the major focus right now, naturally, is the West Philippine Sea as an area of contest.

**[00:13:56.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000163)  
A bigger domain actually is the Pacific Ocean and the Philippine Rise of which we must start already building our infrastructure,

**[00:14:06.330] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000164)  
start our logistical bases in central areas with access that are accessible to the equipment that are germane to the armed forces of the Philippines,

**[00:14:19.330] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000165)  
and proceed to constructing our strategic bases which will allow us to have presence in that maritime domain.

**[00:14:27.390] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000166)  
To even make sure that we have ready and reliable supply chains,

**[00:14:34.090] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000167)  
which is an essential exercise this day and age.

**[00:14:39.770] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000168)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:14:41.390] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000169)  
An area which we cannot vacate for quite some time,

**[00:14:51.490] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000170)  
particularly as we know that it is essential for us to have robust and

**[00:15:00.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000171)  
and substantial presence in majority of the islands of the Philippines in order to deter any recurrence of violence,

**[00:15:12.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000172)  
to prevent the resurgence of violence, and to be able to assist communities immediately when they are in need.

**[00:15:22.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000173)  
And since the threat of malign activities occurring in uninhabited areas is real, therefore some presence in those areas is necessary.

**[00:15:36.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000174)  
Nonetheless, the internal security and stability role remains germane to the external defense role

**[00:15:43.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000175)  
because without internal peace and stability, we cannot build a core for external defense.

**[00:15:52.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000176)  
So this remains a priority area, and we are very proactive right now in the BARMM area

**[00:16:06.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000177)  
because of the fact that we realize it's going to be the first democratic exercise of any of the 22 peace processes that the UN recognizes.

**[00:16:22.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000178)  
And, uh, we devote, uh, a big amount of, uh, resources in personnel and in MOOE under the supervision and control of the Commission on Elections

**[00:16:35.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000179)  
in order to help deter unwanted election-related or non-related violence in the area.

**[00:16:45.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000180)  
Next.

**[00:16:48.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000181)  
Welfare of veterans is extremely important because the veterans, although they are retired,

**[00:16:54.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000183)  
They remain a moral force and a driving force even if they are retired.

**[00:16:59.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000184)  
They serve as valuable additions to the active duty forces,

**[00:17:06.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000185)  
particularly those with technical skills and expertise who we still rely on in order to provide leadership to junior personnel.

**[00:17:18.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000186)  
Secondly, also the President has approved the first stage of the...

**[00:17:24.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000187)  
construction of an annex in the Veterans Memorial Medical Center

**[00:17:30.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000188)  
which will allow us to move our current patients

**[00:17:34.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000189)  
so that the rehabilitation of the legacy building which is more than 70 years old

**[00:17:41.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000190)  
and the structure being unsustainable and the cost of a high infection rate can be commenced

**[00:17:50.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000191)  
and can be, uh, optimized for use.

**[00:17:54.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000192)  
Thirdly, the President has continued appropriations for what we call Valor Clinics,

**[00:18:02.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000193)  
external clinics where we can service veterans closer to where they are situated.

**[00:18:15.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000194)  
However, the challenge we face is to staff these...

**[00:18:22.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000195)  
these clinics with competent personnel who are also security vetted in order to handle medical and data privacy issues.

**[00:18:33.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000196)  
Nonetheless, we, by the end of the year, will have three to five running.

**[00:18:40.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000197)  
Fortunately, we are able to get some personnel and as we are able to fill these up sustainably,

**[00:18:48.740] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000198)  
this will be a program that will continue.

**[00:18:52.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000199)  
Secondly, the health care of veterans is now being merged

**[00:19:00.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000200)  
in the wellness and preventive health care side with those in the active duty

**[00:19:06.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000201)  
so that in the active duty, we can mitigate with preventive health care

**[00:19:15.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000202)  
those diseases that may be mitigated during the time...

**[00:19:22.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000203)  
That they are still young and they do not manifest as severely when they reach retirement age.

**[00:19:32.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000204)  
And we have special programs too for the veterans and senior citizens on other issues that we may not have addressed before.

**[00:19:47.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000205)  
For example, mental wellness, mental health, and geriatric care issues.

**[00:19:58.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000206)  
That basically forms the core of the Veterans Welfare Program for us.

**[00:20:06.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000207)  
We also realize that for veterans, it's quite important for them to be able to socialize and to have an ecosystem after they retire.

**[00:20:16.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000208)  
As we know, retirement from the military service is a traumatic issue.

**[00:20:23.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000209)  
Actually, it's a traumatic incident because for 40 years, 30 years of your life, you are in a structured environment with a support system.

**[00:20:33.980] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000210)  
Then suddenly, the next day, this is cut off.

**[00:20:36.817] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000211)  
So, we need to provide for that transition and the post-retirement [unclear] wellness of the veteran. We're able to approach it in a more holistic manner now. We are trying to come to grips with the issue of mental wellness in active-duty service, where we see cases right now. The incidence of violence is very low.

**[00:21:18.030] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000214)  
But we see the incidence of inability to cope with stressful issues on a personal level, which we need to address. And we're seeing more and more of that. I think it's a phenomenon that is common to all organizations.

**[00:21:42.450] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000215)  
So that's basically the thrust in veterans' welfare. Next slide. On modernizing the armed forces, it is indeed a tough nut to crack, admittedly. And as the day goes on, things get more expensive.

**[00:22:04.850] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000216)  
We are first focusing on our core systems. Those are the foundational systems we need in order to...

**[00:22:15.170] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000217)  
...building block a sustainable defense system. The equipment we use is just the tip of the spear. But without the support systems for those pieces of hardware you see, everything will be just useless because they will be unsustainable.

**[00:22:37.790] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000218)  
And in modernizing the armed forces, there's another factor that needs to be taken into consideration.

**[00:22:44.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000220)  
It is the infrastructure we have to house, to support, and to maintain these capabilities, which is sadly lacking in our budget.

**[00:22:56.150] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000221)  
Because for several years, the capital outlay has not been financed, and it's totally on modernizing.

**[00:23:06.970] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000222)  
So it's equipment-heavy but not support-heavy.

**[00:23:09.550] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000223)  
That goes with our authorized troop strength also, where the operating...

**[00:23:15.170] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000224)  
...to support ratio is about 70-30 or 80-20, and we badly need support personnel.

**[00:23:22.810] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000225)  
And we really badly need more troops to rotate our troops.

**[00:23:26.350] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000226)  
They don't have a break anymore with the mission areas that they go to.

**[00:23:31.070] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000227)  
So modernization is a question of really building core systems, which we are prioritizing now, our IT and cyber systems for command and control.

**[00:23:42.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000229)  
These cost less but are more critical to us because as foundational systems, any compromise in the early stage, you cannot patch anymore on a later stage because the compromise is already present.

**[00:24:05.430] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000230)  
We are also heavily also trying to increase our capacity.

**[00:24:20.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000231)  
For humanitarian assistance and disaster response, you know, we have about 46 Black Hawk helicopters, which help us a lot, but that's not enough.

**[00:24:31.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000232)  
We need larger rotary platforms in order to service with bigger weight and with pallets.

**[00:24:41.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000233)  
Because the Black Hawk, although it can carry a lot, it needs to be loaded by hand.

**[00:24:47.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000234)  
It cannot be palletized.

**[00:24:48.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000235)  
And we need palletized aircraft already on [unclear].

**[00:24:54.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000236)  
On the naval side, we continue to acquire modern corvettes and frigates.

**[00:25:01.980] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000237)  
And on the army side, the first priority really is to make them more mobile so that their ability to respond and to deter in areas,

**[00:25:21.670] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000238)  
I think we agreed with the army that the first thing that they have to do for both internal security and for external defense is to increase their footprint throughout the Philippines.

**[00:25:47.130] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000239)  
This is because they serve also as logistical bases.

**[00:25:51.670] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000240)  
To support the frontline troops, which are badly lacking at this time.

**[00:25:57.310] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000241)  
This is not the day and age of notional inventories or just in time, but it's a question of reserves and stores.

**[00:26:04.870] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000242)  
We need to build our strategic reserve points of storage for critical items that we need.

**[00:26:16.710] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000243)  
And this must be distributed throughout the Philippines.

**[00:26:21.670] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000244)  
With infrastructure that can support the weight of the capabilities we carry.

**[00:26:27.850] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000245)  
So the modernization is not merely a question of acquiring capabilities,

**[00:26:34.830] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000246)  
but also providing the adequate infrastructure to support capabilities.

**[00:26:43.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000247)  
And secondly, providing the access to these capabilities,

**[00:26:53.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000248)  
that can support the dimensions and the weight of the equipment that you intend to use.

**[00:27:04.650] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000249)  
Secondly, the 50 billion, as we know, was an offshoot also of the basis conversion law,

**[00:27:13.150] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000250)  
which had to be subsidized year on year.

**[00:27:19.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000251)  
Which is why we'd like to,

**[00:27:23.723] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000253)  
we have sought an accounting of the amounts gathered by the BCDA.

**[00:27:30.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000254)  
We feel that strategic assets have already been taken,

**[00:27:40.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000255)  
which cannot be replicated,

**[00:27:42.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000256)  
and we have to stop this already.

**[00:27:44.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000257)  
What's there is there, fine.

**[00:27:46.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000258)  
But then we need to get what was fully due us.

**[00:27:52.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000259)  
And we need to stop the hemorrhage.

**[00:27:56.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000260)  
In terms of being the trust beneficiary of the trust,

**[00:28:00.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000261)  
the armed forces of the Philippines.

**[00:28:03.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000262)  
I think in the modernization law,

**[00:28:08.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000263)  
that has to be the end goal.

**[00:28:10.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000264)  
How much you give to the armed forces,

**[00:28:12.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000265)  
and not the corporate structure,

**[00:28:14.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000266)  
and not the value proposition devoid of the beneficiary's interests.

**[00:28:23.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000267)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:28:27.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000268)  
Security.

**[00:28:28.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000269)  
Cooperation agreements have been very important

**[00:28:31.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000270)  
in order to serve as deterrence and to serve interoperability

**[00:28:37.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000271)  
in order for us to gain experience and access to technology

**[00:28:45.660] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000272)  
which will not be available if we do not have these security arrangements.

**[00:28:54.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000273)  
As a main result of this,

**[00:28:58.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000274)  
because of the access and the training we got,

**[00:29:05.860] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000275)  
the Filipinos in Australia,

**[00:29:08.740] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000276)  
for the first time,

**[00:29:09.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000277)  
were able to lead a strike package

**[00:29:12.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000278)  
of 40 aircraft versus 40 aircraft in 90 minutes at night.

**[00:29:21.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000279)  
So those are 80 aircraft at supersonic speed at one time at night.

**[00:29:28.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000280)  
And this is not because of the pilots per se,

**[00:29:31.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000281)  
but because we have a trained core and cadre

**[00:29:35.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000282)  
of what we call air battle managers at this time.

**[00:29:38.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000283)  
Everything is controlled by ground and in microseconds.

**[00:29:46.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000284)  
The visibility that the Philippine Air Force has been able to achieve

**[00:29:52.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000285)  
was because of upgrades that were made to our current platform.

**[00:29:58.163] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000287)  
And because of the knowledge that we have acquired in order to use this platform properly

**[00:30:08.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000288)  
of which the total package we still do not have.

**[00:30:15.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000289)  
But for the skills of the personnel, they are already there.

**[00:30:22.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000290)  
And it goes to show you that in every platform, you have to deal with it as an ecosystem

**[00:30:30.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000291)  
where it's an integrated system of awareness, threat assessments,

**[00:30:40.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000292)  
and application of capabilities in a microsecond.

**[00:30:52.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000293)  
That's how fast it goes without any voice.

**[00:30:55.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000294)  
There's no communications whatsoever anymore.

**[00:31:01.030] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000295)  
I myself was surprised.

**[00:31:03.550] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000296)  
And right now, our challenge is not to lose the proficiency of those that were certified

**[00:31:14.150] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000297)  
this year only.

**[00:31:20.350] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000298)  
And this was an exercise.

**[00:31:24.682] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000305)  
Participated in by 21 countries, uh, where each

**[00:31:28.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000306)  
one, you know, had a role.

**[00:31:31.679] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000307)  
It was an exercise in interoperability.

**[00:31:35.768] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000308)  
Secondly, we received a lot of, uh, uh, security assistance from this cooperation agreement.

**[00:31:45.830] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000309)  
And the cooperation agreements are not limited to, uh, the Indo-Pacific region but also to Europe

**[00:31:53.430] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000310)  
as we have a visiting forces agreement with France.

**[00:31:56.430] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000312)  
We have recently, the Senate has approved the concurrence by the President, ratified

**[00:32:13.010] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000313)  
rather, the concurrence by the President to two new Visiting Forces Agreements, Canada

**[00:32:18.750] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000314)  
and New Zealand, with whom we have these countries, Canada and New Zealand are both maritime countries

**[00:32:30.350] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000315)  
in the Pacific who rely also on our sea lanes of communication for their supply chains.

**[00:32:41.210] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000316)  
With both countries too, we cooperate a lot, we receive a lot of awareness and technology

**[00:32:45.919] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000317)  
with them.

**[00:32:46.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000318)  
And these are both defense leaders.

**[00:32:48.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000319)  
So, I believe this is an area where we are concentrating on and where I spend a lot

**[00:33:00.450] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000320)  
of my time on too, because having had these agreements is one thing, but making them work

**[00:33:07.730] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000321)  
is another, and it needs a lot of work also on that end.

**[00:33:10.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000322)  
Next slide.

**[00:33:17.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000323)  
On disaster and institutional resilience, I have directed the OCD to concentrate on

**[00:33:24.030] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000324)  
this year, totally on disaster response.

**[00:33:29.490] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000325)  
They have to get one thing perfect, and that is the coordination function for awareness,

**[00:33:37.530] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000326)  
prediction and response, before other things come into play.

**[00:33:48.990] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000327)  
I believe the institutional competence of the OCD is best geared towards that coordinative

**[00:33:58.850] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000328)  
function in order to deliver adequate warnings and to respond accurately and preemptively,

**[00:34:13.190] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000329)  
with more granularity as years go on.

**[00:34:18.530] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000333)  
So, I have reviewed the amount of partnerships the NDRRMC has done, and I have seen that

**[00:34:23.870] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000334)  
these are marginal in effect.

**[00:34:29.790] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000335)  
I beg the support of Congress to allow us the time in order to develop these capabilities.

**[00:34:41.679] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000336)  
For example, rehabilitation, etc.

**[00:34:45.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000337)  
It just adds another layer of

**[00:34:48.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000339)  
bureaucracy to the release of badly needed funds to repair infrastructure

**[00:34:59.150] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000340)  
that should be based on systems, river systems, faults, et cetera.

**[00:35:06.670] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000341)  
I realize the concerns of each and every member to really account to their districts for something,

**[00:35:17.930] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000342)  
but it is actually beyond the competence of the OCD except to get the concurrence of the different agencies possible.

**[00:35:32.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000343)  
However, for the OCD, what we really need is a joint logistics centers to preposition perishable logistics and medicines.

**[00:35:48.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000345)  
In areas which we can preposition and properly inventory manage so that the response time will be quicker.

**[00:35:58.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000346)  
And that includes water, too.

**[00:36:00.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000347)  
We have a very difficult time with water right now, too.

**[00:36:05.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000348)  
The major issue right now is almost all supplies, except now we are able to preposition on a more sustainable basis.

**[00:36:18.860] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000349)  
Uh, they used to emanate solely

**[00:36:20.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000350)  
from Metro Manila and have to be airlifted or sealifted from Metro Manila.

**[00:36:28.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000351)  
And so how long that takes for, for those from, uh, uh, Camarines Sur, if you remember, even though we could land in the airport,

**[00:36:40.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000352)  
even though we could, uh, dock in [unclear], uh, there was, uh, there was an area called Milaor.

**[00:36:48.860] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000353)  
or where you could not traverse, so it could not reach, uh, uh, Naga City. So there we had a problem.

**[00:36:59.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000354)  
So that is the only ask which we asked for in the OCD. Next slide, please. The Defense Department

**[00:37:11.350] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000355)  
continues to be understaffed and as we need more uh oversight and more policy directives we had

**[00:37:24.550] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000356)  
to lift up, uh, certain offices to areas of prominence such as IT and cyber systems

**[00:37:36.390] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000357)  
and for real estate given the fact that

**[00:37:38.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000359)  
it manages a large amount of real estate a lot of which the titles aren't perfected and which we

**[00:37:48.870] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000360)  
need to do. Takes a lot of work to do that, and frankly, at 297 [unclear], 267 filled up, we're badly

**[00:37:59.190] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000361)  
understaffed we need to have a department which is more agile a department which is more multi-disciplinary

**[00:38:11.190] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000362)  
in order to

**[00:38:14.230] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000363)  
at least anticipate well enough threats as they come it has been a challenge for us

**[00:38:22.230] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000364)  
and we were pulling more than more weight than each one can do personally because of

**[00:38:32.950] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000365)  
the understaffing that we have that that is the department proper which is supposed to manage

**[00:38:40.310] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000366)  
uh let's say about a stakeholder base of 400 000 to include the retirees and their

**[00:38:47.670] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000367)  
their uh dependents and uh to manage a variety of issues and assets under management of quite

**[00:38:57.350] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000368)  
a large amount it's frankly physically exhausting for each and every one of us and we cannot

**[00:39:04.230] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000369)  
have flexible work arrangements because of operational security considerations

**[00:39:11.110] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000370)  
Uh, next slide. The IT communications and cyber operations are extremely important.

**[00:39:22.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000371)  
they are in the foundational stage we can only work with

**[00:39:28.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000372)  
trusted vendors and

**[00:39:33.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000373)  
original manufacturers actually because of our security of information protocols and the

**[00:39:44.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000374)  
threat of compromise of our systems it is something that we are heavily undertaking

**[00:54:59.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000572)  
advanced weaponry.

**[00:55:00.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000573)  
Because the

**[00:55:02.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000574)  
small arms,

**[00:55:04.980] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000575)  
expenditures

**[00:55:06.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000576)  
versus other capability

**[00:55:10.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000577)  
ratio has been reducing

**[00:55:12.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000579)  
As time goes on, and we cannot have an arsenal which is equivalent to a Philippine mint,

**[00:55:21.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000580)  
which is a sovereign capability, not doing highly technical and technologically advanced activities on a sovereign basis.

**[00:55:34.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000581)  
For the NDCP, it has been undergoing a transition phase.

**[00:55:41.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000582)  
This time, even with the review of curriculum and a review of the indicators that we have,

**[00:55:53.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000583)  
I have taken a personal role in reviewing the outputs that I require of them

**[00:56:01.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000584)  
and the introduction of the notion that national security is multidisciplinary

**[00:56:10.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000585)  
and we cannot have a set paradigm for national security.

**[00:56:13.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000586)  
With more emphasis on research output, not only of the students but of the faculty,

**[00:56:21.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000587)  
it's publish or die slowly that will be the output requirement.

**[00:56:28.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000588)  
Next, for OCD, as we said, their accomplishment was high,

**[00:56:41.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000589)  
but I would like to see more responsive and more preemptive actions.

**[00:56:49.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000590)  
And more coordinated efficiency within them.

**[00:56:58.190] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000591)  
And more structured approaches to partnerships on a more sustainable basis.

**[00:57:02.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000592)  
Next.

**[00:57:06.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000593)  
Next.

**[00:57:12.079] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000594)  
Uh, veterans welfare. It's there and, uh, the

**[00:57:15.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000595)  
veterans, uh, memorial historical preservation. There are other targets that we need to do,

**[00:57:22.799] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000596)  
Uh, uh, that we need to preserve and we

**[00:57:25.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000597)  
need to optimize certain areas for both joint and shrine use.

**[00:57:34.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000598)  
Next.

**[00:57:37.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000599)  
VMMC. Uh, the number of outpatients treated have increased.

**[00:57:47.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000600)  
The 11,100 inpatients treated goes to show that only about, to date,

**[00:58:02.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000601)  
9% of the veterans can be accommodated in the VMMC.

**[00:58:11.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000602)  
So, we continue to try to reach out on a preventive basis to them

**[00:58:20.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000603)  
because hospitalizations in the center right now

**[00:58:24.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000604)  
are something that we need to remediate

**[00:58:31.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000605)  
so that the center can be totally refurbished.

**[00:58:35.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000606)  
Because the infection rate is quite high,

**[00:58:39.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000607)  
given the fact that it is an old design, a dormitory design, post-World War II.

**[00:58:46.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000608)  
And just like the DND proper building, it was molding.

**[00:58:52.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000609)  
And not in the VMMC, but the DND building, there was even asbestos.

**[00:58:58.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000610)  
So, we had to refurbish it.

**[00:59:01.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000611)  
So, we are now working off-site, scattered in different locations.

**[00:59:11.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000612)  
It's a challenge because it's a legacy building, but it needed to be done.

**[00:59:17.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000613)  
With a single elevator, which is not PWD-friendly.

**[00:59:23.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000614)  
And it was used as a service elevator as well.

**[00:59:26.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000615)  
So, it spent more time being conked out than not.

**[00:59:30.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000617)  
Next slide.

**[00:59:33.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000618)  
Financial accomplishments.

**[00:59:35.359] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000619)  
Next.

**[00:59:38.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000620)  
Obligation rates sometimes are less than optimal

**[00:59:45.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000621)  
because of the fact that approved bid ceilings are low.

**[00:59:51.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000622)  
And because of the fact that in the defense space,

**[00:59:56.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000623)  
material goes up in price.

**[00:59:59.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000625)  
It's a seller's and a supplier's market.

**[01:00:03.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000626)  
So rather than pull the trigger on certain things,

**[01:00:06.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000627)  
we'd rather not if it will result in an unsustainable or unsupportable expenditure.

**[01:00:17.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000628)  
Next slide.

**[01:00:22.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000629)  
For the first semester of 2026, we have almost a 50% obligation versus allotment

**[01:00:28.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000630)  
and 85% disbursement versus obligation.

**[01:00:34.319] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000631)  
Next.

**[01:00:37.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000632)  
Uh, comparatives.

**[01:00:39.359] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000633)  
Next.

**[01:00:42.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000634)  
Uh, the comparatives, uh, from 2021 as the base

**[01:00:51.170] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000635)  
the disbursement versus obligation rates have reduced

**[01:00:57.830] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000636)  
because of the increasing cost of material versus the budget.

**[01:01:08.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000637)  
So, uh, the same financial management principle will be followed

**[01:01:17.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000638)  
if defense management...

**[01:01:18.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000639)  
If the material doesn't go any cheaper and the approved bid ceilings

**[01:01:24.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000640)  
versus the release of funds becomes...

**[01:01:29.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000641)  
The gap is increasing, then we cannot obligate the amounts.

**[01:01:35.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000642)  
Next.

**[01:01:38.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000643)  
Issues and concerns.

**[01:01:40.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000644)  
Next.

**[01:01:46.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000645)  
These are the perennial issues and concerns that we have.

**[01:01:56.130] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000646)  
Of note, just to summarize,

**[01:02:02.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000647)  
as a result of the budgeting and the modernization process,

**[01:02:07.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000648)  
we are still in Horizon 2 projects.

**[01:02:10.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000649)  
A lot of Horizon 2 projects we had to cancel already for being obsolete.

**[01:02:19.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000650)  
And we've had to revise Horizon 3 several times

**[01:02:24.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000651)  
to reflect new threats and new developments.

**[01:02:29.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000652)  
Next.

**[01:02:31.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000653)  
Next.

**[01:02:33.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000654)  
This was included in the GAA of last year.

**[01:02:39.359] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000655)  
And since it will be subject to the control of the DOF,

**[01:02:44.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000656)  
we respectfully move for its adoption again.

**[01:02:46.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000657)  
If there is an opportunity that can be financed

**[01:02:50.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000658)  
and is acceptable to the finance department,

**[01:02:53.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000659)  
then we should take advantage of it,

**[01:02:55.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000660)  
particularly if we can get longer tenor financing.

**[01:02:58.319] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000661)  
Next.

**[01:03:02.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000663)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:03:04.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000664)  
That's the end of our presentation.

**[01:05:01.880] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000700)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Chair.

**[01:05:04.707] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000701)  
Una po, salam alaikum marahmatullahi wabarakatuhu.

**[01:05:08.774] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000702)  
Magandang umaga po sa ating lahat,

**[01:05:11.865] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000703)  
lalong-lalo na po sa DND family

**[01:05:14.041] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000704)  
na pinamumunuan po sa DND family.

**[01:05:15.322] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000705)  
At sa mga kasamang ahensyang napapaloob dito,

**[01:05:20.548] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000706)  
ang Armed Forces of the Philippines,

**[01:05:22.541] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000707)  
ang Philippine Army,

**[01:05:24.066] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000708)  
ang Philippine Air Force,

**[01:05:25.632] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000709)  
at syempre ang Philippine Navy po.

**[01:05:28.601] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000710)  
Dadako na po ako sa aking magiging tanong.

**[01:05:32.241] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000711)  
My question is about a report that

**[01:05:34.701] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000712)  
China is recruiting Filipino government workers

**[01:05:38.402] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000713)  
to leak official documents and information

**[01:05:41.453] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000714)  
and turn them over to China.

**[01:05:44.401] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000715)  
In a news report,

**[01:05:45.825] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000716)  
between 2024 and 2025,

**[01:05:49.668] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000717)  
may dating researcher ng Navy,

**[01:05:52.922] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000718)  
may former employee ng DND,

**[01:05:55.443] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000719)  
and a person close to the Philippine Coast Guard,

**[01:05:58.799] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000720)  
ang nahuling nagsusulat ng analysis

**[01:06:01.747] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000721)  
or nagpo-forward ng emails

**[01:06:03.740] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000722)  
to handlers allegedly linked to the Chinese Communist Party

**[01:06:08.458] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000723)  
in exchange for money.

**[01:06:11.244] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000724)  
Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary,

**[01:06:13.949] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000725)  
paano tayo nalusutan po nito?

**[01:06:16.328] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000727)  
Can you detail to this committee

**[01:06:18.056] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000728)  
how these Filipinos were recruited

**[01:06:20.110] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000729)  
and anong klaseng mga impormasyon

**[01:06:22.652] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000730)  
ang kanilang naibigay?

**[01:06:24.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000731)  
Doon po, sa alam ko,

**[01:06:26.544] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000732)  
dito sa agency namin,

**[01:06:28.390] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000733)  
sa internet lang po,

**[01:06:31.397] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000734)  
nakatanggap ng mga email

**[01:06:33.490] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000735)  
na kiniimbita sila na mag-sideline

**[01:06:39.255] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000736)  
in various topics.

**[01:06:42.110] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000737)  
Kung pwede sila.

**[01:06:43.461] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000738)  
Kumuha ng paper sa sinasabing think tank.

**[01:06:48.313] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000739)  
So dahil may pabuya ito,

**[01:06:51.262] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000740)  
wala namang operational security protocols

**[01:06:54.288] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000741)  
na mahigpit noong mga panahon na iyon,

**[01:06:57.142] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000742)  
ito'y di o manay pinatulad

**[01:06:59.901] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000743)  
hanggat sa nagkaroon ng mas detalyadong impormasyon

**[01:07:06.219] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000744)  
ang hinihingi.

**[01:07:12.003] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000745)  
Naman ang Diyos po ay hindi na po pumayag

**[01:07:15.638] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000746)  
sa pagkaalam ko itong mga bata at nagsuplong agad.

**[01:07:21.651] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000747)  
Pagamat ganon, marami pa pong mga

**[01:07:24.277] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000748)  
sari-saring attempts as inventive as people are,

**[01:07:30.195] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000749)  
the attempts to compromise are real.

**[01:07:32.954] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000750)  
And at the end of the day po,

**[01:07:35.313] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000751)  
nasa hygiene na po ng bawat empleyado

**[01:07:38.206] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000752)  
at

**[01:07:39.766] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000753)  
awa ng Diyos po, ito'y nasugpo

**[01:07:43.096] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000754)  
at wala namang pong sensitibong impormasyon na napuha.

**[01:07:49.166] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000755)  
Ngunit ito po ay isa sa matinding paraan talaga

**[01:07:51.925] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000756)  
kung papaano

**[01:07:52.953] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000757)  
nang aakit

**[01:07:55.103] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000758)  
ng researchers

**[01:07:56.892] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000759)  
at ng mga

**[01:07:58.205] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000760)  
particularly po

**[01:08:01.059] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000761)  
kung hindi permanenteng employee

**[01:08:03.209] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000762)  
kung job order or contract of service po

**[01:08:05.588] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000763)  
dahil nga sa civil service regulation.

**[01:08:08.309] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000764)  
Ang baba po ng salary grades na mabibigay sa highly technical people,

**[01:08:15.121] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000765)  
awa ng Diyos po,

**[01:08:17.709] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000766)  
nasugpo po ito kaagad.

**[01:08:20.772] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000767)  
Ngunit,

**[01:08:22.066] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000768)  
be that as it may,

**[01:08:23.836] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000769)  
listo po palagi ang buong organisasyon

**[01:08:26.158] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000770)  
sa mga kompromiso na maaaring ganito.

**[01:08:29.411] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000771)  
Di lang po naman sa atin nangyayari,

**[01:08:31.828] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000772)  
marami pa pong mas delikadong impormasyon na napuha

**[01:08:35.158] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000773)  
sa ibang mga bansa.

**[01:08:36.852] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000774)  
Lalo na based on capabilities na. At ito'y napro-prosecute ng mga countries involved at na pre-preempt.

**[01:08:48.878] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000775)  
So, yung report po na yan, I cannot give further details, ngunit totoo po yan ang nangyayari po. Kami po ay magbabantay ng matindi sa ganito pong mga gawain. Thank you po sa tanong.

**[01:09:14.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000776)  
Maraming salamat po. Ito na lang po, paano na lang po natin masisiguro na ang DND, hindi na po ito mawawala?

**[01:09:34.799] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000777)  
Hindi po natin masisigurado talaga na hindi maulit. Pero ang importante, ang iaalam dun sa empleyado po, ang public platform, ang IT equipment na ginagamit, yan ay minomonitor.

**[01:09:51.865] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000778)  
So, hindi pwedeng gamitin pang personal kung sino man empleyado, whether AFP or DND.

**[01:09:58.927] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000779)  
At sinasubject din po natin sa matinding security vetting itong ating mga empleyado. Pero secondly po, ang hacking ay actually gera araw-araw po yan.

**[01:10:12.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000780)  
So, there will be some compromises as with any organization dun sa IT system.

**[01:10:23.055] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000781)  
Ngunit, the exercise is to detect the compromise as soon as possible and to isolate it as soon as possible.

**[01:10:30.872] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000782)  
Kaya nga po, inulit po natin kanina na on the foundation pa lang ng isang sistema, kailangan pati hardware ay walang kompromiso.

**[01:10:40.105] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000783)  
Kung hindi, hindi nyo na mapapatch yan kahit kailan. Bubunutin nyo na po lahat yan. So, salamat po.

**[01:10:50.080] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000784)  
Can you also, Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary, can you also say to this committee na para hindi na-attract ang ating Filipino government workers sa mga alok ng China,

**[01:11:02.796] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000785)  
we need to ensure our government workers are paid well, have secured benefits, and are able to exercise their rights and duties as citizens?

**[01:11:12.719] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000786)  
No doubt about that, Madam Chair, Your Honor. No doubt about it.

**[01:11:18.753] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000787)  
The civil service regulations and COA regulations allow us to adequately compensate people depending on skill level.

**[01:11:27.120] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000788)  
And do you also support, Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary, the measures providing hazard pay for civilian government personnel deployed in the West Philippine Sea?

**[01:11:38.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000789)  
Sure. Yes, yes, Your Honor. Because they are subjected to risk also.

**[01:11:42.713] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000790)  
And this includes civilian personnel too deployed in highly critical areas, for example,

**[01:11:48.730] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000791)  
maybe teachers who are on duty in election areas, in identified hotspots, even first responders, Your Honor.

**[01:12:01.160] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000792)  
Masaya akong marinig ito mula sa ating Secretary dahil kami po sa Akbayan Reform Block ay nag-file po ng bill patungkol sa hazard pay po ng ating mga civilian personnel po.

**[01:12:13.255] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000793)  
Anyway po, Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:12:15.031] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000794)  
Kung may dadagdag ko lang po.

**[01:12:17.932] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000796)  
I do not know how to approach the problem.

**[01:12:19.786] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000797)  
And I do not know. The problem is, ang hazard pay po sa ating mga tauhan ay isang bagay yun.

**[01:12:32.612] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000798)  
Pero kung ang pinakamalaking inaalala din ng tao, paano may a-apply yung hazard pay pag yung pamilya ay may problema din?

**[01:12:45.556] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000799)  
Kasi psychological weight.

**[01:12:49.384] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000800)  
Po yun eh.

**[01:12:50.528] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000801)  
Let's say magre-respondi siya, alam niyang bahay yung bahay niya, mahirap din po eh.

**[01:12:56.053] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000802)  
So, I really don't have the solution to the problem but it is something that we must tackle by the bull.

**[01:13:02.209] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000803)  
The bull by the horns.

**[01:13:04.754] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000804)  
So, maybe, you know, housing benefits for these people na alam niyang sigurado ang kanyang pamilya para sa ganon lubos-lubos yung pagbibigay ng serpisyo.

**[01:13:18.981] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000806)  
So, yun lang po ang may dadagdag ko.

**[01:13:21.210] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000807)  
Sorry na lang po at wala po akong solusyon.

**[01:13:23.519] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000808)  
Pero I think it is a reality.

**[01:13:26.498] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000809)  
Nag-a-agree po ako sa inyo doon, Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:13:30.543] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000810)  
Nang sa gayon ay hindi na po sila yung tipong mabrive na lang ng kung sino mang bansa, no?

**[01:13:37.114] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000811)  
Na para lamang magbigay ng mga mahahalagang impormasyon.

**[01:13:42.481] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000812)  
But anyway, Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary, do you have a credible information that some Filipinos,

**[01:13:48.578] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000813)  
pin-elected officials, receive support from China and are releasing statements favorable to China's influence operations

**[01:13:57.161] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000814)  
and expansionism in the West Philippine Sea?

**[01:14:03.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000815)  
We have some ground to perhaps suspect it.

**[01:14:11.114] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000816)  
But credible information in respect to evidence is kind of hard to adduce, particularly us, where

**[01:14:21.957] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000821)  
We do not have access to bank deposits.

**[01:14:25.091] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000822)  
I think it is best addressed by financial authorities, actually, Your Honor,

**[01:14:32.022] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000823)  
in the same vein as a lot of syndicated activity was addressed in other countries

**[01:14:41.879] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000824)  
through tax fraud investigations.

**[01:14:48.506] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000825)  
Our problem, really,

**[01:14:50.102] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000826)  
is how much of this is admissible.

**[01:14:52.741] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000827)  
At the end of the day, that's what counts.

**[01:14:55.723] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000828)  
And we're not...

**[01:14:56.796] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000829)  
And we're not prepared to make any statement on that score.

**[01:15:05.323] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000830)  
We cooperate with law enforcement agencies on information, but that's the extent of what we do.

**[01:15:14.159] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000831)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:15:15.021] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000832)  
May maaari po ba kayong mabigay na informasyon dahil may mga speculations na po kayo na

**[01:15:21.345] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000833)  
ano po kaya yung mga particular na nare-receive nilang mga support po?

**[01:15:28.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000834)  
I'm also not prepared to state it, particularly if there are active law enforcement evidence-gathering operations at this stage.

**[01:15:44.239] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000835)  
Thank you, Your Honor.

**[01:15:45.616] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000836)  
At huli na lamang po para dito.

**[01:15:48.447] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000837)  
Do you think that sa influence...

**[01:15:51.843] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000838)  
sa operations po ng China sa social media, may mga elected officials din po ba na nag-a-amplify ng mga pro-China trolls?

**[01:16:02.239] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000839)  
I think the statements speak for themselves.

**[01:16:07.354] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000840)  
I believe, you know, there are some, undeniably, that the pronouncements speak for themselves.

**[01:16:16.206] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000841)  
However, I'd like to point out too that the Chinese embassy website,

**[01:16:21.596] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000842)  
itself, is not a diplomatic website anymore, but it is already a propaganda institution

**[01:16:26.868] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000843)  
that enjoys the freedom of speech that we enjoy without extending reciprocal benefits to us in their own home country.

**[01:16:41.360] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000844)  
Tama po kayo, no, Mr. Secretary?

**[01:16:43.596] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000845)  
Hindi lamang po kayo yung nabiktima dyan dahil kami rin po nabiktima na rin dyan sa website na yan.

**[01:16:48.910] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000846)  
Anyway po, dadako naman po ko sa West Philippine Sea Security at saka yung...

**[01:16:52.772] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000847)  
sa Maritime Security po.

**[01:16:54.435] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000848)  
Ang tensyon sa West Philippine Sea ay malinaw na security concern sa bansa.

**[01:17:00.762] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000849)  
Nito lamang July 20 of 2026, nasaktan ang dalawang Pilipinong sundalo sa Ayungin Shoal.

**[01:17:07.548] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000850)  
Sa harap ng ganitong sitwasyon, magkano ang aktual na pondong inilaan ng DND para sa pagpapatibay ng ating maritime security sa West Philippine Sea?

**[01:17:18.003] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000851)  
At nakapaloob na po ba ito doon sa 64.3?

**[01:17:21.444] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000852)  
Rebuild yun na operations budget para sa Naval Force Defense?

**[01:17:30.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000853)  
Nakapaloob po yan sa regular operational budget ang ating kayang gawin sa West Philippine Sea.

**[01:17:39.546] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000854)  
And hindi lang naman po doon, pati naman po sa northern portion, doon sa Bajo de Masinloc.

**[01:17:46.153] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000855)  
Hindi lang po naka...

**[01:17:48.214] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000856)  
Hindi lang po kami, kung hindi, Coast Guard at saka Philippine...

**[01:17:52.452] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000857)  
Navy ang nagtutulong dito.

**[01:17:58.057] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000858)  
At importante din po, tulad ng alam po ng ibang mga kasamahan po na legislators,

**[01:18:06.551] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000859)  
pag-asa-island, it's a question of habitation and normalization already of life in the area,

**[01:18:13.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000860)  
which is an important part of that aspect.

**[01:18:17.646] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000861)  
That's why we gave the instructions na rin po,

**[01:18:21.344] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000862)  
sa ating WESCOM,

**[01:18:25.601] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000863)  
na to fast-track the deployment of services by other agencies to pag-asa.

**[01:18:53.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000864)  
Kung ito po yung tingin ninyo at nakalagay na budget po ninyo sa NEP,

**[01:18:58.906] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000865)  
sa tingin nyo po ba ito ay sapat para sa modernisasyon,

**[01:19:02.399] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000866)  
dagdag deployment at protection ng sundalo at mangingisda sa West Philippine Sea.

**[01:19:07.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000867)  
Hindi po talaga ito sapat.

**[01:19:09.437] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000868)  
At kung tutusin po natin, today, West Philippine Sea,

**[01:19:13.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000869)  
mas malawak po ang ating Pacific Ocean boundary na kailangan din po natin bantayan,

**[01:19:19.175] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000870)  
lalo na nandun yung Philippine Rise.

**[01:19:21.657] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000871)  
Tapos ang West Philippine Sea po ay kaalinsabay ngayon

**[01:19:27.466] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000872)  
ng pag-file sa UN.

**[01:19:29.816] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000873)  
Nang ating delimitations across some features and Bajo de Masinloc.

**[01:19:38.483] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000874)  
So, naging mas klaro ang mission area na kailangan tutukan ng pansin.

**[01:19:46.134] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000875)  
Kasama din po dito, in order to support West Philippine Sea operations,

**[01:19:51.662] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000876)  
kailangan po natin ng base dito sa mainland.

**[01:19:58.016] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000877)  
Tatlong strategic passage po yan binabantayan.

**[01:20:01.550] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000878)  
Yung Verde Island Passage, of course, yung Batanes Channel,

**[01:20:06.532] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000879)  
Babuyan Channel, Verde Island Passage, at saka yung Surigao Strait Passage,

**[01:20:12.887] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000880)  
at saka yung Sulusi na passage.

**[01:20:18.677] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000881)  
Yung po ang major transit points na actually nagdudugtong

**[01:20:25.689] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000882)  
ng West Philippine Sea sa Pacific Ocean na tayo lamang ang meron.

**[01:20:30.859] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000883)  
So, ito po ang dapat focus din ng ating operations

**[01:20:35.578] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000884)  
na hindi natin magagawa ng lubos-lubusan kung wala tayong base.

**[01:20:40.561] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000885)  
Tama po kayo, Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:20:44.386] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000886)  
Ako po ay sumusuportan dito sa, lalong-lalo na kung ganito,

**[01:20:48.606] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000887)  
na napapaloob na din yung sa operations budget ng neighbor.

**[01:20:52.411] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000888)  
So, ito po ang global force defense yung ating plano para din doon sa West Philippine Sea.

**[01:20:57.950] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000889)  
With that, Madam Chair, I'd like to manifest my support na dapat naman po talaga

**[01:21:03.150] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000890)  
i-increase natin ang budget ng ating modernization or AFP modernization

**[01:21:09.141] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000891)  
dahil hindi po ito makatarungan kung security po ng ating bansa ang pinag-uusapan.

**[01:21:15.189] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000892)  
At huling katanungan na lamang po, Madam Chair,

**[01:21:19.089] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000893)  
bukod sa halaga, paano niyo po natitiyak na mabilis at efektibo ang paggamit ng pondo?

**[01:21:26.625] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000894)  
Halimbawa, meron bang nakalaan na training, intelligence, at joint patrols po?

**[01:21:39.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000895)  
Bawat po acquisition natin, sinisigurado natin po, fully equipped na ang mga bagong gamit

**[01:21:47.309] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000896)  
at sinasama na namin yung life cycle cost nito.

**[01:21:51.044] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000897)  
Sa costing ng bawat halaga.

**[01:21:53.845] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000898)  
Nang kinukuha.

**[01:21:55.712] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000899)  
So dito po tayo malaki na nakakasigurado.

**[01:22:00.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000900)  
Tapos kumukuha na lang tayo sa original manufacturers.

**[01:22:04.115] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000901)  
Ayaw natin po ng middleman o consolidators.

**[01:22:06.915] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000902)  
Tapos meron pong long-term service contracts para nauuna po tayo sa supply chain palagi.

**[01:22:13.451] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000903)  
Hindi kada kung sino na lang ang magbibid for supplies na hindi man sila ang manufacturer.

**[01:22:20.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000904)  
So yun po ang unang-unang guarantee natin.

**[01:22:23.721] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000905)  
Pangalawa, ang training po ay gasgas na rin po ang ating mga sundalo kaya hindi na rin po makapagbakasyon.

**[01:22:31.199] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000906)  
At hindi lang po training ngayon, it's also knowledge.

**[01:22:38.239] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000907)  
Kasi hinihiwalay po natin yung knowledge sa skills.

**[01:22:42.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000908)  
Ang skills ay ang application ng knowledge.

**[01:22:45.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000909)  
Ang knowledge po yung bale, katawan ng knowledge.

**[01:22:52.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000912)  
Pinagkukuna natin ng skills.

**[01:22:54.639] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000913)  
So nag-i-invest din po tayo sa knowledge, sa education ng ating mga tropa.

**[01:23:00.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000914)  
Lalong-lalo na po sa highly technical na mga subjects.

**[01:23:08.159] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000915)  
Kaya nga po tayo naglulungsad din ng direct commission program para makakuha tayo ng less physically fit but more highly technically qualified students.

**[01:23:26.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000922)  
Diyan po tayo kukuha ng skills training. Yun na po ang training na i-apply ng knowledge. At yan po matinding habilin na inilaan ng Departamento lalong-lalo na sa seleksyon ng magtuturo at sa liderato nito.

**[01:24:07.360] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-000923)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:24:09.679] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000923-split-1)  
Yes. Oh, yes. Congressman Pimentel, chairman of the Committee on Intelligence, is recognized.

**[01:24:17.120] Rep. Alexander Ty Pimentel** (asr-000924)  
I'll interject on a relevant point.

**[01:24:21.840] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000925)  
Yes, go ahead.

**[01:24:23.840] Rep. Alexander Ty Pimentel** (asr-000926)  
This is actually the reason, this is the event leading us to file the anti-spy unit law, a bill, an anti-foreign interference bill on all pending in the Committee of National Defense.

**[01:24:41.640] Rep. Alexander Ty Pimentel** (asr-000927)  
That's the problem mentioned by the Honorable Congresswoman.

**[01:24:49.128] Rep. Alexander Ty Pimentel** (asr-000928)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:24:51.360] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000929)  
Yes, thank you, Congressman Pimentel.

**[01:24:53.439] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000930)  
Thank you, Congressman Ismula.

**[01:24:55.706] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-000931)  
Next to interpolate, uh Deputy Majority Leader Arnan Panaligan.

**[01:25:01.867] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000932)  
Thank you very much, Madam Chair. Good morning. Good morning, Secretary Teodoro and to our military officers.

**[01:25:08.110] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000933)  
So, in the proposed 2027 NEP, the AFP modernization program was given 50 billion pesos without any unprogrammed appropriations.

**[01:25:20.080] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000935)  
I'm sure this will affect your Horizon 3.

**[01:25:23.652] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000936)  
And you mentioned that you will revise it again.

**[01:25:26.538] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000937)  
So, this has undergone many re-horizoning, yung Horizon 3.

**[01:25:31.027] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000938)  
Could you please inform us now, yung mga na-charge na dun sa 40 billion pesos for 2026, kung may natitira pa, and your proposal for 2027,

**[01:25:46.501] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000940)  
What particular items are you going to charge for the 50 billion pesos proposed in 2027?

**[01:26:02.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000941)  
Hindi. Sa 50, your honor, lahat ay obligated dahil pambayad ng MAICA. So, walang bago yun.

**[01:26:11.037] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000942)  
So, that's for the 50 billion for next year?

**[01:26:15.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000943)  
Yes.

**[01:26:16.080] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000944)  
And how about for the 40 billion for this year, also for MAICA?

**[01:26:20.740] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000945)  
Ano yung unprogrammed?

**[01:26:23.963] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000946)  
Wala pang unprogrammed.

**[01:26:26.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000947)  
Yes, your honor. Nasa 21.

**[01:26:29.650] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000948)  
For 2026?

**[01:26:32.114] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000949)  
Yes, your honor.

**[01:26:34.308] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000950)  
In the last hearing of the DBCC, first day of the budget hearing, I asked Secretary De Leon of DBM,

**[01:26:41.142] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000951)  
if narilis na yung 50 billion unprogrammed funding for this year. So, sabi niya wala pa.

**[01:26:47.723] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000952)  
And when I asked him kung may possibility na marilis, of course, sabi niya two conditions.

**[01:26:52.080] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000953)  
First, excess revenues, and second, kung kailangan pa rin ng DND.

**[01:26:56.043] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000954)  
So, I'm sure kailangan yun, ano?

**[01:26:58.051] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000955)  
So, I hope that you can make a strong representation sa kanila para kung kailangan talaga ninyo,

**[01:27:04.261] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000956)  
they can do something about it para ma-access ninyo yung unprogrammed funds of 50 billion for this year.

**[01:27:09.273] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000957)  
Considering hindi sila nagbigay ng unprogrammed funds for next year.

**[01:27:11.973] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000958)  
Ang tala lang po dyan sa excess, yung damages po na nangyari po dito sa mga sunod-sunod na habagar at saka sa lindol dun sa Mindanao,
