**[01:27:24.562] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000959)  
yung po malaking.

**[01:27:27.228] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000960)  
Cost, no?

**[01:27:28.190] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000961)  
Inaasahan pa po natin na magkakaroon ng additional na weather disturbances until the end of the year.

**[01:27:38.315] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000962)  
Dun lang nga po sa Occidental Mindoro, may bumahan na nga po ng katakot-takot.

**[01:27:45.065] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000963)  
So, dun po siguro kung meron man, maantala yung excess.

**[01:27:49.385] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000964)  
Madam Chair, can I request that we be furnished with a copy of the items charged?

**[01:27:55.440] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000965)  
40 billion and sa proposal na 50 billion for next year, kahit yun ay for MAICA.

**[01:28:00.860] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000966)  
Now, Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary, at the budget hearing last year, you mentioned that because of budget constraints,

**[01:28:07.119] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000967)  
you preferred that the DND and AFP be allowed to obtain loans to finance your modernization projects, no?

**[01:28:14.866] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000968)  
And you suggested the amendment of PD 415, a 1974 PD that set the ceiling for loans at 300 million US dollars.

**[01:28:25.177] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000969)  
1974 exchange rate.

**[01:28:27.252] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000970)  
So, in response to your proposal, I filed House Bill No. 8059, increasing the ceiling to 1 billion US dollars for a single project.

**[01:28:36.905] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000971)  
But it could be revised, no?

**[01:28:39.807] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000972)  
And remove the ceiling altogether.

**[01:28:42.035] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000973)  
Now, dun sa 27NEP kasi inalis din yung special provision.

**[01:28:45.798] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000974)  
So, yung DBM, hindi na kayo binigyan ng malaki for modernization, inalis pa yung authority, no?

**[01:28:50.877] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000975)  
Na kayo ay mag-loan.

**[01:28:52.733] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000976)  
So, better talaga, merong law.

**[01:28:55.113] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000977)  
That will authorize you.

**[01:28:57.020] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000978)  
That's the rationale for my House Bill 8059.

**[01:28:59.062] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000979)  
So, which do you prefer?

**[01:29:01.188] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000980)  
Wala ng ceiling altogether or may cap pa rin, let's say, from 300 to a billion dollars or so?

**[01:29:07.195] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000981)  
Wala ng cap, your Honor, because the DBM and the DOF sets the cap, actually, based on the fiscal condition of the country

**[01:29:20.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000982)  
and the type of loan which will be approved by them anyway.

**[01:29:25.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000983)  
That will be entered into.

**[01:29:29.977] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000984)  
So, actually, they have total control over the financial accommodation or financial arrangement that is going to be made.

**[01:29:42.633] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000985)  
They will set the ceiling, anyway, depending on what the country can afford.

**[01:29:49.282] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000986)  
And since the loan will be entered into by...

**[01:29:55.199] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000987)  
The DOF.

**[01:29:56.400] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000988)  
Thank you.

**[01:29:57.230] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000989)  
The DOF, at the end of the day, I think that is a sufficient guarantee because for the fiscal ability of the government to service the debt,

**[01:30:10.880] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000990)  
given the fact that revenue raising is the prime mandate of the DOF.

**[01:30:18.442] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000991)  
Well, I was assured by the Chair of the Committee on National Defense that the Committee will deliberate soon on that House bill

**[01:30:24.770] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000992)  
and he assigned me to head the Technical Working Group.

**[01:30:27.199] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000993)  
So, we will discuss this further at the TWG.

**[01:30:30.625] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000994)  
And I have here a copy of the U.S. Senate bill.

**[01:30:35.030] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000995)  
I would suspect that the PD-415, I think it was because of the fact that we are under IMF control at that time

**[01:30:50.133] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000996)  
with currency stabilization loans.

**[01:30:53.200] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000997)  
That was in the 70s.

**[01:30:55.718] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000998)  
So, that was the major imposition probably of the IMF that expenditures on foreign loans

**[01:31:04.400] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-000999)  
because of foreign exchange controls at that time should be limited to 300 million U.S. dollars

**[01:31:09.599] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001000)  
in consideration for the grant of currency stabilization loans by the IMF.

**[01:31:18.787] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001001)  
That is what I suspect, Your Honor.

**[01:31:23.174] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001002)  
So, maybe we can now...

**[01:31:24.639] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001003)  
Remove that cap.

**[01:31:25.621] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001004)  
Anyway, I have here a copy of the U.S. Senate bill 1497 entitled

**[01:31:29.654] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001005)  
A Bill to Modernize the Defense Capabilities of the Philippines and for Other Purposes.

**[01:31:33.974] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001006)  
Do you have any information if this Senate bill in the U.S. has been enacted already?

**[01:31:39.863] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001007)  
What Senate bill, Your Honor?

**[01:31:42.023] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001008)  
Senate bill 1497 or the PERA, Philippine Enhanced Resiliency Act.

**[01:31:45.854] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001009)  
It has been enacted, Your Honor, by the U.S. Congress.

**[01:31:50.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001010)  
Nonetheless, the funding, I believe, for next year will be...

**[01:31:54.800] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001011)  
I thought 100 million U.S.

**[01:31:56.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001012)  
100 million that will be allocated by the U.S. government for it.

**[01:32:01.379] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001013)  
It sets a maximum of 500 million U.S. dollars per year depending on the Treasury availability.

**[01:32:09.023] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001014)  
And according to this law, the priority, coastal defense, long-range fires, maritime security, integrated air defense.

**[01:32:17.360] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001015)  
So, I know that this will not be transferred to our Treasury because it's in the nature of a foreign military financing.

**[01:32:23.485] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001017)  
But do you agree that this will help boost our modernization program and help solve the budgetary constraint problem?

**[01:32:31.973] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001018)  
It will help, and together with the official security assistance that Japan gives us for domain awareness, Your Honor.

**[01:32:40.816] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001019)  
Maybe DBM knows about this. That's the reason why they don't give you much allocation for modernization fund.

**[01:32:46.030] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001020)  
No, Your Honor. Probably, it's really the fiscal consideration of the country on a macro level.

**[01:32:54.805] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001021)  
So, then, it was reported in the Korean media in the past months that the Navy plans to acquire two more Malvar-class missile frigates.

**[01:33:05.487] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001022)  
In addition, the two frigates ordered last year, so that would be the fifth and sixth guided missile frigate of the Navy.

**[01:33:13.942] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001023)  
Are you at liberty to confirm this proposal?

**[01:33:19.004] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001024)  
Definitely, Your Honor. There is an intent, really, to acquire.

**[01:33:23.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001025)  
The trigger has not yet been pulled.

**[01:33:28.471] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001026)  
And also, every budget hearing, I make this follow-up regarding the upgrading and the completion,

**[01:33:35.795] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001027)  
the installation of the still-unfitted sensors and weapon system for the Rizal-class and Malvar-class.

**[01:33:45.076] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001028)  
Your Honor, they are ongoing.

**[01:33:46.038] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001029)  
Thank you. Thank you.

**[01:33:48.974] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001030)  
So, also, some people ask me to relate this to you.

**[01:33:55.235] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001031)  
How about the plan?

**[01:33:56.000] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001032)  
As for the acquisition of multi-role fighters, I know that this is a very complicated matter.

**[01:34:00.415] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001033)  
And for additional air defense missiles.

**[01:34:04.988] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001034)  
And also, coastal defense missiles.

**[01:34:06.828] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001035)  
It's still there, Your Honor, depending on the financing that we need.

**[01:34:11.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001036)  
But now, knowing what we know, we have to have a lot of systems in place before any multi-role fighter is acquired.

**[01:34:23.753] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001037)  
Because a multi-role fighter...

**[01:34:25.119] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001038)  
A multi-role fighter, as we saw recently, is heavily dependent on ground control and domain awareness.

**[01:34:34.159] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001039)  
If not, it will be an unguided pilot-controlled aircraft, and its use will be marginal.

**[01:34:47.463] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001041)  
So, maybe it's, Your Honor, without an air-to-air refueling, it will not be able to take off with adequate stores.

**[01:34:56.542] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001042)  
And if we do not have the adequate hangarage facilities to service it, it will be useless, Your Honor.

**[01:35:09.704] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001043)  
So, it comes as a force package.

**[01:35:11.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001044)  
And as we learned, the minimum force package is 40 at any one mission, actually, for external defense.

**[01:35:24.571] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001045)  
So, maybe, really, the amendment of PD 415 can help.

**[01:35:28.115] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001046)  
It can help in obtaining funding for your programs.

**[01:35:30.400] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001047)  
So, finally, Madam Chair, the Secretary, of course, is aware that it is the Executive Department

**[01:35:36.552] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001048)  
that prepared this program of expenditures under the Constitution, not the Congress.

**[01:35:43.977] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001049)  
And Congress has no power to increase the appropriations proposed by the President.

**[01:35:50.204] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001050)  
We can do that, but we will get it from other items, which is very difficult on the part of Congress to do.

**[01:35:57.578] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001051)  
So, the real battle here is at the level of the DBCC, the DBM, not here in Congress.

**[01:36:05.307] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001052)  
I believe the connectivity and infrastructure are also necessary, Your Honor, for us to perform our mission roles.

**[01:36:12.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001053)  
So, if the infrastructure budget will support the weight, really, of the equipment we need,

**[01:36:19.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001054)  
it will solve a lot of our problems, too, on logistics.

**[01:36:22.857] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001055)  
And I think that's the emphasis.

**[01:36:27.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001056)  
Of course, on education, too.

**[01:36:30.012] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001057)  
And Madam Chair, no more questions.

**[01:36:33.472] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001058)  
But in spite of budgetary constraints, our AFP and DND, they are doing their best to secure our territory and our sovereignty.

**[01:36:41.605] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001059)  
So, on behalf of the people of my district, let me extend my sincerest appreciation to them.

**[01:36:47.520] Rep. Arnan C. Panaligan** (asr-001060)  
Thank you for your service to our officers and men.

**[01:36:50.988] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001061)  
Thank you, Congressman Panaligan.

**[01:36:53.654] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001062)  
Next to interpolate, Congressman Arlyn Ayon from the minority.

**[01:36:59.341] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001063)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:37:01.920] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001064)  
Good morning, Mr. Secretary, to our distinguished members of the committee, our officers and men of the Armed Forces of the Philippines,

**[01:37:10.560] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001065)  
and all the officials and guests present here today.

**[01:37:14.719] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001066)  
First, I would like to express my appreciation to you, Mr. Secretary, for your leadership and for the various initiatives you have undertaken

**[01:37:24.560] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001067)  
to strengthen our national defense.

**[01:37:25.680] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001068)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:37:26.639] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001071)  
I am one of those who strongly support the Department of National Defense.

**[01:37:31.679] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001072)  
Coming from Bukidnon, a province that has experienced the reality of insurgency,

**[01:37:36.560] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001073)  
I have personally witnessed how our gallant men and women of the Armed Forces of the Philippines

**[01:37:43.520] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001074)  
have courageously protected our communities and our nation.

**[01:37:50.320] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001075)  
Today, we are very happy to share that Bukidnon has become one of our country's emerging tourist destination.

**[01:37:57.920] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001076)  
And I believe that this progress has been made possible in large part because of the stable peace and security that our province now enjoys.

**[01:38:07.119] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001077)  
Our active soldiers have contributed tremendously, not only for our national security, but also to our economic and national development.

**[01:38:15.679] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001078)  
But, Mr. Secretary, I would also like to take this opportunity to highlight our veterans, both our uniformed personnel and the civilian human resources

**[01:38:25.679] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001079)  
who have devoted many years to the defense of our country.

**[01:38:39.920] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001080)  
I believe that we must never forget that these heroes who once carried the burden of protecting our nation.

**[01:38:49.760] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001081)  
May I therefore ask, Mr. Secretary, in the Department's plans and budget priorities,

**[01:38:56.560] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001082)  
have we given equal weight and importance to the welfare of our veterans throughout the country,

**[01:39:02.880] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001083)  
particularly those who are now elderly and facing increasing health care and living expenses?

**[01:39:09.600] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001084)  
And finally, how can we as lawmakers work together with the Department to properly honor these heroes,

**[01:39:17.760] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001085)  
not merely through words of gratitude, but through concrete programs, adequate benefits, health care,

**[01:39:24.719] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001086)  
and support for those who are suffering from illnesses and long-term effects of the stress, trauma, and sacrifices they endured in the service of our nation?

**[01:39:34.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001087)  
Thank you very much to the distinguished colleague, former distinguished member of the House.

**[01:39:43.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001088)  
I believe that it's well appropriate that you raise the issue.

**[01:39:50.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001089)  
And I think what we should do, really, is to decouple.

**[01:39:58.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001090)  
We should decouple the veterans' pensions from active duty salaries.

**[01:40:04.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001091)  
So we can adjust their pensions without adjusting necessarily the pay increases for our active duty personnel.

**[01:40:16.159] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001092)  
Actually, the recent increases in pay were due to a compromise we reached with the

**[01:40:25.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001094)  
veterans to increase their pension rates 5% for three years, in total 15% by 2028.

**[01:40:35.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001095)  
But unless the increases are decoupled by law from the active duty rates,

**[01:40:50.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001096)  
you need to increase the rates of the active duty personnel.

**[01:40:57.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001097)  
Secondly, Your Honor, we are trying really to reach out to them.

**[01:41:00.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001098)  
And we still are hopefully going to be able to launch our first hospice hopefully by the end of the year

**[01:41:15.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001099)  
for taking care of the older veterans who have no place to call home

**[01:41:21.119] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001100)  
and who are forced to be confined in hospitals.

**[01:41:25.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001101)  
Because there's nowhere else to go.

**[01:41:27.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001102)  
Our issue there is the accreditation of the caregivers and the finding of caregivers

**[01:41:34.719] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001103)  
and the building of the facility.

**[01:41:38.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001104)  
Nonetheless, we have to get our feet wet with it in order to provide them adequate

**[01:41:46.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001105)  
a place where they can retire with dignity.

**[01:41:51.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001106)  
Thirdly, Your Honor, it's knowing fully well that

**[01:41:55.199] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001107)  
the veterans need to adequate socialization, if not some of them confined to home really suffer.

**[01:42:12.239] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001108)  
And I've seen it with even younger veterans who were very active before but when withdrawn from their comfort zone,

**[01:42:22.719] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001109)  
so to speak, have become.

**[01:42:26.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001110)  
Have become, you know, very withdrawn and their families talk to me about it.

**[01:42:34.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001111)  
Fourthly, Your Honor, a lot of the predicament of veterans in terms of health

**[01:42:39.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001112)  
can be addressed preventively while they are still in active duty.

**[01:42:44.639] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001113)  
That's why the active duty care right now, when we are seeing the onset of diseases like heart disease,

**[01:42:53.350] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001114)  
diabetes,

**[01:42:55.199] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001115)  
heart attacks at a younger age, right now at 35,

**[01:42:58.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001116)  
we've seen a lot of people really get affected by strokes.

**[01:43:06.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001117)  
So we'll have to be more proactive in monitoring the diseases

**[01:43:15.199] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001118)  
which may exacerbate their situation when they become veterans and require extensive care,

**[01:43:23.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001119)  
which could have been prevented.

**[01:43:24.660] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001120)  
So it's not a big deal for us to do that.

**[01:43:27.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001121)  
But it is a big problem and it's not something that is perfectly anticipated

**[01:43:29.119] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001122)  
had we known of the situation beforehand.

**[01:43:34.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001123)  
So, Your Honor, I think on the pension system, while not opposed to pension reform,

**[01:43:42.239] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001124)  
I think the key there is not to lump the AFP retirees with the others.

**[01:43:48.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001125)  
I know it's kind of controversial, but the AFP is a national force that is part of the national system.

**[01:43:56.239] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001126)  
receives no subsidies from any other agency.

**[01:44:01.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001127)  
Unlike, let's say, the BFP or the PNP,

**[01:44:05.199] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001128)  
they have more roles with the local communities that they are there to serve.

**[01:44:15.119] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001129)  
And so the basis of the pension on the basis of fairness should be segregated

**[01:44:22.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001130)  
and agencies or communities which derive benefits from directed or targeted services

**[01:44:30.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001131)  
like PJMP or PNP should share in the pension burden.

**[01:44:34.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001132)  
Unlike the AFP, which is a purely sovereign and national force.

**[01:44:41.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001133)  
I realize, really, that there will come a time when pensions, if not reformed now,

**[01:44:52.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001134)  
will be more important.

**[01:44:54.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001135)  
Will eat up.

**[01:44:55.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001136)  
will eat up a lot of fiscal space.

**[01:44:57.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001137)  
Nonetheless, if at this day and age,

**[01:45:04.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001138)  
at this stage of our national development

**[01:45:07.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001139)  
where the AFP is fully engaged with the law troop ceiling

**[01:45:12.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001140)  
on several mission areas 365 days of the year,

**[01:45:18.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001141)  
I think they need the peace of mind.

**[01:45:23.199] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001142)  
And since the retirees also form part of the AFP family

**[01:45:27.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001143)  
who are there helping each and every step of the way,

**[01:45:35.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001144)  
I think at this stage, it's best the pension be studied

**[01:45:40.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001145)  
unlike the old RSPS system which was not a sustainable system.

**[01:45:50.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001146)  
And until we are...

**[01:45:51.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001147)  
We are sure that there is a full-proof system

**[01:45:55.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001148)  
which will guarantee them peace of mind when they retire

**[01:46:01.679] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001149)  
and which will motivate them to commit themselves.

**[01:46:07.679] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001150)  
And if not the same way, perhaps even a greater way

**[01:46:12.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001151)  
to mission accomplishment,

**[01:46:15.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001152)  
then I think it's best to err on the side of giving peace of mind

**[01:46:19.860] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001153)  
to our activities.

**[01:46:20.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001154)  
To our duty personnel and our veterans.

**[01:46:28.480] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001155)  
Thank you for that, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:46:30.480] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001156)  
That's our concern.

**[01:46:31.440] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001157)  
So hopefully, and can BND assure this committee

**[01:46:36.960] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001158)  
that no Filipino veterans who serve our country honorably

**[01:46:40.880] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001159)  
will be left behind because of lack of funds,

**[01:46:43.440] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001160)  
delayed benefits, or inadequate health care?

**[01:46:48.239] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001161)  
Rest assured, we are at the forefront of fighting for veterans' benefits.

**[01:46:52.159] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001162)  
And my personal connection with our close relationship

**[01:46:58.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001163)  
with veterans' groups of whatever sort,

**[01:47:00.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001164)  
and particularly even our retired enlisted personnel

**[01:47:04.719] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001165)  
and our retired NCOs and civilian personnel

**[01:47:08.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001166)  
because they develop a lot of expertise.

**[01:47:15.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001167)  
Post-retirement, actually, we still try to use them as best as we can.

**[01:47:21.679] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001168)  
Keeping them occupied.

**[01:47:22.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001169)  
At the same time, while training the next generation

**[01:47:27.199] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001170)  
of people who will succeed in leadership positions, particularly.

**[01:47:32.719] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001171)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:47:33.600] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001172)  
That will be all, Madam Chairman.

**[01:47:35.600] Rep. Arlyn P. Ayon** (asr-001173)  
Thank you.

**[01:47:36.159] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001174)  
Thank you to the other.

**[01:47:37.679] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001175)  
Thank you, Congressman Ayon.

**[01:47:40.320] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001176)  
Next interpellate, Congressman Munir Erbison from the majority.

**[01:47:49.720] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-001177)  
Magandang umaga, Madam Chairman.

**[01:47:54.640] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-001179)  
Magandang umaga sa lahat.

**[01:48:00.080] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-001180)  
Just want to make a manifestation.

**[01:48:04.620] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-001181)  
I would like to express my full support for the Department of National Defense

**[01:48:10.280] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-001182)  
and recognize its important role in maintaining peace and security

**[01:48:14.520] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-001183)  
in the 2nd District of Sulu.

**[01:48:15.880] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-001184)  
We are grateful to the DND and the Armed Forces of the Philippines

**[01:48:20.020] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-001185)  
for their continued presence and support.

**[01:48:24.460] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-001186)  
In our communities, their efforts have helped us sustain peace

**[01:48:29.360] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-001187)  
and create a better environment for the development of the province of Sulu.

**[01:48:37.580] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-001188)  
We hope for their continued support as we work together to preserve peace

**[01:48:42.880] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-001189)  
and progress we have achieved.

**[01:48:45.880] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-001190)  
Dati-dati, ang tinatanong ng mga kababayan natin,

**[01:48:52.040] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-001191)  
Kumusta ng Sulu?

**[01:48:54.360] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-001192)  
Ang daming sabi niya mga problema.

**[01:48:57.180] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-001193)  
Pero ngayon, ang tinatanong kung anong magandang beach sa Sulu kasi peaceful.

**[01:49:04.000] Rep. Munir N. Arbison Jr.** (asr-001194)  
Maraming salamat.

**[01:49:06.980] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001195)  
Papasalamat po ako, magsukul, sa ating kapatid na taga-Sulu.

**[01:49:11.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001196)  
Malaki pong tulong sa atin ang katahimikan sa Sulu.

**[01:49:15.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001197)  
At iyan po ay magbibigay po ng oportunidad sa ating WestMinCom.

**[01:49:23.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001198)  
Lalo na ang Naval Forces West.

**[01:49:27.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001199)  
Na mag-perform po ng functions to guard the Sulu Sea passage na papasok

**[01:49:41.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001200)  
sa ating major sea lane or international sea lane of communication sa internal waters po natin.

**[01:49:50.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001201)  
So marami pong salamat sa dati kong classmate, si Congressman Arbison,

**[01:49:56.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001202)  
sa katahimikan, sa Sulu, at hope to see you soon.

**[01:50:08.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001203)  
Thank you. Thank you, Congressman Munir Arbison.

**[01:50:12.480] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001204)  
Next to interpellate from the minority, Congressman Antonio Tinio.

**[01:50:20.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001205)  
Salamat, Chair. Meron pong powerpoint kung pwede. Pero tutuloy ko na habang hihintay natin. Una yung tungkol pa rin sa pension reform, MUP pension reform. Nung 2015 ang budget para sa MUP pension was 68.6 billion. Ngayong 2027, the proposed budget for pension is 154 billion. So mahigit nag-doble na po. Nag-discuss na kanina pero sumasang-ayon po ba ang DND na urgent. Kailangan na pong mapatupad yung pag-reforma. At ang pinaka-reforma na kailangan ay kailangan magkaroon na ng contribution system ang AFP at uniformed personnel, katulad nung ibang mga government employees at saka private na nagkocontribute sa SSS, may counterpart, at doon ang gagaling ang pensyon. Hindi yung kasalukuyan na buong-buo na sinasubsidized ng GAA.

**[01:51:32.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001217)  
Ito po ang aking kasagutan. Yung 154 po, katulad nung naipaliwanag dating kanina, 49% lang po doon ang nasa AFP. Umakyat po dahil humingi po ang ating mga veterano ng adjustment. Dahil wala pong adjustment for the past years. Ako po hindi po tutol sa pension reform, lamang kung papaano ito ma-implement. Kailangan po pag-aaralan natin ang efekto ng engganyo na sumama po sa serbisyo. And actually, yung risk po na hinaharap na pwede pong lalong-lalo na po yung mga totally disabled na bata, yun po ang talagang malaki. Kasi automatic discharge po na magkakaroon siya ng benefits based on his current rank. So hindi po ako tutol kahit nasa contributions but when will we start it and who will be affected. So it has to be studied. And the most important thing po is to separate the armed forces from the rest of the civilian agencies. Katulad po ng PNP at saka BJMP, part of the service is local or a major part. So it's also fair that the LGUs share the pension burden. Ngayon po, yung disposable income po nila naiiwan din sa pamilya. Hindi wala po akong debate yung mga teacher natin. Malaki din po ang risk. Lalo-lalo na nakikita po natin yung teacher na tumatawid sa baha para pumunta lang sa eskwelahan. Ngunit ang pakiusap lang, pag-aaralan po itong mabuti at kung kailan po i-implement.

**[01:53:44.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001236)  
So, Chair, pakalawang beses binanggit ni Secretary yung pangangailangan ihiwalay yung pension ng military sa civilian uniform. I suppose. So, yun yung police, BJMP, Bucor, etc. At ang kuha ko sa sinasabi nyo, dapat may contribution din ng LGU sa kanilang pensyon. Could you elaborate on this? Parang ngayon ko lang narinig ang ganitong proposal. Parang sinasabi, ngayon, national government ang buong-buong nagbabayad ng pensyon. So, ang sinasuggest nyo ba dapat kalgahinan ng LGU? At least a part of it? Of the civilian uniform personnel pension?

**[01:54:31.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001245)  
Opo, dapat pag-aaralan po natin. Hindi ko po sinasabi that is one way. Pero kailangan po i-examine po natin dahil ang servisyo ng police is basically local, no? Except for national crime-fighting authorities. So, in terms of burden sharing, dahil yung era po, alam naman po natin that that goes in block, which is undiminished. I think we might want to study the fiscal impact of burden sharing for them. I'm not saying that that's a cut-and-dried solution. Pero tingnan po natin para naman ma-ease yung pension burden ng ating national government.

**[01:55:16.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001252)  
Okay. Ngayon ko lang narinig ito na burden sharing ang ibig sabihin ipakarga yung pensyon ng police. At iba pang uniform aside from military sa LGU. Pero...

**[01:55:32.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001255)  
Hindi po totally. Marami pong financial permutations na puwedeng mangyari on an actuarial basis. So importante din po talaga itong pag-aralan on an actuarial and sustainable basis.

**[01:55:53.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001259)  
Pero ang basic po doon: sa ngayon, kayo, dapat may contribution?

**[01:55:58.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001261)  
It depends po on when and how. Sa ngayon po, nakatutok po at punong-puno ngayon yung ating sundalo dahil halos 90% na yung fill-up. Hindi po ako, in conscience, puwedeng humingi sa kanila ng contribution sa ngayon. And we are requiring them to do more, insofar as I'm concerned.

**[01:56:22.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001273)  
So kayo, sa ngayon, tingin ninyo hindi dapat hingan ng contribution?

**[01:56:29.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001276)  
Hindi po dapat. Tingnan po natin kung may sustainable na programa na kakayanin naman po nila at patas po sa kanila; we will consider it. We will be open to it. All proposals for easing the fiscal burden are open, but there are no definitives until we see the plan.

**[01:56:56.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001290)  
Hindi po bago yung mga proposals for pension reform. I mean, sa totoo lang, nung una akong pumasok sa Kongreso, pinag-uusapan na po ito. I would imagine, nung nasa Kongreso pa kayo, issue na rin ito. At isa sa mga proposal doon po doon na yung mga bagong papasok, sa kanila ipapatupad yung contribution system. Ibig sabihin, yung mga nasa servisyo na, pwedeng hindi na. Sabi nyo nga, parang may kontrata yan, nung pumasok sila, kailangang i-fulfill yun. Pero sa mga bago, hindi ba pwedeng ganon?

**[01:57:32.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001307)  
Actually po, that would make sense because then you have time for the pension plan to mature. So that, may drawdown. Pag unang drawdown po doon sa pension system na iyon, yung mga nag-contribute na magbe-benefit. So i-carry na lang yung mga [unclear]. Siyempre, maaari din po iyon. Iyon nga lang, pag-aralan lang po natin yung optional at mandatory retirements kasi baka naman po maubos yung optional at makaapekto din yung pension sa kung magkano ang sahod. Depende na po sa actuarial. Lalo-lalo na po kung may legislated benefits. Iyon po ang problema. Iyon po ang kakain sa buhay ng pondo. But, you know, sa bagong entry po na mag-contribution, and then if you can design a pension system that will benefit them to allow the system to mature, why not? We can consider it all.

**[01:58:34.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001339)  
Okay. May baka tanong pa, pero let me move on to another issue. Recently, the Secretary made statements regarding Chinese language teaching and cultural exchange sa mga Filipino-Chinese schools dito sa Pilipinas. Could you elaborate on your concern regarding this?

**[01:58:58.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001351)  
It was a matter of concern, Your Honor, because it was reported that the training was done by Jinan University. And Jinan University was the source of the supposed study that Batanes was part of China. And we all know, Your Honor, that this is a state university, and depending on how you look at things, I think if it's state-sponsored, then you mean party-sponsored. And so, it is a matter of concern that even as a normal parent, that Chinese language teachers who may even be teaching minors are coming from the same institution that published these kinds of supposed academic studies. It's a matter of concern, but our agency has alerted other agencies, and we're not actively pursuing anything on it.

**[02:00:03.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001380)  
Mayroon po kayong statement sa isang Kapihan sa Manila Bay Forum. Sabi ninyo rito na malaking ebidensya na itong mga eskwelahan ay ginagamit sa malign influence. Ang tinutukoy ninyo po ay yung mga local Filipino-Chinese schools. Hindi naman lahat, pero mayroon diyang malign influence. Kaya dapat maging masusi tayo, hindi lamang sa university kundi sa mga mababang paaralan kung saan may cultural and language and other exchanges. Sabi ninyo po may ebidensya, malaking ebidensya na may mga eskwelahan.

**[02:00:40.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001387)  
It's res ipsa loquitur on their part. If they publish something, I was referring to the university, Jinan.

**[02:00:50.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001389)  
So ang tinutukoy ninyo po rito ay yung university, hindi yung mga school. I-clarify po natin kasi very sensitive po ito.

**[02:00:57.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001391)  
Yes, I beg to clarify. Thank the gentleman for asking the clarification. It's [unclear] University because...

**[02:01:03.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001393)  
So hindi ninyo po tinutukoy yung mga local Chinese schools?

**[02:01:06.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001394)  
We have no evidence.

**[02:01:07.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001395)  
You have no evidence?

**[02:01:08.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001396)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:01:09.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001397)  
Okay, thank you for that clarification. Kasi nga po, parang ang... We don't want to cause panic.

**[02:01:14.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001399)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:01:15.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001400)  
Yes, especially among the Filipino-Chinese community.

**[02:01:19.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001402)  
Okay. Now, of course, sangayon tayo na talagang kabalbalan yung claim nung Jinan University na ang Batanes ay bahagi ng Pilipinas. Pero itong... Let me wind up, Madam Chair. Itong issue na to ay nagmula sa isang report ng SeaLight. Ayan po, that is the report. The university that said batanes is China is also training Manila's Chinese teachers. Okay. This is an article published by Juan Ray Powell, the director of SeaLight. Ngayon, itong SeaLight po, are you aware of the funding and the origins of SeaLight?

**[02:02:16.660] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001409)  
I'm not aware of the funding, Your Honor, but I know the individual.

**[02:02:20.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001410)  
Si Ray Powell?

**[02:02:21.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001411)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:02:21.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001412)  
Of course. Si Ray Powell, of course, is actually laman ng balita yan, lumalabas sa media often, and he talks about these issues. Now, next slide po. Actually, yung kanyang SeaLight, you are... I don't know, maybe... Ito ang lumalabas po, na yung kanyang SeaLight is a project of the Gordian Knot Center for... The Gordian Knot Center for Strategic Innovation. Tama po. Is that correct?

**[02:02:55.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001418)  
I'm not in a position to confirm or deny, Your Honor.

**[02:02:58.010] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001419)  
Next slide po. Yung Gordian Knot Center for... Sorry, it's called Stanford University's Gordian Knot Center for National Security Innovation. Okay. According to this article published on the navy.mil website, meaning to say U.S. Navy, website, this was established by the Office of Naval Research, which is a part of the U.S. Navy. So, next slide. And so, the director, ayan na po, the Office of Naval Research, which is part of the U.S. Navy. And, next slide. Yung kanyang Sea... This is the social media Twitter account of Ray Powell, and his handle is Gordian Knot Ray. Yun yung koneksyon. So, in other words, itong si Ray Powell, na aktibong-aktibo sa media ng Pilipinas, and in fact, sets the media agenda, dahil siya yung unang naglabas nung report regarding the Chinese University, apparently, ay, you know, nakaugnay pala sa U.S. Navy. Yung kanyang center ay tinatag ng U.S. Navy. So, hindi po ba maituturing din ito? In the same way na yung ginagawa ng China ay tinatawag na foreign information manipulation and interference, ay, di ba, pwede rin, dapat din ituring na foreign information manipulation, and interference, ang SeaLight at itong si Ray Powell. In other words, kailangan may disclosure din, at may transparency at disclosure sa pinanggagalingan ditong Ray Powell, at madalas na sina-cite ng DND, ng Coast Guard, si Admiral Tarriela, at iba pa.

**[02:05:16.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001440)  
I think that that's up to him to address, Your Honor. For the Gordian Knot Center to address. For us, we have our own independent sources. Now, what's important here is what the information is. Is it accurate or not? When we compare a Batanes narrative with activities in the West Philippine Sea, the proof of the pudding is in the eating. And what we are after in foreign malign influence is misinformation. So, and, and, not information per se, it's misinformation. So, at the end of the day, the credibility will lie on who has the more accurate information. But in terms of his connections, that is for him to disclose.

**[02:06:11.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001451)  
Of course. Okay. So, agad ko na lang po yung tanong ko, pero meron pa akong mga tanong. So, next round na lang po. Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:06:19.220] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001453)  
Yes. Thank you. Thank you, Congressman Tinio. Next to interpolate from the majority, Representative Johanne Bautista.

**[02:06:28.100] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001456)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. And good morning po to the members of the committee, my fellow colleagues, and of course, Secretary Teodoro and the DND family. Magandang umaga po sa inyong lahat. I will be asking questions po pertaining to our uniform personnel. I'm not sure if it's classified information. To ask, more or less, ilan po ba ang ating uniform personnel? And if that is classified information, may I ask, Madam Chair, magkano po ba sa pondo ng department ang allocated for the uniforms and fabrics of our uniform personnel, more or less, Madam Chair?

**[02:07:14.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001466)  
For the uniform personnel, Your Honor, we have 162,000. As for the clothing, I'm not too sure. Your Honor, because we have to aggregate, the clothing is per service. We don't have the aggregated amount at this time. But we can provide you with the information.

**[02:07:43.500] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001471)  
All right, Madam Chair. We can await that figures. The reason I'm asking this, Madam Chair, is that pertaining to RA 9242 or the Philippine Tropical Fabrics, this was passed in 2004, and this requires the use of Philippine Tropical Fabrics for government uniforms and to support locally manufactured products, local resources, and promote employment, no? This is for our local industries. Particularly po, yung IRR po niya, strengthened implementation by requiring local sourcing and the DOST PTRI testing and certification, and there are also allowed exemptions. In other words, it is not enough that our uniforms are made in the country, it is required that we use a certain amount of Philippine fabrics. So, may we ask kung wala man pong ano for the department, Madam Chair, to submit if they have projected figures on what would the budget to be spent on RA 9242 compliant Philippine-made fabrics. Madam Chair.

**[02:08:57.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001484)  
I don't have the figure right now, Your Honor, because the fabric must not only be Philippine-made, but must conform to minimum standards of safety and fireproofing that we have. We know that an indigenous fabric industry is hard to get, because we don't have indigenous cotton and other fabrics which actually cannot be substituted based on the safety considerations. Nonetheless, Your Honor, we will provide you with the information, and it's not a question of the manufacturer, really. It's the sourcing of the fabric, and we have to resource fabric, actually, to more trusted sources, meaning to say countries which, if at all, are not involved in disputes with us.

**[02:09:57.180] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001494)  
Thank you for that, Madam Chair and Mr. Secretary. Now, going to the issue of local sourcing, Madam Chair, if the Philippine manufacturers cannot yet supply all of the, or at least some of, the uniform requirements or meet certain military specifications, meron po bang ginagawa ang department together with the DOST, PTRI, and the local industry to at least address that gap and eventually comply with this law?

**[02:10:28.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001500)  
Thank you for the question, Your Honor. Honestly, I have not had the chance to devote time with the DOST and with the local fabric industry, because I'm really concerned now on our supply chains more than anything. So, it's something I'll schedule to look into it on a department-wide basis. But then, it will depend also on the production of, and the agricultural production that we have for cotton. And I know that for silk, for example, for parachutes, there were some who tried to venture into silk, but there was no market unless silk was branded with a country. So, the experiments failed.

**[02:11:19.760] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001510)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. Actually, I think the IRR of the law that I'm mentioning, only requires 5% to use Philippine fabrics. And I believe that the Army, under former General Navarrete, I read somewhere that they actually started meeting with the DOST on the matter. Now, I think as regards the issue of local sourcing, it's just a suggestion and maybe the department can also help by providing maybe projected needs. In order for us to help develop our local industry for the fabrics. And at the same time, comply po with the requirement of using at least 5% of Philippine fabric for our uniforms, Madam Chair.

**[02:12:11.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001521)  
I was informed to the Honorable Representative and Madam Chair that the issue there is our battle dress uniforms, which have safety standards. But for the general office attire, I think there is some component of Philippine fabrics and of course the office barongs.

**[02:12:35.360] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001527)  
Yes, Madam Chair. Just to me, if we are able to communicate the needed requirements, standards, then hopefully maybe we can help develop or grow that industry and locally source our own products for the making of our uniforms, Madam Chair.

**[02:12:58.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001532)  
I think, Your Honor, I will welcome the idea because locally made should not necessarily be locally grown, just as long as the growing is on a Philippine [unclear] country basis, like the experiment made with the beef industry before, where we had no grazing land and the original plan was to have a beef or cattle industry somewhere else where land was cheap and just imported for the Philippines. So we really have to be creative about these arrangements that we make, particularly if all our arable land is for food production rather than non-food items.

**[02:13:46.120] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001543)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. I think that's a very valuable suggestion. We welcome it. Yes, Madam Chair and Mr. Secretary. And anyway, the requirement so far of the law is only 5% of the entire uniform, I think. Now, moving on, Madam Chair, under RA 12024 or the Self-Reliant Defense Posture Law, it directs the government to strengthen the local defense industry, support in-country enterprises, promote technology transfer, develop resilient supply chains, and prioritize local employment. For the 2027 budget, Madam Chair, can the Department provide us on how much procurement or program spending is expected to support local production, local maintenance, Filipino suppliers, more or less, Madam Chair?

**[02:14:40.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001559)  
There will be, honestly speaking, none, Your Honor. Why? The law is fatally flawed. It does not provide for co-equity and co-investment, and it does not provide for research and development. So it will not meet the needs of a future armed force. So unless you put in the law provision for equity or research and development, there will be no right-thinking producer of technology that will allow you to benefit on the upside when you do not share the risk of the downside. That is a simple value proposition, Your Honor. Because the most important thing is not the production, even though 100% production is done outside. It's the intellectual property rights that are important, that you have equal rights to the IPR. That is the key and the cornerstone. If you cannot acquire the intellectual property rights, then you have no rights at all, even though you are co-producing. There will be no technology transfer. That's a value proposition that those who have been in the private sector know and know fully well.

**[02:15:59.840] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001597)  
Thank you, Madam Chair and Mr. Secretary, for that very straightforward answer. And I think it is the position of the Secretary that there is a need to amend this law?

**[02:16:11.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001603)  
Definitely, Your Honor. And you can download the R&D for it to the DOST. If you don't have a budget for risk capital, then you cannot derive any benefits whatsoever. It's just like people in the mining industry. If you don't spend for the exploration, which is risky, I mean, how can you value something properly?

**[02:16:38.780] Rep. Johanne Monich G. Bautista** (asr-001616)  
Thank you, Madam Chair and Mr. Secretary. Just wanted to thank the Secretary and commit to look into the proposed suggestions of Mr. Secretary to amend the law and ensure its implementation. Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:16:57.300] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-001625)  
Thank you, Congressman Johanne Bautista. Uh, at this point, I would like to acknowledge the additional, uh, House members, um, who are present today. **[02:17:39.820] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001645)  
Good morning, Secretary Teodoro and the rest of the DND family. My father was my late father was a former member of the Philippine Air Force. A non-commissioned officer before he worked with Air America in Vietnam. So we have a long relations with the U.S. At the same time, we have also a very long relations with China. With the U.S. militarily, with China, economically. So foreign policy really is as I see it now, it's really, you know, I think there's a divergence between the statements of President Marcos and Secretary Teodoro. Well, it might be a strategic way of doing things, but there's also dangers. So let me just let me just for example, President Marcos says that we need a reset in terms of relations with China. Secretary Teodoro says broader defense engagement with China is impossible because China lacks good faith. Marcos says China remains an important investment and economic partner. Secretary Teodoro says China uses coercion, blackmail, and foreign influence. President Marcos discusses joint oil exploration. Meanwhile, the Philippine defense system becomes increasingly interoperable with the United States, especially with the EDCA sites. So, there might be a decline on the trust on both countries, the U.S. and China. Ayoko na pong ayoko pong pangunahan yung strategy as a strategy. As I very well know that the President is the chief architect of foreign policy. However, there are public concerns. At ito po yung gusto ko sana itanong. First question, po, Secretary. What exactly triggers the Mutual Defense Treaty?

**[02:20:26.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001725)  
The triggering of Article 5 of the Mutual Defense Treaty, is a call of the Philippine government or the U.S. government if an attack is made on either country, public vessel, and the like. So, the trigger really is a call of an executive or the triggering party. Here, it will be the President of the Philippines based on an attack, or any public vessel, if he feels that Article 5, which is the active portion of the Mutual Defense Treaty, comes into play.

**[02:21:18.680] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001747)  
What happens if China and the U.S. engage in the Taiwan War? What are the possibilities of the Philippines participating in this?

**[02:21:33.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001753)  
The President has made clear, Your Honor, that the Philippines is constitutionally prohibited from engaging in foreign—

**[02:21:40.740] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001757)  
Pardon?

**[02:21:40.980] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001758)  
The President has made clear that the Philippines is prohibited from engaging in any offensive activity, so it's purely defensive.

**[02:21:49.900] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001762)  
But the U.S. might use the EDCA sites, several EDCA sites in the Philippines to launch their attacks.

**[02:22:00.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001767)  
That's a speculative thing, might, but it has not.

**[02:22:03.740] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001770)  
It's a reality.

**[02:22:06.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001771)  
Under the President's guidance, it cannot.

**[02:22:12.800] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001773)  
So it remains to be speculative as far as you are concerned?

**[02:22:16.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001775)  
Oh, sure, Your Honor. It's speculative, but insofar as the President's guidance is concerned, it cannot be used for any external factor.

**[02:22:25.500] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001779)  
Can they use the Philippine territory during the Taiwan conflict?

**[02:22:29.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001781)  
The EDCA sites? It can only be used if, Your Honor, the Philippines is under attack or threat.

**[02:22:37.580] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001786)  
So this is very clear in the...

**[02:22:40.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001788)  
Oh, yes, Your Honor.

**[02:22:41.220] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001789)  
...in the treaty.

**[02:22:41.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001790)  
It is clear. And under the agreement on enhanced defense cooperation, it cannot violate any Philippine law or the Constitution.

**[02:22:49.440] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001796)  
Could U.S. offensive missiles be permanently positioned here?

**[02:22:54.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001798)  
If they're with the Philippines, Your Honor, then we will welcome them if they're under the Philippine armed forces.

**[02:23:01.990] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001803)  
Pardon, Mr. Secretary, I can barely hear you.

**[02:23:03.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001805)  
The Philippine arsenal, Your Honor.

**[02:23:10.620] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001808)  
so they can launch a missile attack to China?

**[02:23:13.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001810)  
The Philippines will launch, Your Honor, if ever. If they become part of the armed forces of the Philippines, we will welcome them.

**[02:23:20.180] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001816)  
So your answer is they cannot use offensive missiles from Philippine territory? From Philippine territory.

**[02:23:29.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001821)  
No, Your Honor, for offensive purposes, unless Article 5 is activated. It's to defend the Philippines.

**[02:23:38.240] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001825)  
And what is our long-term West Philippine Sea Objective?

**[02:23:43.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001828)  
The long-term West Philippine Sea Objective, Your Honor, it remains the same under the Constitution. It's to explore and exploit for the Filipinos each and every item in there that we can.

**[02:23:56.760] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001839)  
So, how would you relate this to the pronouncement of the President that we can have joint oil exploration to China?

**[02:24:06.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001846)  
Well, that's subject to Philippine law, Your Honor, and the Constitution.

**[02:24:13.500] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001850)

**[02:24:34.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001862)

**[02:26:48.900] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001969)  
But no espionage or military issues?

**[02:26:55.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001973)
