**[02:46:19.919] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002300)  
Lalo-lalo na po ngayon, ang dami dyan sa mga areas na yan ay sa barm area na nakatutok po yung mga tao para maiwasan kung anumang violence na mangyari.

**[02:46:27.759] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002301)  
At yan po ay ino-augmentan pa namin ng tao para lang masigurado na successful yung election.

**[02:46:35.599] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002302)  
So susubukan po natin na bago plenary.

**[02:46:43.439] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002303)  
Pero kung hindi nun po namin…

**[02:46:51.279] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002304)  
Kung hindi namin maabot, it needs to be settled also between us.

**[02:46:55.399] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002305)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[02:46:59.519] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002306)  
Dapat po hindi po mayag ang Committee on Appropriations na hindi magkaroon ng maayos na report dito.

**[02:47:03.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002307)  
Hindi lang po sa DND, pero maski po sa DPWH na sila naman po yung nag-undertake ng bidding, etc., yung mismo construction.

**[02:47:07.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002308)  
Dahil hindi po biro ang 15 billion pesos.

**[02:47:11.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002309)  
Ito po ay kalahati na ng buong budget ng DOST.

**[02:47:16.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002310)  
So imagine how huge that…

**[02:47:19.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002311)  
Amount is.

**[02:47:22.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002312)  
And next, Madam Chair, gusto ko pong magtanong tungkol sa revised AFP, Modernization Program.

**[02:47:27.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002313)  
Sinabi niyo ko na may mga sampung agreement na napirmahan sa ilalim ng programa na ito.

**[02:47:33.279] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002314)  
Can you share what particular agreements have been signed in relation to this program?

**[02:47:52.399] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002315)  
You can only give the category because these are restricted.

**[02:47:56.319] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002316)  
So these are some major weapon systems, some domain awareness systems for external defense.

**[02:48:04.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002317)  
Can you also share, Madam Chair, sino po yung… o ano yung mga bansa na kausap natin sa mga bilateral agreements na ito?

**[02:48:16.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002318)  
Kontrata po ito. G2G halos lahat. And it's from a host of countries.

**[02:48:22.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002319)  
Alright.

**[02:48:22.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002320)  
Not only one.

**[02:48:24.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002321)  
Which are? Okay.

**[02:48:25.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002322)  
We cannot disclose some of them, Your Honor, because if they tie the system…

**[02:48:32.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002323)  
to the country of origin, makukompromiso na po yung sistema.

**[02:48:37.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002324)  
Alright.

**[02:48:38.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002325)  
So all I can say, they are like-minded countries who share shared interests in the West Philippine Sea with us.

**[02:48:54.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002326)  
Okay, Madam Chair, hingin na lang din po ng representation na ito, yung ulat ng PNP.

**[02:48:58.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002327)  
Those countries that will not pose a supply chain problem because of…

**[02:49:04.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002328)  
Conditions attaining in those countries.

**[02:49:07.359] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002329)  
Yes. So, report on the AFP modernization program from the year 1995 to this year.

**[02:49:14.559] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002330)  
Hingin po natin para makapaghanda rin sa mga… ilan pa nating katanungan tungkol sa programa na ito.

**[02:49:21.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002331)  
Ngayon, Madam Chair, tungkol naman po sa pag-fire ng Tomahawk missile mula Takloban na ang impact area ay sa Nueva Ecija bilang parte ng balikatang exercises.

**[02:49:27.626] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002332)  
Sino po ang nag-a-approve ba?

**[02:49:33.493] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002333)  
Ang Republika po ng Pilipinas.

**[02:49:39.359] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002334)  
Based on list of approved activities na every year po ating pinapasukan with the United States MDB Mutual Defense Board na pinamumunuan po ng DFA.

**[02:49:58.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002335)  
Sa ganito pong activities, meron bang rule yung local government units natin pagdating po sa matter lang ng information?

**[02:50:03.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002336)  
Na magkakaroon ng ganito?

**[02:50:08.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002337)  
Nasa operational security na po namin yan at sinisigurado namin po na walang pinsala sa community.

**[02:50:12.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002338)  
Kaya nga po ang nilapagan nito ay Fort Magsaysay mismo.

**[02:50:17.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002339)  
Alright, Madam Chair. Nag-raise tayo dito ng concern dahil nung nakita nga natin sa balitan na Tomahawk missiles

**[02:50:23.621] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002340)  
na hindi naman lingit din sa ating kaalaman na ginagamit din po sa mga gera ngayon na nangyayari sa West Asia, sa Middle East, sa Iran.

**[02:50:29.642] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002341)  
Ay nababahala po.

**[02:50:35.663] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002342)  
Presentasyon na ito na nangyayari na ito sa ating kapuloan.

**[02:50:41.684] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002343)  
Ngayon naman, tungkol pa rin sa balikatan exercises, tungkol dun sa nasa 6,300 na mga official folk na affected ng fishing ban

**[02:50:47.705] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002344)  
sa kung saan po natin sinasagawa ang balikatan exercises.

**[02:50:53.726] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002345)  
Ang sabi po ay may binibigay per person na tulong, aid, dahil hindi sila makapangisda sa duration ng balikatan exercises.

**[02:50:59.747] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002346)  
Nabigyan na po ba lahat itong mga mga...

**[02:51:05.768] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002347)  
Panginisda na naapektuhan ang kanilang kapuhayan dahil dito?

**[02:51:11.789] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002348)  
Tayo po sa local government units ay natutulungan po lahat.

**[02:51:17.811] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002349)  
At ito ay tulong din ng BFAR at ng iba pong maahensya.

**[02:51:23.832] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002350)  
Madam Chair, saan ang gagaling ang fund para dito?

**[02:51:29.853] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002351)  
Ang tumutulong po dito ay BFAR.

**[02:51:35.874] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002352)  
Oo po. Sila po ang tumutulong.

**[02:51:41.895] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002353)  
Dahil sila pong may charge sa ating fisher phone.

**[02:51:47.916] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002354)  
Okay, so kahit po balik ka ng exercises, would you know kung magkano po yung ginastos dito ng before?

**[02:51:53.937] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002355)  
Hindi ko po matidiscuse yung amounts at based on LGU na cooperation po yun.

**[02:51:59.958] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002356)  
Okay, Madam Chair, na-raise ko din itong concern na ito kasi out of 6,300 na nangangailangan ng manging isda,

**[02:52:05.979] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002357)  
sa balita po at sabi rin ng mga fisher phone organizations, nasa 226 lang po yung nakatanggap.

**[02:52:12.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002358)  
Ayan, kaya we'll definitely raise this matter before the Department of Agriculture and before.

**[02:52:19.159] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002359)  
Kasi po, ang Pilipinas po pala gumagastos din para po sa sinasagawang balikat ng exercises dito sa bansa.

**[02:52:26.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002360)  
Opo, gumagastos po talaga tayo dahil it's for mutual defense.

**[02:52:29.439] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002361)  
Okay, so magkano po ang nagastos natin for the year 2025 to 2026?

**[02:52:33.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002362)  
Opo, no.

**[02:52:33.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002363)  
There's a portion there we cannot disclose for operational security because these amounts can be directly tied to activities.

**[02:52:42.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002364)  
So, I hope the representative understands that these are operationally secure matters and part of the exercise is to remain operationally secure.

**[02:52:56.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002365)  
Yes, pero Madam Chair, hindi ba dapat kung...

**[02:52:59.439] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002366)  
We have some budget for this and we account to the...

**[02:53:05.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002367)  
on an operationally secure basis.

**[02:53:08.872] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002368)  
Okay, so saan po naka-lodge ang budget na ito?

**[02:53:11.437] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002369)  
You said meron po sa BEFAR?

**[02:53:14.913] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002370)  
Sa training.

**[02:53:16.415] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002371)  
Okay.

**[02:53:18.170] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002374)  
Okay, pero bukod po ba sa DA at BEFAR, meron pang ibang mga departments?

**[02:53:22.355] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002375)  
I would not know.

**[02:53:22.895] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002376)  
Na gumagastos.

**[02:53:23.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002377)  
I would not know.

**[02:53:25.645] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002378)  
O, sige po.

**[02:53:26.995] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002380)  
I will raise this before DA.

**[02:53:30.539] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002382)  
The Department of Agriculture.

**[02:53:31.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002383)  
Kasi nasa kanila din po.

**[02:53:34.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002384)  
At sila po ang magsasama-sama nung mag-discuss atong conclusion.

**[02:53:38.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002385)  
I will register for the round two, Madam Chair.

**[02:53:42.284] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002387)  
Yes, thank you, Sarah.

**[02:53:46.030] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002388)  
Next to interpolate from the majority, Representative Leo Rodriguez.

**[02:53:53.120] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002389)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:53:55.600] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002390)  
Good afternoon, Madam Chair.

**[02:53:57.760] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002391)  
Mr. Secretary, DND family.

**[02:54:02.160] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002392)  
Madam Chair, under RA-1207,

**[02:54:07.040] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002393)  
or the Ligtas Pinoy Center Act,

**[02:54:10.399] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002394)  
government is mandated to establish permanent evacuation center

**[02:54:14.080] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002395)  
in every city and municipality.

**[02:54:17.600] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002396)  
For 2027, 3.06 billion is proposed for 34 Ligtas Pinoy Center,

**[02:54:26.640] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002397)  
two per region at 90 million each.

**[02:54:31.760] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002398)  
We note that BICOL has been identified as among the most important

**[02:54:35.840] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002399)  
areas for the implementation of this program as mentioned by our good President in his recent sona.

**[02:54:46.007] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002400)  
Inasaan po namin na ito dahil kami ang sumasalubong sa mga paparating na bagyo,

**[02:54:52.617] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002401)  
lalo na ngayon na mas nagiging matindi ang mga ito.

**[02:54:57.243] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002402)  
So, malaki po ang may tulong nito sa amin.

**[02:55:01.520] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002403)  
Provided this, may we ask the OCD,

**[02:55:06.240] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002404)  
have the specific sites or priority LGOs for 2027 centers already been identified

**[02:55:14.240] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002405)  
and what criteria will be used in selecting them?

**[02:55:22.799] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002406)  
Ito ang problema natin.

**[02:55:26.033] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002407)  
Mabuti na yun, na yung tanong ni Representative Rodriguez ang tanong.

**[02:55:31.250] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002408)  
Kung bawat city, bawat municipality,

**[02:55:36.425] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002409)  
e nalubog yung tatlo,

**[02:55:38.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002410)  
sabay-sabay,

**[02:55:38.990] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002411)  
papano natin mapapatupad?

**[02:55:42.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002412)  
So, pwedeng itayo sa ibang lugar for the benefit of probably the three cities.

**[02:55:49.654] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002413)  
Nangyayari po yan sa Pangasinan.

**[02:55:52.089] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002414)  
Pagka bumaha yung isa,

**[02:55:54.792] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002415)  
pagka galing sa southwest ang baha,

**[02:55:59.456] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002416)  
lubog ang apat na bayan,

**[02:56:02.531] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002417)  
alangan naman dun tayo magtayo.

**[02:56:05.379] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002418)  
Yun po kami nahihirapan.

**[02:56:06.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002419)  
For example, Lindon.

**[02:56:11.359] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002420)  
Pag no construction area, yung 6 kilometer,

**[02:56:20.018] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002421)  
at alam niyong lalago pa yung vulkan,

**[02:56:27.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002422)  
papano niyong patatayuan dun sa bawat municipality and city?

**[02:56:35.261] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002423)  
So, kailangan siguro ng...

**[02:56:41.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002424)  
pag-aaral din sa batas na liwanagin for the benefit of evacuees na i-identify ng DNR

**[02:56:48.524] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002425)  
sa kanilang hazard mapping sa isang lugar,

**[02:56:51.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002426)  
dun itatayo yung evacuation center sa isang lugar.

**[02:56:55.489] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002427)  
Ang problema po, kailangan may consent nung pagtatayuan na munisipyo

**[02:57:00.918] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002428)  
dahil magpupuno din siya ng tao na hindi niya kababayan.

**[02:57:05.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002430)  
At least for a temporary basis.

**[02:57:09.157] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002431)  
So, alam po natin yung prinsipyo,

**[02:57:13.102] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002432)  
eto po ang pinagtutuunan ng pansin ngayon.

**[02:57:16.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002433)  
Maybe we can identify certain areas na walang issue.

**[02:57:21.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002434)  
Pero sa total number of areas,

**[02:57:25.913] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002435)  
eto po'y magiging function din

**[02:57:30.939] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002436)  
ng bumabagsak ba yung mga kalye dun sa dalawang munisipyo.

**[02:57:34.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002437)  
Kaya kailangan may pre-emptive evacuation, lalo na po sa Katanduanes,

**[02:57:38.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002438)  
hindi ba dun sa eastern portion ninyo, babakwit yan papuntang Badabirak area.

**[02:57:46.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002439)  
So, dito po kami humihingi ng tulong sa inyo at saka po sa DNR na kompleto ang hazard mapping

**[02:58:03.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002440)  
na mag-identify kung saan sensible na magtayo ng evacuation centers depending on the hazard.

**[02:58:07.001] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002441)  
Kung Baguio or Vulcan.

**[02:58:08.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002442)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary, Madam Chair.

**[02:58:11.879] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002443)  
So, kasi alam niyo naman po yung Katanduanes.

**[02:58:13.600] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002444)  
So, I hope, Madam Chair,

**[02:58:17.471] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002445)  
medyo Katanduanes sana itong makonsider na malagyan natin ito

**[02:58:23.381] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002446)  
dahil alam niyo naman na talagang kami palagi ang nai-hit ng Baguio,

**[02:58:27.733] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002447)  
which is na talagang na-isolate kami,

**[02:58:30.917] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002448)  
lalo na pag medyo wala nang...

**[02:58:35.154] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002449)  
biyahe.

**[02:58:37.674] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002450)  
So, nahihirap po kami marating kagad.

**[02:58:40.881] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002451)  
So, pag may ganito kami, medyo kahit paano, medyo...

**[02:58:46.172] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002452)  
medyo ano naman tayo, secured.

**[02:58:49.814] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002453)  
Opo, kaya isa yan po sa pinaparating din natin na makonsider po

**[02:58:56.319] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002454)  
ang Katanduanes, Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary,

**[02:59:00.964] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002455)  
dito po sa programa natin na Ligtas Pinoy Center.

**[02:59:04.891] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002456)  
So, napagaganda po nito ng programo.

**[02:59:06.937] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002457)  
ng ating gobyerno, lalo na po doon sa mga talagang madalas mapiktuhan ng kalamidad ng mga probinsya.

**[02:59:16.560] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002458)  
Madam Chair,

**[02:59:19.359] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002459)  
our province, Katanduanes space, the Pacific and firm parts of the Eastern Seaboard,

**[02:59:25.920] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002460)  
while our national security discussing...

**[02:59:30.479] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002461)  
discussion under...

**[02:59:31.677] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002462)  
under standard bill,

**[02:59:32.399] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002463)  
will be focused on the West Philippines,

**[02:59:39.600] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002464)  
since we as DND mayroon po ba tayong sapat na surveillance o monitoring

**[02:59:42.857] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002465)  
sa Eastern Sea, seaside ng bansa at sa mga area malapit sa Katanduanes,

**[02:59:47.343] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002466)  
paano natin sinisecure ang Eastern Seaboards in among specific...

**[02:59:50.240] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002467)  
specific investment sa ilalim ng 2027 budget at AP modernization program

**[03:00:02.319] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002468)  
ang nakatoon sa pagpapalakas ng ating radar communication in the air and naval presence.

**[03:00:07.840] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002469)  
Kasi po, noong mga previous years, may mga nakakapasok po sa amin,

**[03:00:15.200] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002470)  
ang mga foreign vessels na minsan hindi natin namumonitor, Madam Chair.

**[03:00:21.218] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002471)  
Meron po yan patuloy doon sa domain awareness ng mga budget natin,

**[03:00:28.561] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002472)  
pero malaking tulong po dyan yung mga bagong vessel ng Philippine Coast Guard

**[03:00:32.754] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002473)  
na makakakuha po sila ng karagdagan na maya-assign na rin doon sa mga lugar ninyo

**[03:00:38.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002474)  
na maaring mag-increase po ng vigilance doon sa area.

**[03:00:50.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002475)  
Admitido po tayo doon sa eastern seaboard, medyo kulang po talaga ang ating existing infrastructure,

**[03:00:53.871] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002476)  
ngunit nakaprograma po.

**[03:00:54.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002477)  
Ako sa atin na yung domain awareness at least doon sa eastern seaboard matatagdagan po.

**[03:01:04.319] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002478)  
Madam Chair, actually, we raised a similar concern during the agency budget deliberation last year.

**[03:01:12.399] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002479)  
Alam to ni Secretary, we received response from the good Secretary

**[03:01:16.080] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002480)  
that they will study our proposal of establishing a naval base in the province of Catanduan

**[03:01:23.040] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002481)  
to provide support in patrolling the eastern seaboard.

**[03:01:26.880] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002482)  
We hope the ND can also provide us with the updates of this possibility of this request.

**[03:01:37.439] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002483)  
So, sa kalaman lang po, Madam Chair, Mr. Secretary,

**[03:01:40.260] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002484)  
yung pinatayo po natin doon na structure para doon sa Philippine Coast Guard,

**[03:01:42.630] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002485)  
yun po ay natapos na.

**[03:01:43.520] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002486)  
So, the Philippine Coast Guard are planning to medyo magamit na po nila yun

**[03:01:49.359] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002487)  
sa siguro ito, isang bagay ito na makakatulong po doon sa...

**[03:01:55.315] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002488)  
...Lorana Station po doon sa barangadong po sa munisipalidad po ng Bagamanok.

**[03:02:05.040] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002489)  
Madam Secretary, ay Madam Chair.

**[03:02:08.033] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002490)  
Sa ngayon po, ang kasi requirements ng Coast Guard, iba po sa amin.

**[03:02:13.171] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002491)  
Mas malaki po yung kailangan namin at mas malalaki po ang mga barko na pumapasok.

**[03:02:20.189] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002492)  
Ang report sa akin po ng FOIC, maraming claimants doon sa area.

**[03:02:26.924] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002493)  
Ang alawa po, ang pinakamalaking problema, nakita naman po natin kanina,

**[03:02:30.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002494)  
yung capital outlay budget natin binawasan.

**[03:02:33.931] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002495)  
So, paano po tayo magproceed kung gano'n?

**[03:02:41.273] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002496)  
Bagamat gano'n, baka magtulungan na lang po kami sa Coast Guard.

**[03:02:48.414] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002497)  
Pag-usapan na lang po namin kung paano kami makapagtulungan sa kanila.

**[03:02:57.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002498)  
Based on yung capabilities namin na makakapasok sa mainland po ng Bicol.

**[03:03:08.399] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002499)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[03:03:11.600] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002500)  
So, yung ano naman po, Madam Chair, yung sa lupa naman po doon, alam ko naman na okay naman po, Mr. Secretary.

**[03:03:25.520] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002501)  
Philippine Coast Guard.

**[03:03:26.507] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002502)  
Alam naman tayong problema doon.

**[03:03:30.160] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002503)  
Anyway, thank you po, Madam Secretary, Madam Chair and Mr. Secretary, sa napawaganda mong kasagutan.

**[03:03:41.809] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002504)  
Alam ko naman na hindi mo papabaya ng katanduanis dahil isa yun po sa talagang dapat protektan din naman natin.

**[03:03:48.513] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002505)  
Dahil nandoon nga kami sa, ikang malapit kami sa Binham Rice.

**[03:03:53.479] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-002506)  
Salamat po. Salamat po.

**[03:03:55.279] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002507)  
Thank you, Kong Leo.

**[03:03:58.479] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002508)  
Next to interpolate from the minority.

**[03:04:00.800] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002509)  
Representative Elijah San Fernando.

**[03:04:02.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002510)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[03:04:05.040] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002511)  
Mr. Secretary, good morning po.

**[03:04:08.240] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002512)  
It is the duty of the state to protect its citizens at all times.

**[03:04:12.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002513)  
Ito po ang state policy natin, Mr. Secretary. Tama po ba?

**[03:04:15.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002514)  
Oh, yeah.

**[03:04:17.120] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002515)  
So, to protect effectively, ang ibig sabihin po nito, our men in uniform must be well-equipped and well-rationed po, Mr. Secretary.

**[03:04:26.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002517)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[03:04:27.279] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002518)  
And being well-equipped and well-rationed.

**[03:04:30.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002519)  
So, means being able to provide for our own.

**[03:04:33.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002520)  
Tama po, Mr. Secretary.

**[03:04:34.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002521)  
Not necessarily, Your Honor.

**[03:04:36.479] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002522)  
We provide in several ways.

**[03:04:39.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002523)  
Of course, that's part of resilience.

**[03:04:41.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002524)  
But the reality of the situation is worldwide.

**[03:04:46.399] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002525)  
There are defined supply chains for specific items of need.

**[03:04:53.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002526)  
We should provide for our own when we can.

**[03:04:58.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002527)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[03:04:59.520] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002528)  
Now, in the PDP midterm update,

**[03:05:01.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002529)  
So, the Philippine Development Plan is the plan upon which all plans of all departments must be anchored upon.

**[03:05:10.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002530)  
In Chapter 13.1, Outcome No. 4, it says that one of the outcome indicators is to develop a modern, credible, self-reliant defense force.

**[03:05:24.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002531)  
Mr. Secretary, can you discuss what is the operational definition of self-reliance?

**[03:05:31.439] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002532)  
It is to secure your supply chains for the most expeditious and cost-effective means possible.

**[03:05:41.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002533)  
We know it cannot be totally indigenous.

**[03:05:46.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002534)  
Self-reliance does not mean indigenous.

**[03:05:49.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002535)  
It means securing your supply chains on the most favorable of terms and at the least lead time possible.

**[03:05:56.560] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002536)  
So, it doesn't necessarily mean, Mr. Secretary, na tayo lang.

**[03:06:02.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002537)  
So, as much as possible, if we can acquire it.

**[03:06:04.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002538)  
That's called import substitution already, which is different from self-reliance.

**[03:06:09.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002539)  
Because, I mean, as I made an example earlier, there was a program during the 70s, although it was cut short,

**[03:06:22.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002540)  
where, knowing fully well, there was a lack of grazing land in the Philippines.

**[03:06:26.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002541)  
It would be cheaper to buy land in other countries.

**[03:06:30.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002542)  
Which were...

**[03:06:31.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002543)  
Lightly populated, grow the cattle there for the Philippine cattle industry.

**[03:06:36.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002544)  
So, although the activity is somewhere else, the beneficiary being in this country, that is a way of resilience too.

**[03:06:47.359] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002545)  
So, for us, it's to get what we need at the speediest possible time and on an evolutionary basis.

**[03:06:55.279] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002546)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary. I'll get back to that point po.

**[03:06:58.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002547)  
Now, in your NEP po, Mr. Secretary,

**[03:07:03.279] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002548)  
dito po sa government arsenal, nabawasan po ng 21.8% yung budget.

**[03:07:10.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002549)  
Can you enlighten the committee as to why the budget for government arsenal was reduced by 21.8%?

**[03:07:18.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002550)  
Capital outlay, Your Honor, for structures and facilities.

**[03:07:24.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002551)  
And we already have a new roadmap for the government arsenal.

**[03:07:27.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002552)  
It being a sovereign enterprise, just like the Philippine mint.

**[03:07:33.439] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002553)  
Central bank mint.

**[03:07:34.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002554)  
And so, the future activity will be to concentrate not on small arms but on something else.

**[03:07:45.120] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002555)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[03:07:46.240] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002556)  
On the first quarter target po, or output, ang nakasulat po dito sa 2026 target is we need to produce 60 million rounds of ammunition.

**[03:07:58.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002557)  
The actual output is only...

**[03:08:02.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002558)  
Only 500,000.

**[03:08:03.439] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002559)  
Can you enlighten the committee as to bakit po ganito kababa?

**[03:08:08.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002560)  
It's the market sourcing for precursors, Your Honor.

**[03:08:11.279] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002561)  
It is a worldwide shortage right now.

**[03:08:13.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002562)  
It's a supplier's market.

**[03:08:15.040] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002563)  
So, where do we usually get these precursors, Mr. Secretary?

**[03:08:20.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002564)  
Depends, Your Honor, on the availability.

**[03:08:22.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002565)  
We can get from the United States, we can get from South Africa, we can get from Brazil and other countries, which are very few.

**[03:08:31.439] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002566)  
Because these are nitrocellulose.

**[03:08:38.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002567)  
And it's a seller's market.

**[03:08:44.479] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002568)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[03:08:48.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002569)  
Dito po sa Rule 4, Section 1F ng SRDP Revitalization Act,

**[03:08:54.560] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002570)  
any foreign procurement must include a mandatory understudy program to train Filipino workers and secure technology transfer.

**[03:09:02.399] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002572)  
Of the multi-billion peso contracts in the 2027 program of DND,

**[03:09:05.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002573)  
ilan po dito yung mga contract na merong audited, binding, understudy programs and tech transfer clauses?

**[03:09:16.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002574)  
There are no new 2027 contracts except very few, Your Honor.

**[03:09:22.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002575)  
And as I said earlier, that law is unworkable unless you spend for R&D and for...

**[03:09:31.359] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002576)  
Ah...

**[03:09:33.359] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002577)  
Equity in order to have a share of the intellectual property rights.

**[03:09:38.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002578)  
You have to invest on the risk side, on the R&D side to benefit from the upside.

**[03:09:46.319] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002579)  
Or else you'll not get any viable self-reliance program whatsoever.

**[03:09:52.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002580)  
Because the most important thing is you share in the intellectual property rights.

**[03:09:57.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002581)  
That's the crown jewel of everything.

**[03:10:00.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002582)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[03:10:01.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002583)  
Madam Chair, kaya ko po

**[03:10:03.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002584)  
Tinanong itong mga tanong ko.

**[03:10:04.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002585)  
Because it is in my position that defense also has something to do with industrial policy.

**[03:10:11.120] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002586)  
For us to be able to defend ourselves and for us to have an effective defense plan,

**[03:10:18.319] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002587)  
we must have an industrial policy.

**[03:10:21.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002588)  
Definitely, Your Honor.

**[03:10:22.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002589)  
And it means that being able to produce for our own, being able to equip our own.

**[03:10:27.040] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002590)  
Now, Mr. Secretary, what is your comment about the statement of...

**[03:10:32.960] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002591)  
...Secretary Arsenio Balisacan?

**[03:10:35.439] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002592)  
Wherein he said that we do not need to do or we don't have to do industrial policy like what our neighbors did.

**[03:10:43.040] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002593)  
And this is in line with the PDP, the Philippine Development Plan,

**[03:10:47.120] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002594)  
wherein one of the outcome indicators says that we must develop our self-reliant defense force.

**[03:10:56.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002595)  
Your Honor, probably what he means is that you don't need to reinvent the wheel

**[03:11:01.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002596)  
if you can get the technology.

**[03:11:04.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002597)  
But there are some immutables, that there are some basics that you need to remediate.

**[03:11:10.479] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002598)  
Ease of doing business, transaction costs, administrative costs, connectivity and power.

**[03:11:19.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002599)  
Unless you lower these costs, then any industrial activity is a value proposition

**[03:11:30.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002600)  
that must have a positive outcome indicator.

**[03:11:32.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002601)  
If not, it will be a sunk cost that will be marginal for any country.

**[03:11:39.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002602)  
Unless it's a public good.

**[03:11:42.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002603)  
That, for example, a railroad, which doesn't make money.

**[03:11:47.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002604)  
So, maybe the statement of Secretary Balisacan is that it's useless to reinvent things

**[03:11:55.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002605)  
that we can have with partnerships or with sustainable like-minded...

**[03:12:02.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002606)  
...co-venturing and place what you can value-add based on your own characteristics to the initiative.

**[03:12:12.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002607)  
But nonetheless, there is no input without an output.

**[03:12:18.399] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002608)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[03:12:19.920] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002609)  
As you can see, I'm a supporter of industrial policy.

**[03:12:24.240] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002610)  
I definitely support it.

**[03:12:25.520] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002611)  
All of the problems that we are facing now, like high electricity costs, etc.,

**[03:12:30.536] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002612)  
hinarap din po kasi ito ng mga problema ng mga kapitbahay natin.

**[03:12:34.560] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002613)  
We have South Korea, we have Taiwan, we have Vietnam, even China, say, for example.

**[03:12:40.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002614)  
But their economic managers have that ambition to really develop their industries.

**[03:12:49.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002615)  
My point here, Madam Chair and Mr. Secretary, again, for us to have an effective defense plan

**[03:12:58.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002616)  
and for us to really do what is written in the Constitution

**[03:13:02.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002617)  
to protect our people at all times,

**[03:13:05.439] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002618)  
industrial policy is a must so that in times of war, say, for example,

**[03:13:14.399] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002619)  
or nagkaroon naman mga challenging times,

**[03:13:16.114] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002620)  
kung hindi man makapag-provide yung mga pinagkukunan natin ng mga precursors, materials, etc.,

**[03:13:22.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002621)  
at least we can say that we can provide for our own and we can equip our men in uniform.

**[03:13:30.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002622)  
Totally agree, Your Honor. There's no debate about it. I totally agree.

**[03:13:33.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002624)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary, for agreeing with me. Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[03:13:38.720] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002625)  
Thank you, Kong Eli San Fernando.

**[03:13:41.680] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-002626)  
Next to interpolate, Congressman Ahong Chan.

**[03:13:45.279] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002627)  
Thank you, Madam Secretary.

**[03:13:48.475] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002628)  
Madam Chair, thank you.

**[03:13:50.332] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002629)  
Good afternoon to our good Secretary, DND family, and to my colleagues.

**[03:13:56.187] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002630)  
Good afternoon.

**[03:13:58.263] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002631)  
Lapu-Lapu City hosts two major AFP facilities.

**[03:14:06.399] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002632)  
The Philippine Air Force Base and the Philippine Navy Base.

**[03:14:13.600] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002633)  
We are proud and fortunate to have these military facilities in our city.

**[03:14:22.880] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002634)  
As representative of the Lone District of Lapu-Lapu City,

**[03:14:27.279] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002635)  
I have consistently supported their infrastructure projects and development programs.

**[03:14:38.479] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002636)  
However, there is an ongoing illegal reclamation activity inside the Philippine Navy area.

**[03:14:50.080] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002637)  
It has no clearance from the Lapu-Lapu City government, no permit from the Philippine

**[03:15:00.640] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002638)  
Reclamation Authority, and no permit or environmental clearance from the DENR.

**[03:15:10.239] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002639)  
Therefore, this is clearly an illegal reclamation. Before this reclamation,

**[03:15:17.760] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002640)  
the area was part of the sea and served as a natural waterway.

**[03:15:26.160] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002641)  
But because this portion of the sea has been filled up, the natural flow of water has been blocked,

**[03:15:36.080] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002642)  
causing flooding in the neighboring barangays. We must also consider that Lapu-Lapu City

**[03:15:45.359] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002644)  
is a small island with very limited land. Any major development or reclamation, especially

**[03:15:56.080] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002645)  
one undertaken illegally and without coordination, can seriously affect the entire city master plan,

**[03:16:09.040] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002646)  
including the land use, drainage, infrastructure, environmental protection,

**[03:16:16.160] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002647)  
and future development.

**[03:16:20.239] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002648)  
Mr. Secretary, this illegal reclamation is already affecting the overall development of Lapu-Lapu City

**[03:16:29.840] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002649)  
and the implementation of our city master plan. That is why I respectfully request

**[03:16:40.000] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002650)  
that the DND, the office of the representative of the Lone District of Lapu-Lapu City,

**[03:16:46.479] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002651)  
and the Lapu-Lapu City Government sit down immediately to discuss and resolve this matter.

**[03:16:57.200] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002652)  
We fully support the AFP, but all developments must be properly coordinated

**[03:17:05.600] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002653)  
and harmonized with the City Master Plan for the protection of our people

**[03:17:13.600] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002654)  
and the sustainable development of our small island city.

**[03:17:21.760] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002655)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Secretary and Madam Chair.

**[03:17:24.239] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002656)  
Your Honor, we'll make an internal study and see what's appropriate

**[03:17:29.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002657)  
insofar as we are concerned and so far as presidential guidance is concerned regarding military lands.

**[03:17:35.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002658)  
That's all I can say for now, Your Honor.

**[03:17:38.160] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002659)  
But, Mr. Secretary, we know that this is under presidential

**[03:17:44.399] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002660)  
proclamation, but what I'm telling is the illegal reclamation, Mr. Secretary.

**[03:17:50.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002661)  
Your Honor, if in case of legality or illegality, I'll refer you to the other departments

**[03:17:55.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002662)  
with consultation with us.

**[03:18:01.920] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002663)  
So that we can have this, I mean, smooth for the development

**[03:18:11.279] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002664)  
that the Master Plan of the City will not affect, especially for the outfall

**[03:18:17.279] Rep. Junard "Ahong" Q. Chan** (asr-002665)  
of our drainage, Mr. Secretary.

**[03:18:19.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002671)

**[03:18:26.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002672)

**[03:18:41.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002674)

We need more domain awareness, we need more internal hardening systems, and we

**[03:46:00.015] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003269)  
need more deterrent systems. I hope that local industry will come to such a

**[03:46:09.656] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003270)  
stage, Your Honor, but at this stage, we cannot afford to be on the...

**[03:46:15.087] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003271)  
backside of the learning curve, given the fact that the evolution of

**[03:46:23.305] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003272)  
technology is really, really quick and really fast. And to handhold industries,

**[03:46:34.911] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003273)  
which, on an industry basis, Your Honor, if we can scale the production, which industry must,

**[03:46:44.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003274)  
have the capacity for, in terms of value propositions, then it's fine.

**[03:46:50.545] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003275)  
But it must be forward-looking, and they must not cater to what would normally be a police item,

**[03:47:02.270] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003276)  
or something that we need to move, not beyond, but in addition to.

**[03:47:11.871] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003277)  
Okay. So, Madam Chair, tama.

**[03:47:15.218] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003278)  
na yung mga acquisition of arms na beyond yung small arms ay nandiyan naman sa budget,

**[03:47:21.552] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003279)  
including po ba drones, et cetera?

**[03:47:25.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003280)  
Drones per se, Your Honor, are, you know, of course, part of the toolkit of any country right now,

**[03:47:32.239] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003281)  
but the production of drones is quite easy. It's the technology you put in depending on what you want to use them for.

**[03:47:39.439] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003282)  
So, meron po bang local production of drones, or kasama rin siya sa importation, Madam Chair?

**[03:47:43.027] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003284)  
I'm sure there are, Your Honor. This is easily done. But it's what you carry them, what you put into them, actually, which is important, and which changes.

**[03:47:52.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003285)  
Because of the advent of AI, it changes every three days, sometimes.

**[03:48:01.359] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003286)  
May operational guidelines po ba sa usage of drones ng military, Madam Chair, right now?

**[03:48:06.206] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003287)  
Your Honor, it's on the evolution stage.

**[03:48:08.813] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003288)  
So, right now, wala pong written guidelines? Because meron po ang commercial, so sa military right now, wala pa?

**[03:48:15.004] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003289)  
Wala pa po. We are still evolving the technology.

**[03:48:18.129] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003290)  
And actually, in order to use drones, we have to develop our backbone systems in order to use this responsibly.

**[03:48:26.132] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003291)  
Ako, I'd like to flag that, Madam Chair. As you said po, as the Secretary said, kailangan po ng responsible use.

**[03:48:32.721] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003292)  
And I think it starts at guidelines. Kung paano po siyempre gamitin, lalo na it's possible na pwedeng ma-abuse.

**[03:48:41.599] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003293)  
So, kailan po ba maasahan, if ever, na magka-guidelines?

**[03:48:44.863] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003294)  
Right now po, there are no offensive uses for drones, insofar as we're concerned.

**[03:48:51.359] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003295)  
Uh, they are for surveillance, but naturally, we will conform with guidelines for the external defense use of drones,

**[03:49:02.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003296)  
if it is for purposes other than surveillance.

**[03:49:05.744] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003297)  
Kung meron pong mga updates tungkol po sa pagkaroon ng guidelines on its operations,

**[03:49:14.244] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003298)  
magkaroon po sana ng copy na maipasa.

**[03:49:16.314] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003299)  
So, through this committee, para po ma-insure yung responsible use sa kanya.

**[03:49:20.216] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003300)  
Much as they are disclosable, Your Honor, I don't think it's anything wrong,

**[03:49:27.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003301)  
just as long as the nuts and bolts and the granularity that may compromise our systems are not compromised.

**[03:49:37.359] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003302)  
For now, no more questions given the limitations and time.

**[03:49:41.199] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-003303)  
Madam Chair, I would like to sign up for the second round. Thank you.

**[03:49:45.840] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-003304)  
Yes, thank you, Cong. Renee Co.

**[03:49:48.965] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-003305)  
Next to interpolate from the majority, Vice Chair Rufus Rodriguez.

**[03:49:53.920] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003306)  
Thank you, Madam Chair, Chair Bella Suansing.

**[03:49:58.640] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003307)  
A pleasant good afternoon to the Department of National Defense Officialdom

**[03:50:04.000] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003308)  
and also the Armed Forces of the Philippines.

**[03:50:08.720] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003309)  
First, let me congratulate and thank Secretary Teodoro

**[03:50:15.120] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003310)  
for being a patriot.

**[03:50:18.160] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003311)  
For fighting for the West Philippine Sea

**[03:50:21.359] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003312)  
and standing up against China, the bully of our region.

**[03:50:27.600] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003313)  
So, you are, you have been able, since the time you were Secretary,

**[03:50:32.800] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003314)  
you were able to protect Philippine interests

**[03:50:36.399] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003315)  
as there should be no other interest paramount to our sovereignty.

**[03:50:41.920] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003316)  
It is Article 1 of the Philippine Constitution.

**[03:50:46.319] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003317)  
It also goes without saying that he is our pride of the U.P. College of Law.

**[03:50:56.640] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003318)  
Having number one in the bar examinations, I was only number 17.

**[03:51:01.840] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003319)  
So, I have to bring this list by the Taliba who were in the top 20

**[03:51:07.920] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003320)  
because nobody believes if you are number 11 to 20.

**[03:51:12.080] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003321)  
But no doubt that Secretary Gilbert,

**[03:51:16.720] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003322)  
Secretary Teodoro, number one in the bar.

**[03:51:18.960] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003323)  
So, I have a question.

**[03:51:20.640] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003324)  
Number one is, what is the budget of the DND for the Lumbia 15th Strike Wing?

**[03:51:32.408] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003325)  
If that is right, yeah.

**[03:51:33.920] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003326)  
What is now the budget for 2027 to further develop our Lumbia EDCA site?

**[03:51:46.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003327)  
Your Honor, I beg a few seconds to retrieve the data.

**[03:51:52.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003328)  
In the facilities, for this year, Your Honor, it's 115 million.

**[03:52:10.647] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003329)  
But again, for this year, 2026?

**[03:52:14.157] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003330)  
Yeah, yeah, 115.

**[03:52:16.324] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003331)  
Million?

**[03:52:17.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003332)  
Yes, Your Honor, that's the remaining.

**[03:52:18.960] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003333)  
And for next year?

**[03:52:20.654] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003334)  
That's the remaining, Your Honor.

**[03:52:23.067] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003335)  
Completed?

**[03:52:23.978] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003336)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[03:52:24.960] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003337)  
Yes, thank you. 115 million.

**[03:52:26.160] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003338)  
So, there's already a budget.

**[03:52:28.000] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003339)  
Save enhancements that may be done.

**[03:52:31.150] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003340)  
Thank you.

**[03:52:31.840] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003341)  
And what is now also the counterpart of the United States Government? Being an EDCA site?

**[03:52:39.199] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003343)  
Not yet, Your Honor.

**[03:52:41.359] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003344)  
I thought as EDCA site,

**[03:52:44.560] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003345)  
there will already be infusion of American dollars to further develop that Lumbia airport.

**[03:52:53.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003346)  
Your Honor, the development of, even for us, for the 15th Strike Wing,

**[03:53:00.125] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003347)  
and to have a maintenance base.

**[03:53:01.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003348)  
Maintenance base. They're hampered by right-of-way issues.

**[03:53:06.800] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003350)  
What is the problem?

**[03:53:09.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003351)  
Informal settlers,

**[03:53:11.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003352)  
Your Honor, that has hampered us.

**[03:53:14.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003354)  
And it's only now that we have

**[03:53:18.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003356)  
taken the appropriate

**[03:53:21.359] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003357)  
legal proceedings and

**[03:53:23.199] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003358)  
working towards looking for

**[03:53:24.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003359)  
adequate relocation for us

**[03:53:28.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003361)  
to fully utilize,

**[03:53:30.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003362)  
not only for the 15th Strike Wing,

**[03:53:35.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003364)  
but it shall serve as the

**[03:53:38.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003366)  
Northern Mindanao Operations Center for the OCD.

**[03:53:48.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003370)  
There are still illegal settlers

**[03:53:51.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003371)  
and the access road to the highway,

**[03:53:53.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003372)  
Your Honor, to the highway.

**[03:53:55.711] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003373)  
Thank you, Secretary

**[03:53:57.359] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003374)  
Gibo. Now, in the

**[03:53:58.800] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003375)  
master plan, what is the contribution of

**[03:54:02.399] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003377)  
the United States Government

**[03:54:03.760] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003378)  
to the Lumbia Airport?

**[03:54:07.591] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003380)  
They are still scoping, Your Honor,

**[03:54:10.510] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003381)  
what they can do with the area, particularly knowing that

**[03:54:14.342] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003383)  
again, Cagayan de Oro is

**[03:54:15.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003384)  
vulnerable to mudslides.

**[03:54:17.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003385)  
And since it will be also a

**[03:54:22.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003387)  
coordination center

**[03:54:23.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003388)  
for Northern Mindanao for HADR,

**[03:54:26.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003389)  
they're scoping in those

**[03:54:27.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003390)  
lines, Your Honor.

**[03:54:34.730] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003391)  
They're scoping,

**[03:54:35.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003392)  
The scoping is for a multipurpose warehouse, and for us,

**[03:54:39.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003393)  
Your Honor, it's air traffic control enhancement.

**[03:54:43.439] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003394)  
So, in the plan, what is the structures

**[03:54:46.080] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003395)  
to be built by the U.S.

**[03:54:48.800] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003396)  
Government?

**[03:54:50.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003397)  
So far, there's nothing agreed,

**[03:54:51.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003398)  
Your Honor. They're scoping for a warehouse.

**[03:54:56.731] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003399)  
Warehouse.

**[03:54:56.880] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003400)  
There will be social costs when the Americans would go there.

**[03:55:00.399] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003401)  
What are the plans of our Armed Forces of the Philippines and the DND?

**[03:55:06.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003402)  
On social costs,

**[03:55:08.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003403)  
Your Honor, that's why the right-of-way issues

**[03:55:09.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003404)  
will be the first.

**[03:55:12.319] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003405)  
And the

**[03:55:14.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003406)  
relocation issues

**[03:55:15.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003407)  
will be the most important thing, which we are

**[03:55:18.399] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003408)  
addressing, because we need to address

**[03:55:19.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003409)  
it in order to provide base security.

**[03:55:24.592] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003412)  
Referring to, you know, when there are Americans,

**[03:55:27.840] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003413)  
you know, there will be prostitution.

**[03:55:34.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003414)  
Your Honor, there will be...

**[03:55:35.120] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003415)  
Any plans for that to protect?

**[03:55:36.995] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003417)  
our women in Cagayan de Oro.

**[03:55:39.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003418)  
No, they will be guided by SOFA, Your Honor, if ever they are there, present, you know, and...

**[03:55:45.199] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003419)  
The SOFA will provide for how do we protect our women of Cagayan de Oro.

**[03:55:48.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003420)  
Yes, Your Honor, in the same way as they should be protected

**[03:55:52.399] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003421)  
from our own exploitation by our own men, too.

**[03:55:58.207] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003422)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[03:55:59.680] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003423)  
Next question is, how is now the proposed naval base in our neighboring town of Tagoloan?

**[03:56:08.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003425)  
That will be the dockyard, Your Honor.

**[03:56:11.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003426)  
It's still awaiting finalization of the titling.

**[03:56:15.976] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003428)  
The titling?

**[03:56:16.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003429)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[03:56:17.439] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003430)  
Is that in the PHIVIDEC area, Honorable Secretary?

**[03:56:20.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003431)  
It is the southern...

**[03:56:24.319] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003432)  
It is in the southern portion of the PHIVIDEC area, Your Honor.

**[03:56:28.399] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003433)  
How much will that entail in cost

**[03:56:31.040] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003434)  
once the titling is already done?

**[03:56:36.867] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003435)  
For a complete dry dock.

**[03:56:40.239] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003436)  
Your Honor, that will be able to support our fleet, growing fleet.

**[03:56:45.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003437)  
That's about 40 billion pesos.

**[03:56:49.473] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003438)  
How much, Secretary?

**[03:56:50.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003439)  
Forty. Forty billion, Your Honor.

**[03:56:53.120] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003440)  
Ah, the cost of the dry dock is 40 billion?

**[03:56:55.227] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003441)  
Indeed.

**[03:56:56.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003442)  
The whole thing, Your Honor.

**[03:56:57.100] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003443)  
Whole thing?

**[03:56:57.607] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003444)  
Together with equipment.

**[03:56:59.680] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003445)  
Indeed, that will bring forth substantial economic activity for Cagayan de Oro.

**[03:57:04.330] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003446)  
Thank you very much for that, Secretary.

**[03:57:08.913] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003448)  
From there.

**[03:57:09.976] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003449)  
Yes.

**[03:57:10.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003450)  
It's strategic because it can be a dry dock

**[03:57:15.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003451)  
because it's relatively free from typhoons.

**[03:57:19.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003452)  
And it can accommodate even the largest vessel, which is an LPD.

**[03:57:26.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003453)  
But because the Subic Naval Base right now is...

**[03:57:31.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003454)  
We cannot use it as a dry dock.

**[03:57:36.399] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003455)  
It's an operating naval base.

**[03:57:38.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003456)  
This one will be adopted.

**[03:57:39.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003457)  
It is a real dry dock where we can adequately maintain and supervise all our vessels.

**[03:57:45.040] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003458)  
So, thank you for thinking of relocating it in Misamis Oriental, in Tagoloan, which is beside the city of Cagayan de Oro, the city of golden friendship.

**[03:57:56.640] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003459)  
So, we thank you for that, Secretary.

**[03:57:58.080] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003460)  
And when will the title be?

**[03:58:00.319] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003461)  
What is the timetable?

**[03:58:01.760] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003462)  
Of course, you have lawyers and the title would be finished about when.

**[03:58:05.600] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003463)  
And when will the start of the construction be?

**[03:58:08.399] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003464)  
We're awaiting that.

**[03:58:09.612] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003465)  
For Cagayan de Oro and Misamis Oriental.

**[03:58:12.262] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003466)  
Your Honor, I await too.

**[03:58:13.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003467)  
And we're trying our level best to get the titling done.

**[03:58:16.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003468)  
Because we need to leave, vacate Sangley as soon as possible

**[03:58:22.399] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003469)  
because it is extremely shallow, extremely small.

**[03:58:26.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003470)  
And it's getting to be unmaneuverable for our naval platforms.

**[03:58:32.208] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003471)  
Thank you.

**[03:58:32.800] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003472)  
That is good news to Mindanao, specifically Northern Mindanao and entire Mindanao, and Cagayan de Oro and Misamis Oriental.

**[03:58:40.479] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003473)  
My other question, Mr. Secretary, is before there were talks about the transfer of the 4th military area which we oppose,

**[03:58:50.080] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003474)  
are there still plans to transfer the 4th Infantry Division in Cagayan de Oro, Patag, to another place?

**[03:58:58.479] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003475)  
That is not a specific guidance, Your Honor.

**[03:59:00.399] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003476)  
Our specific guidance is for the Army to relocate.

**[03:59:05.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003477)  
To scope their areas where they need to have the homes

**[03:59:09.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003478)  
Of their divisions and brigades.

**[03:59:14.399] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003479)  
With respect to having a division headquarters or a brigade headquarters as a connectivity node,

**[03:59:24.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003480)  
as a logistical supply point, as a motor pool,

**[03:59:32.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003481)  
and for divisions to contain medical facilities adequately to support.

**[03:59:38.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003482)  
And this must be able to...

**[03:59:39.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003483)  
Provide connectivity to strategic bases of other services.

**[03:59:45.279] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003484)  
If not, then the strategic bases...

**[03:59:47.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003485)  
bases cannot be resupplied adequately, so it is a whole exercise in logistics, Your Honor, and I know

**[03:59:57.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003486)  
there are certain legacy areas which first have developed massive roots already and we will keep

**[04:00:06.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003487)  
this into consideration nonetheless the exercise really is in logistics your honor because we have

**[04:00:14.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003488)  
a very very big difficulty particularly in humanitarian assistance and disaster response

**[04:00:20.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003489)  
and resupplying our own troops it's quite difficult so we need to have the logistics

**[04:00:30.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003490)  
capabilities in order for the armed forces to pursue its uh external defense role thank you

**[04:00:40.800] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003491)  
Secretary, you know, Patag

**[04:00:44.880] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003492)  
for the division is really very, very strategically located. Of course, Cagayan de Oro is the gateway to

**[04:00:54.880] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003493)  
Mindanao. If we talk about Davao, you go around the Zamboanga Peninsula, and so in

**[04:01:00.239] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003494)  
terms of logistics that's the best side and I believe that there is still uh area that will

**[04:01:06.399] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003495)  
be used for that. So we ask you on behalf of the city of Cagayan de Oro to consider that as the

**[04:01:13.608] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003496)  
continuing

**[04:01:14.960] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003497)  
area for the Armed Forces because it is very strategically located. From Cagayan de Oro, you

**[04:01:21.680] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003498)  
go to Bukidnon, and then you go to Butuan. So please consider that, Mr. Secretary, because we would wish

**[04:01:29.920] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003499)  
that to remain in the city of Cagayan de Oro. On the three domains—

**[04:01:38.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003500)  
Your Honor, and the fourth domain, the cyber domain and the IT domain, that is indeed a valuable

**[04:01:46.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003501)  
position, and as a matter of fact we are adding on the naval domain with the dockyard that is

**[04:01:51.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003502)  
proposed, and Lumbia as the home of the 15th Strike Wing where they will train and they will

**[04:01:59.199] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003503)  
maintain, and it will also be the home of the Northern Mindanao Operations Center

**[04:02:10.319] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003504)  
of the OCD.

**[04:02:18.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003505)  
So, Your Honor, rest assured that that is an area of prime consideration for us.

**[04:02:26.960] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003506)  
Thank you, Secretary Gibo. It now looks that, uh, the Secretary is looking at Cagayan de Oro and Misamis Oriental. We have the 15th Strike Wing

**[04:02:38.239] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003508)  
in Lumbia, and then we have the dockyard, 40 billion, in

**[04:02:43.680] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003510)  
in the municipality, and then our Army is there in Patag to be enlarged to become a logistical

**[04:02:56.560] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003511)  
center because Cagayan de Oro is the center of Northern Mindanao and gateway to entire Mindanao.

**[04:03:03.600] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003512)  
So, with that we thank the Secretary for the Lumbia airport, we thank the Secretary for the dockyard,

**[04:03:11.359] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003513)  
and we thank the Secretary for enlarging the 4th Infantry Division. Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[04:03:18.960] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003514)  
Again, we thank you for your service to this nation, which is unequaled in recent history.

**[04:03:24.479] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-003515)  
Thank you very much.

**[04:03:26.255] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003515-b)  
Thank you, Your Honor.

**[04:03:29.022] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-003516)  
Thank you, Vice Chair Rufus Rodriguez. Given that there are no other minority members who are listed for the first round, we will proceed

**[04:03:38.319] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-003517)

**[04:05:12.483] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-003531)

**[04:05:18.338] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003532)

**[04:05:37.947] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003534)  
Uh, [unclear].

**[04:05:56.071] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003535)  
Uh, now, given the parochial concern, anyway...

**[04:06:04.964] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003537)

Cong., thank you. Thank you. Meeting suspended.

**[04:19:39.638] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-003543)  
Okay, next to interpolate, Vice Chair Anna York Bondoc. Vice Chair Anna York Bondoc.
