**[04:20:32.700] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003544)  
Okay na. Magandang hapon po, Mr. Secretary and the DND family. Thank you so much for the opportunity for us to be able to work closely with you and support your 2027 budget.

**[04:20:52.060] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003545)  
First of all, I would like to greet our new AFP Chief of Staff.

**[04:20:56.580] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003546)  
Nag-meet po kami for the first time and I had the pleasure of meeting him for the first time last year in Bongabon, Nueva Ecija

**[04:21:04.460] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003547)  
with the 703rd Agila Brigade.

**[04:21:08.020] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003548)  
At nagkita po kami because I wanted to express my support and thanks po to the 7th Infantry Division last year

**[04:21:17.720] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003549)  
and the brigade and the battalion [unclear] sa baha.

**[04:21:24.740] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003550)  
So, I thought last year was the worst thing that ever happened to me.

**[04:21:29.420] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003551)  
Ayun pala, this year it was going to be worse. So, I have a few pictures in case you're interested po to see.

**[04:21:36.780] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003552)  
Up to today po, I come from the 4th District of Pampanga, which is the catch basin po, lahat po ng tubig from Aurora.

**[04:21:47.400] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003553)  
Ayan po si Kong Angara. Yung tubig niyo bumabagsak sa amin. Yung buong Nueva Ecija po. buong province of Bulacan, and even the Sierra Madre Mountains. So the amount of water pouring in is truly tremendous.

**[04:22:04.780] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003554)  
So ayan po ang tumutulong po sa amin. Pakita ninyo iyong baha mismo, iyong plaza po namin iyan. So yan, and that is, later on I will speak to you about MacArthur Highway. So it was actually a good thing.

**[04:22:21.880] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003564)  
I had never worked with the Philippine Army previous to last year and I was very grateful sa kanila pong efficiency

**[04:22:32.900] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003565)  
and ang bilis po nilang kumilos and most of all their compassion towards us.

**[04:22:38.680] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003566)  
So from someone who previously didn't know much about the Army, it was really a revelation to me.

**[04:22:45.100] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003567)  
So, ang maganda po from what happened last year,

**[04:22:49.820] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003568)  
I am of the philosophy that kung kumikilos ka, pag may baha, huli ka na.

**[04:22:56.040] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003569)  
In other words, it is better to prepare for a disaster rather than, even though ang dami hung tumulong, mali pa po yun and that we need to prepare.

**[04:23:05.580] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003570)  
So, I would like to again commend,

**[04:23:07.600] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003571)  
And nagkaroon po kami ng malawakang Bantay Sapa, or the largest coastal cleanup of Manila Bay. Hindi po makalabas

**[04:23:18.000] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003572)  
yung tubig sa amin because of siltation po sa mga sapa. So this last

**[04:23:23.520] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003573)  
June, ako po ang nagpondo, lahat po ng tupad ko, pinondohan namin more than 1,500 tupad members.

**[04:23:32.740] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003574)  
Last [unclear] slide, please. More than 1,500 tupad members po joined us dun sa Bantay Sapa.

**[04:23:39.380] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003575)  
This is already the third year.

**[04:23:41.640] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003576)  
So we have been doing this for the last three years.

**[04:23:44.240] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003577)  
Hindi po kami kumikilos dahil may baha.

**[04:23:46.700] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003578)  
Kumikilos po kami bago po magkabaha.

**[04:23:50.100] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003586)  
So you can see this is the Philippine Army, the Philippine Coast Guard, and the Philippine Navy with the DENR and the local government.

**[04:23:58.640] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003587)  
So nagkaroon po kami ng Memorandum of Understanding. And I would like to thank Philippine Army,

**[04:24:05.280] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003588)  
Philippine Navy, ayan po, Coast Guard, at lahat po ng tumulong sa amin.

**[04:24:09.460] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003589)  
So year one, we were able to clear 9,000 basura.

**[04:24:13.660] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003590)  
Year two, 17,000. And year three, 15,000.

**[04:24:17.400] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003591)  
So despite all of that, binaha pa rin po kami this year.

**[04:24:21.780] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003592)  
So I would personally like to thank, again, I would like to personally mention

**[04:24:26.200] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003593)  
[unclear], General Osias, and our battalion commander,

**[04:24:32.680] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003594)  
si Lieutenant Colonel [unclear], for their kindness po this year.

**[04:24:36.320] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003595)  
Hindi lang po sila kind, they are very efficient at mabilis pong kumilos.

**[04:24:40.680] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003596)  
So, Secretary, pinupugayan po namin sila sa kanila pong kasipagan at kabaitan sa amin. So, wala po akong masyadong tanong. I would just like to thank them.

**[04:24:53.180] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003598)  
I would also like to mention an incident po nang nangyari. One of the ways to prevent flood po is through dredging.

**[04:25:02.460] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003599)  
and palabas po yung tubig namin sa Manila Bay. And personally po, nagka-problema na rin po pala ang Philippine Navy sa Manila Bay

**[04:25:11.560] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003600)  
because of the siltation. So tinulungan po namin sila and we were very happy to work with them

**[04:25:18.140] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003601)  
to dredge po, grabe na po ang siltation, pati po ang Philippine Navy tinamaan na rin po.

**[04:25:23.620] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003602)  
And we had to dredge together yung pagdating po ng kanilang destroyer.

**[04:25:30.740] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003603)  
So upon conversation po with former Commodore, now present Admiral, tama po ba ako Admiral Nera

**[04:25:43.180] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003604)  
nagkausap po kami and nalaman po namin and I told him that if Manila [unclear] silted, and if our destroyers, and we are modernizing the Navy, at mas malalim pa

**[04:25:55.700] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003605)  
siguro yung kailangan po nating volume of tubig, tama po ba ako?

**[04:25:59.200] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003606)  
So, therefore, dredgers are a very important component po, and very much badly needed by our Navy.

**[04:26:06.420] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003607)  
So, Sec, nagka-intindihan po kami that the Philippine Navy needs to purchase dredgers. Kasi po, I told them,

**[04:26:15.540] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003608)  
if we are going to purchase large ships, kailangan po talaga ng dredgers. So, I hope you could support the much-needed dredgers po ng Philippine Navy.

**[04:26:30.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003609)  
Of course, Madam Chair, because we really need it also for our own naval bases and ports. There's one in the pipeline

**[04:26:39.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003610)  
right now, coming in.

**[04:26:41.440] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003611)  
Opo. Actually, medyo nagkaagawan nga po ng dredger. Kasi we need naman to dredge sa Manila Bay. Kasi babahain kami. But we gladly lent our dredger po to the Philippine Navy.

**[04:26:57.220] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003612)  
So tinanong po sa akin [unclear] kataas yung dredger na kailangan. And then they said 1991 pa daw yung pinag-agawan namin. So

**[04:27:07.180] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003613)  
it would be really a good idea. May isang suggestion po ako

**[04:27:09.320] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003614)  
na madali. Lahat po ng nase-seize na illegal dredgers lagyan ninyo ng exception sa Tariff and Customs code na imbis na isubasta iyan, dalhin na lang sa Coast Guard at saka sa Navy lahat ng [unclear] ng mga dredgers para magamit ho natin.

**[04:27:28.840] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003615)  
Kasi kailangan isubasta eh under the Tariff and Customs Code.

**[04:27:33.160] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003616)  
So minsan, kapag nakaka-seize ng vessels na malalaki, sinusubasta ito.

**[04:27:39.880] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003617)  
And then, nariregurgitate o narirecycle.

**[04:27:43.760] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003618)  
So, kung puwede, [unclear] na lang ho iyan, lalo na pag kailangan ng Navy o ng Coast Guard or ng DENR, for example. Depending on the size, na illegal na mga vessels na imbis na mandatory na isubasta, i-turnover po kung magagamit. Mas [unclear], malaking tulong din po sa atin iyan. Thank you po. And sana po masuportahan niyo po yung purchase of dredgers kasi kailangan po ng Navy.

**[04:28:17.600] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-003621)  
Okay, thank you so much po.

**[04:28:20.920] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-003622)  
Thank you, Vice Chair Anna York Bondoc.

**[04:28:23.740] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-003623)

**[04:28:56.400] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-003629)

**[04:29:16.055] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-003636)

**[04:29:24.620] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-003637)

**[04:29:40.140] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-003638)  
Ang pinakamataas na peak doon sa amin sa Nueva Ecija po, ang pangalan po ay Paatas Peak sa Mount Kimalugong. Para po maintindihan po ng lahat.

**[04:29:55.820] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-003643)  
Sa Mount Kemalugong, ito po ay parte ng Sierra Madre.

**[04:30:01.100] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-003644)

**[04:30:06.340] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-003645)  
So ang dahilan po ng pagbaha ay zero meters above sea level dito sa Metro Manila.

**[04:30:14.400] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-003646)  
Alin po ba ang zero meter above sea level?

**[04:30:17.140] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-003647)  
Ang zero meter po ay Manila Bay.

**[04:30:21.140] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-003648)

**[04:31:05.180] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-003655)

**[04:31:14.680] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-003657)

**[04:31:44.120] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-003662)

**[04:32:31.260] Rep. Julius Cesar "Jay" V. Vergara** (asr-003669)

**[04:47:01.600] Rep. Jeffrey P. Ferrer** (asr-003845)  
So just a reminder.

**[04:47:02.400] Rep. Jeffrey P. Ferrer** (asr-003846)  
Thank you very much, Madam Speaker.

**[04:47:04.000] Rep. Jeffrey P. Ferrer** (asr-003847)

**[04:47:04.798] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003848)

**[04:47:06.958] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003849)

**[04:47:08.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003850)

**[04:47:13.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003852)

**[04:47:21.879] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003856)

**[04:47:30.718] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003859)

Next to interpolate

**[04:47:42.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-003864)  
is Congressman Tonypet Albano.

**[04:47:44.080] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003867)  
Thank you, Chair. Actually, it's not an interpolation

**[04:47:47.740] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003868)  
but more of a

**[04:47:50.520] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003869)  
giving my

**[04:47:52.180] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003870)  
trust and confidence

**[04:47:53.340] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003871)  
to the Secretary

**[04:47:54.220] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003872)  
for doing a good job,

**[04:47:55.940] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003873)  
especially the AFP.

**[04:47:58.880] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003874)  
And good afternoon,

**[04:48:00.660] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003875)  
Sir Gilbert

**[04:48:02.920] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003876)  
and your Chief of Staff

**[04:48:05.000] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003877)  
whom we've

**[04:48:05.680] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003878)  
confirmed in the CA recently.

**[04:48:09.260] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003879)  
And congratulations

**[04:48:10.460] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003880)  
to my cousin,

**[04:48:11.960] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003881)  
Lieutenant General Donald Gumiran for... [unclear]. But more than anything, Secretary, I talked to your Chief of Staff, si Toto, General Toto, who was asking

**[04:48:28.300] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003885)  
How we can strengthen your engineering brigade, because if you remember history, Europe and even Asia was built by the Marshall Plan. And if you have a strong engineering brigade that you have there, and we can add budgets, then you will be more receptive not only to help in disaster mitigation, especially in cases where flooding is there, but more so during...

**[04:49:00.000] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003904)  
the non-delicate seasons

**[04:49:02.480] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003905)  
where there's no typhoon,

**[04:49:03.880] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003906)  
we can use the engineers

**[04:49:04.860] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003907)  
to actually rehabilitate

**[04:49:07.020] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003908)  
a lot of areas

**[04:49:08.100] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003909)  
in the Philippines,

**[04:49:08.940] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003910)  
especially a particular

**[04:49:10.520] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003911)  
airline like what you did

**[04:49:11.340] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003912)  
in Mindalao.

**[04:49:14.620] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003913)  
So, I'd just like to ask, Secretary, if you'd be able to help us create some type of Marshall Plan for your engineering brigade.

**[04:49:23.180] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003914)  
Is that feasible or not?

**[04:49:24.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003915)  
Actually, our engineering brigades are converting into combat engineering brigades,

**[04:49:30.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003916)  
which are there to allow our equipment to work safely and sustainably.

**[04:49:46.980] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003917)  
For example, the emergency bridging that will support our heavy equipment.

**[04:49:52.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003918)  
Secondly, it will be the demining or equipment.

**[04:50:00.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003919)  
Which are engineers.

**[04:50:02.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003920)  
Secondly, if we have them, they should be devoted to base development and not contracted out anymore for civil engineering services.

**[04:50:12.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003921)  
So, actually, what has happened is we were devoting civil engineering equipment to non-AFP matters.

**[04:50:27.980] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003922)  
And our base...

**[04:50:30.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003923)  
Base development suffered.

**[04:50:31.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003924)  
So, one thing led to another.

**[04:50:35.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003925)  
Secondly, also, I understand the need for civil engineering, which is why the Army's game plan right now is to increase its footprint in areas which may be unoccupied.

**[04:50:52.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003926)  
And the first thing you need to do is access.

**[04:50:55.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003927)  
And the access to the base...

**[04:50:58.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003929)  
whether it be a division headquarters or a brigade headquarters, will be an all-weather, heavy-duty road access,

**[04:51:08.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003930)  
which will benefit the economy.

**[04:51:12.570] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003931)  
And it will be a connectivity node.

**[04:51:16.090] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003932)  
And it will allow resupply, and it will allow presence and deterrence at the same time,

**[04:51:22.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003933)  
whether it be for deforestation, illegal mining, or what have you.

**[04:51:27.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003934)  
So, uh...

**[04:51:29.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003935)  
We have that as a strategic plan because we need to increase the Army's footprint,

**[04:51:36.660] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003936)  
and we need strategic basing that will be resupplied by the nodes created on the baselines.

**[04:51:45.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003937)  
It's part of the integrated defense concept, which will, in turn, benefit the economy because of the all-weather access.

**[04:51:55.080] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003938)  
Thank you. Thank you, Secretary.

**[04:51:57.020] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003939)  
But in terms of disaster,

**[04:51:59.120] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003940)  
I'm sure you're always there because you're head of the NDRMC, if I remember.

**[04:52:03.780] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003941)  
So, like, yung mga basura na nangyayari dito, it's a man-made thing in Metro Manila,

**[04:52:09.580] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003942)  
and sometimes the government is being blamed for not implementing flood controls.

**[04:52:14.720] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003943)  
I'm sure with your help, Secretary, we will hopefully give enough budgets for your engineering brigade to help in that natural disaster, at least.

**[04:52:25.340] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003944)  
Because we are the number one prone disaster country in the whole world.

**[04:52:29.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003945)  
And, yes, yes, Your Honor, we totally agree.

**[04:52:32.640] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003946)  
And lastly, Madam Chair, we talked also in the Commission on Appointments about the drone program,

**[04:52:42.380] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003947)  
which, of course, it is highly sensitive, and the Chief of Staff has not really given us...

**[04:52:52.040] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003948)  
Well, you're supposed to report to us the progress of the drones.

**[04:52:56.200] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003949)  
Because, as you can understand,

**[04:52:58.980] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003950)  
a small country like Ukraine now can actually fight a superpower like Russia and beat them.

**[04:53:05.160] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003951)  
And if you remember, Secretary, we once talked about this two years ago,

**[04:53:10.440] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003952)  
and I told you that drone technology is the best way we won't lose anything.

**[04:53:14.820] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003953)  
And during armed conflicts, you can send it into critical areas.

**[04:53:19.080] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003954)  
So, I'd like lang the status of this, and I'm in full support.

**[04:53:23.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003955)  
We're building the framework and the backbone for it.

**[04:53:26.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003956)  
That's all I can disclose now.

**[04:53:29.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003957)  
And, you know, as I said, the drones themselves are easy to do.

**[04:53:33.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003958)  
It's what you put in them, depending on the purpose that we are.

**[04:53:40.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003959)  
And these are not autonomous devices [unclear], right?

**[04:53:46.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003960)  
So, we are developing the control systems for it alongside the capabilities.

**[04:53:52.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003961)  
We do have capabilities [unclear].

**[04:53:59.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003962)  
And that is well within the engagement framework of the Armed Forces of the Philippines.

**[04:54:10.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003963)  
That's all I can disclose.

**[04:54:12.180] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003964)  
Thank you, thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[04:54:14.120] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003965)  
With that, Madam Chairman, this representation is in full support of the Filipino Army and, of course, the DND.

**[04:54:21.400] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003966)  
Kayo po ang heroes ng Pilipino.

**[04:54:24.720] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003967)  
Nakakatulog po kami ng mahingbing dahil sa servisyo na inaalay niyo yung sarili niyong buhay.

**[04:54:30.300] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003968)  
Para sa kapakanan ng ating bansa at mga mamayang Pilipino.

**[04:54:35.660] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003969)  
Kaya saludo po kami at full support po ang House of Representatives.

**[04:54:40.060] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003970)  
Lalo na tinapunta po ako ni Speaker Boji dito para sabihin po sa inyo,

**[04:54:45.800] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003971)  
full support po ang opisina ni Speaker at ang mga congressman dito po.

**[04:54:50.220] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003972)  
Maraming salamat.

**[04:54:50.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003973)  
Gusto ko rin pong kunin itong pagkakataon na ito na batiin ang ating mabuting kaibigan.

**[04:54:59.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003974)  
Sa kanyang recent na estado sa buhay, finally, siya ay malapit na mag-sixty.

**[04:55:09.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003975)  
Isang taon lang yan.

**[04:55:11.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003976)  
Sixty na pala siya dahil ako ay sixty-two na.

**[04:55:14.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003977)  
Nagdesisyon na rin lumagay sa tahimik.

**[04:55:18.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003978)  
Hindi ko lang alam yung agwat nung dalawa.

**[04:55:21.540] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003979)  
Mr. Secretary, 32 years.

**[04:55:24.160] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003980)  
Mas matanda pa ako sa tatay at nanay nung pinakasalan kong Amerikano.

**[04:55:27.740] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003981)  
Kaya meron pa lang.

**[04:55:29.940] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003982)  
Pag-asa ang pagmamahal kahit na papalo.

**[04:55:33.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003983)  
[unclear].

**[04:55:36.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003984)  
And we really, as a personal friend, I congratulate you, Representative Tony P.

**[04:55:43.140] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003985)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[04:55:44.060] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003986)  
I will tell Nicky, [unclear].

**[04:55:50.280] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003987)  
Finally, you have reached that level of maturity to have [unclear].

**[04:55:55.900] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003988)  
Ako yata na-interpellate dito, Madam Chair.

**[04:55:58.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003989)  
Hindi naman.

**[04:55:59.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003990)  
[unclear] lang. [unclear] n'yo lang naman.

**[04:56:02.600] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003991)  
Salamat. Salamat, idol.

**[04:56:04.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003992)  
Congratulations.

**[04:56:05.140] Rep. Antonio "Tonypet" T. Albano** (asr-003993)  
Salamat. Salamat.

**[04:56:06.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003994)  
Hindi mo 'ko [unclear].

**[04:56:11.140] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-003995)  
Thank you. Thank you, Congressman Tonypet Albano.

**[04:56:15.100] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-003996)  
So that concludes our first round of interpolations.

**[04:56:19.340] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-003997)  
So for the second round, may I remind our colleagues that instead of the five minutes allotted time, it will be three minutes.

**[04:56:26.800] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-003998)  
Okay. So let's start.

**[04:56:33.620] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-003999)  
For the second round, we recognize Congressman Renee Co.

**[04:56:40.642] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004000)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[04:56:42.702] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004001)  
Madam Chair and to the department, I'd like to ask questions about what happened at Toboso.

**[04:56:50.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004002)  
I'm a human rights advocate.

**[04:56:53.080] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004003)  
I'm familiar with international humanitarian law.

**[04:56:57.119] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004004)  
As I assume, the rest of the department is also familiar with.

**[04:57:03.038] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004005)  
And I have questions.

**[04:57:05.450] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004006)  
Lalo na po.

**[04:57:06.037] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004007)  
Kung paano ginasta yung pera at resources para po mangyari yung operasyon.

**[04:57:11.576] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004008)  
Can I ask po, magkano po ba ang nagamit para po sa operasyon at Toboso?

**[04:57:16.366] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004009)  
We cannot disclose that, Your Honor.

**[04:57:18.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004010)  
It's operationally secure.

**[04:57:21.630] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004011)  
All we can say is that so far as we are concerned, the operation was legitimately conducted.

**[04:57:32.536] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004012)  
There were four separate encounters.

**[04:57:35.826] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004013)  
Chances were given to the NPA to surrender.

**[04:57:42.167] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004014)  
And as a matter of fact, one soldier was retaliated upon when he went home and he was murdered.

**[04:57:49.803] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004015)  
This was an offshoot also of complaints of the community as we gathered here.

**[04:57:57.338] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004016)  
Madam Chair, I just remember that it is on public record that the AFP mentioned that this is an intelligence operation.

**[04:58:06.417] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004017)  
Definitely.

**[04:58:07.341] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004018)  
Definitely, it is an intelligence operation because we cannot operate indiscriminately, Your Honor.

**[04:58:12.887] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004019)  
However, the amounts involved will compromise our operational security.

**[04:58:19.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004020)  
Because to our knowledge, last year, there was a proposal of 94 billion of maintenance and operating expenses.

**[04:58:28.080] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004021)  
So may maaasahan po ba tayong liquidation of the amounts that was spent on this operation?

**[04:58:36.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004023)  
Ballpark figure?

**[04:58:37.028] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004025)  
May liquidation yan dahil mahirap naman itago yung operational secure basis.

**[04:58:43.118] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004026)  
Gawa pa ng kulang talaga ang budget natin.

**[04:58:46.773] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004027)  
Eto pinag-iingatan talaga.

**[04:58:51.237] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004028)  
What transparency measure, Madam Chair, can the department provide na nagamit hindi masyadong maraming ginasta

**[04:59:01.414] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004029)  
and hindi po nasayang yung pera on this operation?

**[04:59:04.467] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004030)  
Ito po ay operation na ang expenditures nito ay minimal, hindi katulad nung pag-gastos natin ng gasolina doon sa West Philippine Sea

**[04:59:15.519] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004031)  
or sa tulong sa humanitarian assistance and disaster response.

**[04:59:19.807] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004032)  
At ito ay batay din sa mga tulong ng community members kung saan nakakalap ng pagsusumbong.

**[04:59:27.603] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004033)  
Pagsusumbong, aking mismong inalam nung dinalaw ko yung bangkay at labi nung namatay na private,

**[04:59:37.505] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004034)  
sargent, corporal ba, inamatay, inamatay, sabay din ng kawayan na insidente.

**[04:59:44.307] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004035)  
incidente kung saan naman

**[04:59:46.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004036)  
bago ito nangyari, may pati

**[04:59:52.279] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004037)  
lola na binitay

**[04:59:54.111] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004038)  
ng NPA na inaako nila.

**[04:59:57.067] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004039)  
Speaking of community

**[04:59:58.483] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004040)  
members, Madam Chair,

**[05:00:00.816] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004041)  
kaya naman po natin tinatanong

**[05:00:02.431] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004042)  
ito dahil may nilabas po

**[05:14:20.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004459)  
And we cannot allow any attached agency

**[05:14:23.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004460)  
to be used improperly.

**[05:14:25.484] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004461)  
Kasi ito po ay niliwanag po ng PAOC.

**[05:14:30.854] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004462)  
No?

**[05:14:31.895] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004463)  
Bagamat Chinese yung kumpanya,

**[05:14:35.101] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004464)  
hindi basta-basta ito mati-disrupt

**[05:14:38.307] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004465)  
or kung gumagawa ng legal,

**[05:14:41.513] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004466)  
eh illegal po yung nangyayari.

**[05:14:44.719] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004467)  
Talamak na...

**[05:14:45.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004468)  
Talamak na iligal. So much so that there is a million tons of ore in the area now which we need to dispose of.

**[05:14:56.949] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004469)  
Pinapasok po nila ang substandard material, nilulusaw po nila ito at binibenta na rebars sa lugar na prone nga sa lindol.

**[05:15:08.958] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004470)  
So ito'y nakapipinsala sa community. Among other violations, Your Honor, no?

**[05:15:13.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004471)  
So much so that the ECC was cancelled and there was a cease and desist order issued by DNR.

**[05:15:23.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004472)  
So ang sinasabi nyo may legal basis?

**[05:15:26.145] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004473)  
Meron po. Kasi po bago po ito sinalakay ng NBI, may search warrant po na kinuha sa isang judge.

**[05:15:38.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004474)  
Of course, the process of, uh, uh, uh, securing a search warrant.

**[05:15:42.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004475)  
The search warrant needs, uh, personal examination of witnesses.

**[05:15:57.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004476)  
Ayon po sa NBI, nag-test-buy pa nga po sila ng produkto at ito'y lumalabas na labag sa Philippine standards po.

**[05:16:01.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004477)  
Okay. Could you elaborate on the plans regarding the development of the area as a dry dock?

**[05:16:07.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004478)  
At kasama ba ito sa budget proposal and what is the timeline?

**[05:16:11.969] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004479)  
Kailangan po siya sa budget proposal pero kailangan po natin dahil ang dry dock po ng Philippine Navy ngayon ay nasa Sangli, Fort San Felipe po.

**[05:16:28.287] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004480)  
At ito'y mababaw na mababaw na po, hindi mapasukan ng mga barko natin na atin.

**[05:16:35.982] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004481)  
So kailangan po natin dahil may papadating po tayo na 6 hanggang 8 na barko, wala na po siyang pag-aayusan.

**[05:16:47.879] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004482)  
So ang mangyayari po dito?

**[05:16:50.275] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004483)  
So kung walang dry dock, maliban pa po sa ibang equipment, ilalabas po natin for maintenance, kailangan po natin ito para ma-maintain yung fleet natin.

**[05:16:59.911] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004484)  
Ito'y solely Philippine Navy use at pagka nakuha na natin po yung titulo dahil dapat i-separate po ito sa Phoebe Deck area, popondohan na po.

**[05:17:06.593] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004485)  
Okay. So ano ang timeframe po ng pag-develop nito?

**[05:17:10.036] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004486)  
As soon as possible.

**[05:17:12.027] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004487)  
Oo po, dahil nauuna na po yung equipment kesa sa dry dock eh.

**[05:17:15.672] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004488)  
So ang sinasabi nyo ba ay basically ay aabondanahin na ng Navy ang Sangli dry dock?

**[05:17:29.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004489)  
Oo po, dahil ito po ay magiging international airport na po as a, para po sa domestic flights din na hindi na kakayanin ng naiya.

**[05:17:41.328] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004490)  
Secondly po, talagang mababaw na po ang Sangli.

**[05:17:44.396] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004491)  
Pangalawag, para pumasok po sa Sangli, marami pong baklad na iiwasan.

**[05:17:49.676] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004492)  
So hindi na po sustainable ang Sangli.

**[05:17:52.798] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004493)  
At ito pong Sangli ay designed para sa mas maliit na mga sasakyang pandagat, hindi po designed sa lalim na kailangan pong i-service ang Philippine Navy vessels.

**[05:18:06.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004494)  
Okay.

**[05:18:09.107] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004495)  
Okay.

**[05:18:09.607] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004496)  
So,

**[05:18:10.494] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004497)  
ngayon,

**[05:18:11.818] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004498)  
ang tanong po natin ngayon ay,

**[05:18:17.754] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004499)  
ang nagkukomplika dito,

**[05:18:18.975] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004500)  
yung reports kaugnay sa Tagaloan or FIVIDEC,

**[05:18:22.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004501)  
na merong US soldiers na nagtangkang pumasok pero hindi sila nakapasok.

**[05:18:26.958] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004502)  
And this was noted in a police blotter of the PNP as well as acknowledged by the US Embassy.

**[05:18:34.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004503)  
So ano po ang interest ng US sa FIVIDEC?

**[05:18:39.238] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004504)  
Last year,

**[05:18:39.893] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004505)  
nagkaroon po tayo ng balikatan activity doon sa area.

**[05:18:44.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004506)  
So this year, kaugnay po ng hindi po solely US yun, may kasama din pong,

**[05:18:52.836] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004507)  
ano na, tawag nito, 4th Infantry Division, hindi lang nagkaroon ng coordination doon sa area.

**[05:19:05.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004508)  
Kasi hindi po pwedeng magamit yung PBDEC mismo kasi hindi po environmentally cleared ng DNR yung area.

**[05:19:13.683] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004509)  
Malamang nag-a-access lang po sila.

**[05:19:15.430] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004510)  
Nag-a-access lang po sila para makita yung bay.

**[05:19:16.862] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004511)  
Pero ganun pa man, hindi sila pinayagan ng Philippine Army na pumasok dahil sa lack of coordination.

**[05:19:22.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004512)  
At mahigpit po yung bilin sa Philippine Army dahil po the area is not environmentally cleared by PNRI and DENR.

**[05:19:27.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004513)  
We cannot take the risk of people going in there and getting whatever—uh, whatever disease.

**[05:19:35.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004514)  
So ano pong interest ng US military sa area na yun?

**[05:19:39.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004515)  
Do they also want to have access or use to the facilities that will be put up?

**[05:19:45.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004516)  
That is purely balikatan, Your Honor.

**[05:19:48.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004517)  
Balikatan?

**[05:19:48.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004518)  
Yeah, a loading exercise which happened last year.

**[05:19:51.864] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004519)  
Pero posible rin po na gamitin ng US Navy kung sakaling maitayo na yung dry dock, pwede rin nilang gamitin?

**[05:19:58.191] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004520)  
Sa atin lang po puno na yun eh. Kaya kailangan din na po natin.

**[05:20:02.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004521)  
So I don't see any space for expansion on that area.

**[05:20:06.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004522)  
So you do not see that it is?

**[05:20:09.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004523)  
No, Your Honor. We need to have our own dedicated dry dock actually.

**[05:20:13.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004524)  
Okay. Ngayon, yugnay ko na po sa Pax Silica.

**[05:20:21.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004525)  
Okay. Nabanggit nyo kanina na hindi kayo directly involved dyan.

**[05:20:29.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004526)  
Pero yung Pax Silica, as the US explains it, this has to do with securing the defense industrial base of the US.

**[05:20:38.878] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004527)  
Yung kanilang supply chain.

**[05:20:39.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004528)  
Lalo na for semiconductors, especially given the importance of AI in economic as well as military competition with China.

**[05:20:50.638] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004529)  
Okay. So merong Pax Silica sa New Clark City. Magtatayo daw ng manufacturing facilities.

**[05:21:04.718] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004530)  
Bukod doon, nage-expand ang US ng kanyang not just military but also civilian or dual-use presence.

**[05:21:12.638] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004532)  
So halimbawa yung Subic, regular na ginagamit ng US Navy for repairs.

**[05:21:16.878] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004533)  
Yung recent na destroyer ng US na nawala ng kuryente for four days in the South China Sea, dinala yan sa Subic.

**[05:21:28.813] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004534)  
Sa Mindanao, sa Davao Gulf, magtatayo daw ng oil depot ang US para sa kanila, sa US Navy.

**[05:21:43.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004535)  
Ito po ang tanong ko. Kasi Pax Silica, data and so on. Pinakita po sa gera sa Iran that in the 21st century, with the use of drones and missiles,

**[05:21:58.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004536)  
ay pinakita ng Iran na winasak niya yung US Navy base in Bahrain, yung Manama base, kaya namumuroblema yung mga aircraft carrier nila ngayon.

**[05:22:18.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004537)  
Hindi masuplayan. Inataki din ng Iran, di lang yung mga military bases ng US, kung hindi pati yung mga commercial facilities ng US in the area.

**[05:22:30.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004538)  
For example, yung mga data centers ng Amazon at saka ng Oracle in places like UAE and Kuwait.

**[05:22:47.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004539)  
Ang tanong ay, napakaraming mga pasilidad natin ngayon na may presensya ng US, both military and civilian.

**[05:22:52.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004540)  
Subic, yung Davao possibly, and itong New Clark City. Ngayon, kung sakaling magkaroon ng gera sa pagitan ng US at China,

**[05:23:09.085] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004541)  
sa issue ng Taiwan, pwede po nating mahiduha na tatarget, katulad ng ginawa ng Iran sa Middle East,

**[05:23:19.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004542)  
ay tatargetin ng China ang mga pasilidad, both military as well as commercial, ng US sa Pilipinas.

**[05:23:31.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004543)  
At ang concern po natin, yung mga presensya ng US sa Pilipinas ay nasa mga strategic and critical economic.

**[05:23:40.878] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004544)  
At yung mga infrastructure natin, Subic, New Clark City, ay yung planong joint ammunition facility sa, I think also Subic,

**[05:23:51.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004545)  
between the US and the Philippine Armed Forces, Davao possibly kung mayroong fuel depot doon.

**[05:23:58.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004546)  
So in other words, pwedeng maging target ito. It is logical to assume na yan ang unang-unang tatargetin.

**[05:24:09.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004547)  
ng China kung sakaling magkaroon ng gera with the US.

**[05:24:16.349] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004548)  
The question here is, ano ang kakayahan ng armed forces na depensahan ang mga pasilidad na ito?

**[05:24:21.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004549)  
Kasi ang nangyari in the Gulf, in the Middle East, ay nawasak po yun.

**[05:24:24.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004550)  
There is huge economic damage to the economies of the Gulf.

**[05:24:34.101] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004551)  
Sila mayamang bansa.

**[05:24:35.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004552)  
High-tech missile defenses.

**[05:24:39.724] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004553)  
Paano po ang Pilipinas?

**[05:24:42.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004554)  
Ibig sabihin, at risk ang mga buhay ng Pilipino at ang ekonomiya ng Pilipinas

**[05:24:52.158] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004555)  
dahil sa lumalawak na presensya ng US at lalo pang lalawak, halimbawa kung matuloy ang Paxilica.

**[05:24:58.983] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004556)  
So, di ba pinag-iisipan ito ng DND?

**[05:25:05.830] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004557)  
At paano mapoprotektahan?

**[05:25:11.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004558)  
Sa laban po ng marami sa katanungan, I understand the point of the representative.

**[05:25:18.878] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004559)  
First, doon po sa hinggil sa China threat.

**[05:25:21.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004560)  
Whether or not meron tayong mga alyansa, kalinsabay ng Amerika bilang treaty partner,

**[05:25:30.701] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004561)  
may overt acts na po ang China na may balakin sila sa atin.

**[05:25:36.947] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004562)  
Lately nga po.

**[05:25:38.616] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004563)  
Ang kanilang statement na gagawa sila ng maritime observatory sa Scarborough, sa Bajo de Masinloc

**[05:25:52.190] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004564)  
na maliwanag po, wala silang karapatan.

**[05:25:56.878] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004565)  
At ang pagtatawag nga po nila ng island, Tau is island, dito sa Seoul,

**[05:26:02.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004566)  
maybe presages, pre-messages an intention to fortify it.

**[05:26:05.682] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004567)  
Just like they did in Mischief Reef.

**[05:26:10.070] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004570)  
So, given that, may choice po tayo na itutuloy po ng China ang kanilang ginagawa na wala tayong gagawin

**[05:26:20.934] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004571)  
or kukuha tayo ng kaalyado.

**[05:26:23.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004572)  
Ang sagdanang amin po sa DND, the more we can do resilience with as many partners as possible

**[05:26:27.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004573)  
in the quickest amount of time, given the fact na assumption po namin na may balak ang China

**[05:26:34.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004574)  
because geographically,

**[05:26:42.385] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004575)  
ang Pilipinas lang ang tanging hadlang sa China, sa Pacific Ocean.

**[05:26:47.330] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004576)  
Secondly, kahit na po sa Pacific Islands, nababahala na rin po ang dati nga na nakikipag-ayos sa kanila,

**[05:26:56.638] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004577)  
Solomon Islands, na nagpabagsak sila ng ballistic missile doon na may kukunting warning.

**[05:27:05.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004578)  
Pati po Australia and New Zealand,

**[05:27:08.742] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004579)  
na napakalayo na po, ay nababahala din kasi ang supply chains nila na-threaten

**[05:27:16.317] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004580)  
at ang activities ng China doon sa lugar na yon ay patent.

**[05:27:23.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004581)  
So, alam ko po yung punto ng ating congressman na will the alliances presage or be a precursor to a Chinese attack.

**[05:27:36.878] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004582)  
I believe.

**[05:27:37.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004583)  
That since China has already by overt action its intention to the Philippines,

**[05:27:44.958] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004584)  
it's beside the point already, it's either you be co-opted or not.

**[05:27:51.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004585)  
Now, to build defense resilience, we know that it is a very expensive proposition and growing more expensive every day.

**[05:28:01.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004586)  
Which is why also countries like Australia and Japan,

**[05:28:07.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004587)  
and South Korea for that matter and Canada as distant as that feel that their supply chains are threatened and France as distant as that and India for that matter who although pound for pound they can match the People's Liberation Army,

**[05:28:29.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004588)  
there is still a lot of shall we say,

**[05:28:38.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004589)  
defense spending going on in there because of their neighbors that are also being accommodated within that axis.

**[05:29:01.638] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004590)  
So,

**[05:29:02.638] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004591)  
sa akin pong palagay ang pagpapalawak ng alyansa at pagdedevelop ng más resilient supply chain lalo na sa critical technologies

**[05:29:07.277] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004592)  
na,

**[05:29:08.458] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004593)  
nga,

**[05:29:08.958] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004594)  
I really am not familiar with the corporate workings of Paxilica but the end product we need and we need the supply chain as fast as possible because the experience has it in combat areas, they need to retool this every three days and perhaps even faster with AI.

**[05:29:33.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004595)  
It's either we adopt the technology and try to use it as best as possible as we can or be taken over.

**[05:29:43.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004596)  
taken over by the technology without anything happening.

**[05:29:48.958] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004597)  
So I know it is a debate which there is – there are concerns, however, in view of the fact

**[05:29:59.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004598)  
that we see the – and not only us, even Vietnam sees it, even other countries see

**[05:30:08.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004599)  
the expansion of China.

**[05:30:11.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004600)  
And given the fact that we have already tried the latest attempt, ang latest pagsubok yung

**[05:30:28.878] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004601)  
pag-file natin ng delineation ng ating maritime waters sa ITLOS under the UN, eh dito naman

**[05:30:36.592] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004602)  
tayo talagang nakatutok.

**[05:30:38.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004603)  
But at the end of the day, we also need to stand up for –

**[05:30:41.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004604)  
as best as we can for the territorial integrity and sovereignty of the country in the face

**[05:30:49.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004605)  
of overt attempts by China.

**[05:30:51.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004606)  
Even as the gentleman raised earlier, yung pagpapalit ng naratibo sa Batanes, by a

**[05:30:58.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004607)  
state university, part and parcel ng UFW ng China ito eh.

**[05:31:12.266] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004608)  
At ito'y realisasyon, hindi lang ng Pilipinas, pero maraming bansa.

**[05:31:17.347] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004609)  
So, sabi'ng pong palagay ay kesa sa wala tayong kapabilidad na to deter, no?

**[05:31:25.784] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004610)  
Because the exercise is in deterrence, eh, rather than in reaction.

**[05:31:32.558] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004611)  
That's what we're trying to do as best as we can.

**[05:31:35.963] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004612)  
So, salamat po sa katanungan.

**[05:31:42.558] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004614)  
Thank you, Cong. Tinio.

**[05:31:48.400] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004616)

Madam?

Yes.

**[05:32:02.798] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004621)

**[05:32:05.520] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004622)  
He would like to, um, ask his questions as well.

**[05:32:08.000] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004623)  
But if it's okay, with the indulgence of Cong. Pleyto, may we recognize Cong. Sarah?

**[05:32:13.709] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004625)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[05:32:17.130] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004626)  
Maigli lang po dahil tayo rin po ay magsisponsor ng isang bill ngayon sa plenaryo.

**[05:32:20.552] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004627)  
Ang question ko po ay saan po kumukuha ang AFP ng PONDO para sa kanyang mga learning tours?

**[05:32:23.974] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004628)  
Meron po bang learning tours?

**[05:32:29.302] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004633)  
Learning tours?

**[05:32:30.162] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004634)  
Educational trips?

**[05:32:31.023] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004636)  
Meron po education budget at meron naman po grants.

**[05:32:34.170] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004638)  
All right.

**[05:32:35.039] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004655)  
institutional arrangements with other countries like Australia, India, the United States, Japan, and Korea

**[05:32:40.979] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004656)  
for professional military education like cadets.

**[05:32:49.734] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004657)  
But we see the need to have to invest on a department level

**[05:32:56.871] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004658)  
and on the military level for higher education on multidisciplinary aspects.

**[05:33:04.081] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004659)  
Okay. Ang katanungan ko, Madam Chair, ay tungkol doon sa mga learning tours

**[05:33:08.329] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004660)  
involving educational institutions as well as other institutions for learning

**[05:33:14.653] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004661)  
na hino-host po natin dito sa bansa. May specific line item po ba para sa bansa?

**[05:33:19.167] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004662)  
Ito ay reserve force development po. Pero yung courses po sa education,

**[05:33:24.850] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004663)  
eh, wala pong budget para doon.

**[05:33:26.710] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004664)  
Okay. Sige. Hingin na lang po natin yung kopya ng report kung paano yan ginagastas.

**[05:33:31.030] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004665)  
These are the ROTC programs, actually.

**[05:33:34.422] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004666)  
Natanong natin ito, Madam Chair, kasi po, may concern din po na nilapit sa amin ng mga child rights advocates.

**[05:33:40.941] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004667)  
Naghanda po kami ng ibang photos ng mga nakaabot sa atin na ulat

**[05:33:46.113] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004668)  
na meron daw pong mga educational tours na hino-host ang ating military

**[05:33:53.081] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004669)  
kung saan dinaduluhan po ng mga bata na sila po ay pinapahawak ng mga baril.

**[05:34:00.604] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004670)  
Opo. So, ito po ay tatlong instance.

**[05:34:05.191] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004671)  
Ang mga detalye. Pero nandyan na po yan lahat. Pero ang katanungan ko po ay bakit po pinapahintulutan ito ng DND at AFP?

**[05:34:13.032] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004672)  
Duly noted, Your Honor. Hindi dapat talaga umano yan.

**[05:34:17.065] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004673)  
Yes.

**[05:34:17.950] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004674)  
Duly noted.

**[05:34:18.736] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004675)  
Madam Chair, nakapost pa po ito sa social media. At nakakabahala po kasi marami ng mga conventions.

**[05:34:28.810] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004676)  
Ako rin po nababahala dito. At hindi po po ito tolerate yung ganyang gawain. Whether posted or non-posted.

**[05:34:34.193] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004677)  
Yes.

**[05:34:35.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004678)  
Madam Chair, ang partner po dito sa Bukidnon, halimbawa Child Development Center,

**[05:34:40.474] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004679)  
ibig sabihin po nasa six years old below yung mga participants niyan. So, ganun po ka-bata.

**[05:34:46.755] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004680)  
Kaya dapat may malinaw na palisiya na maipagbawal po yung ganitong paghahawak ng baril ng mga bata.

**[05:34:54.083] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004681)  
Lalo na po sa mga incidents ngayon sa mga eskwelahan. Kung saan malaking issue ang gun access at ang safety po ng ating mga bata at kabataan sa ating mga bata.

**[05:35:05.902] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004682)  
This is what I will do, Your Honor. I'll ask the DSWD to work with us to lecture to the units what are the proper engagement parameters for the youth under 18.

**[05:35:18.575] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004683)  
Pati po sa mga bata, dapat po matiyak natin na wala pong ganito.

**[05:35:25.006] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004684)  
Yun nga po, yung minoritidad. As legally defined.

**[05:35:27.502] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004685)  
Yes.

**[05:35:28.454] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004686)  
I will ask the DSWD to, which we have close working relationships with.

**[05:35:35.118] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004687)  
This is something that I cannot tolerate, Your Honor.

**[05:35:39.668] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004688)  
Meron po ba tayo sa inyong mga standards pagdating sa pag-uphold ng ating responsibility?

**[05:35:46.036] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004689)  
Meron po bang parusa yung ganitong tipo ng mga paglabag?

**[05:35:50.013] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004690)  
Ito po gawa ng palagay ko sa pagpapalit ng mission area ngayon ay lack of awareness ito.

**[05:36:00.728] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004691)  
Ngunit ako po ay personal kong...

**[05:36:04.625] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004692)  
I papaloap ito at i-dedirect ko ang Undersecretary for Defense Administration to take charge of this.

**[05:36:17.288] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004693)  
This should not happen.

**[05:36:18.953] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004694)  
Yes, Madam Chair. Dapat po mahigpit yan na maipagbawal.

**[05:36:21.902] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004695)  
Ngayon naman, Madam Chair, tungkol dun sa mga na-report po na ulat ng sexual assault within the military.

**[05:36:29.766] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004696)  
Napalakas na po ba natin halimbawa yung mga mechanisms for sexual assault prevention?

**[05:36:33.698] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004697)  
Para po matiyak na yung mga kaso, mga complaints na iimbestigahan at natitiyak natin na may pananagutan?

**[05:36:43.726] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004698)  
That is one area po na nagtutulungan din po kami sa DSWD.

**[05:36:48.417] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004699)  
Kasi victim identification is also very important.

**[05:36:54.985] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004700)  
And it's certainly something that we really cannot tolerate.

**[05:36:57.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004701)  
Whether it is...

**[05:37:00.615] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004702)  
ah...

**[05:37:02.491] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004703)  
heterosexual or otherwise.

**[05:37:08.212] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004704)  
In this regard, Madam Chair, I'd like to put on record our request for the DND as well as all its attached agencies

**[05:37:15.978] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004705)  
to submit its compliance report on its gender and development mandate.

**[05:37:20.832] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004706)  
Mahalaga po yan dahil malaking bahagi din ang ating pondo.

**[05:37:23.744] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004707)  
And lastly, Madam Chair, alam ko po patapos na rin po yung aking oras.

**[05:37:28.598] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004708)  
At tungkol po sa deployment ng Civil-Military Operations Battalion

**[05:37:33.452] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004709)  
sa Metro Manila at sa ating mga siyudad.

**[05:37:38.306] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004710)  
May guiding principles, may policies po ba ang DND tungkol dito?

**[05:37:43.565] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004711)  
Meron po.

**[05:37:44.565] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004712)  
Lalo-lalo na ngayon ang major threat area po na pinagtututukan po nila ay ang United Front Works.

**[05:37:52.561] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004713)  
It's against foreign influence.

**[05:37:56.559] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004714)  
We now have a consolidated CMO command.

**[05:38:02.556] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004715)  
Which takes threats to national security on a more information-based basis.

**[05:38:17.548] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004716)  
And now the major concentration is on foreign malign influence.

**[05:38:23.255] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004717)  
Naintindihan po natin yung may kinalaman dun sa mga threats pagdating sa national security.

**[05:38:29.174] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004718)  
Pero may mga nakaabot din po tayo na balita na...

**[05:38:33.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004719)  
Halimbawa po, subject ng House Resolution No. 1203,

**[05:38:37.066] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004720)  
yung presence po ng 12 Civil-Military Operations Battalion sa Marikina, sa Caloocan.

**[05:38:42.985] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004721)  
At may mga balita po, halimbawa, pinapapunta yung isang nanay na nagsasalita tungkol sa karapatan niya sa pabahay

**[05:38:50.877] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004722)  
na pinapatawag po at pinapasurrender.

**[05:38:52.850] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004723)  
So yung ganitong tipo po ng mga kaso, dapat sa tingin po natin, dahil nagdadala ng tensyon, ng takot dun sa mga kababayan natin,

**[05:39:01.728] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004724)  
na wala namang kinalaman sa external threats, wala namang kinalaman sa threats sa ating national security.

**[05:39:09.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004725)  
In fact, ang hinaharap nila, threats sa kanilang housing, threats sa kanilang livelihood.

**[05:39:16.526] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004726)  
Sila po yung naapektuhan ng presence ng ganitong tipo ng batalyon sa kanilang mga lugar.

**[05:39:23.617] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004727)  
Doon po sa usapin na yan, of course, internal security is also part and parcel nito.

**[05:39:29.589] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004728)  
Part and parcel din ng CMO batalyon operations po ito.

**[05:39:35.561] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004729)  
What I can guarantee, Your Honor, is that we have to really also marshal our resources into something more tangible,

**[05:39:51.486] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004730)  
given the fact that the President himself has said that we need to pinch each and every peso right now.

**[05:39:59.448] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004731)  
Given that, Your Honor, that's why we already created the CMO command on a national level,

**[05:40:09.402] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004732)  
so to have a more structured approach to information dissemination and to combat foreign malign information.

**[05:40:21.262] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004733)  
Yes, Madam Chair, kasi ang nangyayari dito, parang wala ho tayong tiwala dun sa civilian bureaucracy,

**[05:40:25.956] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004734)  
tsaka sa local police natin para ma…

**[05:40:28.303] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004736)  
ma-care of yung ganitong tipo ng mga usapin, kung meron man.

**[05:40:31.433] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004737)  
At we'll take the word of the Secretary on this. At sana rin po, hindi lang po sa DND, dito rin po sa Kongreso,

**[05:40:38.474] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004738)  
sana ho hindi natin mapalagpas na mapag-usapan ang mga mahalagang resolusyon na ito para matiyak ang…

**[05:40:42.386] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004739)  
Maliwanag po yung pinakita na may bata na may armas eh, yan di ko po…

**[05:40:47.131] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004740)  
Of course, yes. Opo.

**[05:40:53.290] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004740-b)  
Pero pagdating po dito sa presence din ng CMO,

**[05:40:57.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004741)  
sa presence din po ng military sa ating mga communities, dapat may malinaw na pabantayan.

**[05:41:02.977] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004742)  
Especially with the incidents of school shootings in Zamboanga, then you have this kind of things.

**[05:41:06.953] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004743)  
I cannot allow that to happen.

**[05:41:10.503] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004744)  
Yes. Maraming salamat, Madam Chair. At abangan po natin yung mga hiningi nating reports tungkol sa TICAS implementation,

**[05:41:18.263] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004745)  
gayon din po sa lahat ng affected ng fishing ban na may kinalaman sa balikatan exercises, at iba pa nating mga hiningi.

**[05:41:26.993] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004746)  
Manghingi na din po tayo ng status report tungkol po sa presence ng 12th CMO dito sa Metro Manila.

**[05:41:33.783] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004747)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Chair. Maraming salamat.

**[05:41:37.368] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004748)

Maraming salamat.

**[05:43:00.080] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004760)

**[05:43:07.557] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004761)

**[05:43:20.400] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004762)

**[05:43:28.287] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004763)  
Saludo po ako sa agarang pagkakataon.

**[05:43:30.835] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004765)

**[05:43:40.558] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004766)

**[05:44:00.495] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004767)  
[unclear]

**[05:44:01.520] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004768)  
malakas na ulan. Sa Disaster and Institutional Research Program, na nabanggit mo kanina,

**[05:44:12.373] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004770)

**[05:44:34.232] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004773)

Kaya, Madam Chair,

**[05:44:41.760] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004775)

**[05:45:17.975] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004780)

**[05:45:29.804] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004781)

to address how the OCD can facilitate their resilience measures to ensure...

**[05:46:37.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004787)  
The...

**[05:46:38.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004788)  
As...

**[05:46:39.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004789)  
The distinguished representative is familiar, the Metro Manila Earthquake Impact Resilience Study is circa 2003.

**[05:46:48.798] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004790)  
And a lot has changed since then.

**[05:46:53.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004791)  
And we are...

**[05:46:55.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004792)  
I approved yesterday the JICA papers to be processed to the Cabinet for a revisiting of this earthquake impact study.

**[05:47:09.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004793)  
And...

**[05:47:09.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004794)  
We already agreed that the shepherding institution will be the DOST.

**[05:47:17.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004795)  
That's step one because we have to ensure that in order for us to operate,

**[05:47:26.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004796)  
that connectivity and other critical services continue.

**[05:47:29.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004797)  
If not, we are hampered.

**[05:47:31.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004798)  
On the Armed Forces side, Your Honor, we have the capability to operate.

**[05:47:39.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004799)  
We have the capability to operate even except for major thoroughfares.

**[05:47:43.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004800)  
If those are hampered, then our ability to access the areas may be hampered except by aerial capabilities.

**[05:47:56.958] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004801)  
We are also trying to also differentiate between two major earthquake...

**[05:48:07.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004802)  
earthquake...

**[05:48:09.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004803)  
sources.

**[05:48:10.638] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004804)  
The West Valley Fault and the Manila Trench.

**[05:48:14.878] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004805)  
On the impact of an earthquake in these areas.

**[05:48:18.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004806)  
Because in the Manila Trench, Your Honor, the effect will be a tsunami.

**[05:48:22.958] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004807)  
And in the West Valley Fault, as we are very familiar with, it will be subsidence, liquefaction, major damage to connectivity and to lives.

**[05:48:39.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004808)  
So, I think it is healthy.

**[05:48:43.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004809)  
And although we cannot predict when it happens,

**[05:48:48.798] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004810)  
and according to Secretary Solidum, it happened historically every 400 to 500 years.

**[05:48:59.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004811)  
And give or take 200 years, but we are within that 200-year grace period.

**[05:49:06.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004812)  
Nonetheless, I think the earthquake...

**[05:49:09.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004813)  
impact study is essential to have any properly engineered solution at least to allow first responders to access communities.

**[05:49:27.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004814)  
On the issue of flooding, Your Honor, I think it is a major concern which needs major engineering.

**[05:49:34.798] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004815)  
In an area which is now prone to liquefaction...

**[05:49:39.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004816)  
subsidence, and sea-level rise.

**[05:49:43.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004817)  
The concern of Congresswoman Bondoc is shared also by my home province on the MacArthur Highway side.

**[05:49:51.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004818)  
As Engineer Pleyto is very familiar with.

**[05:49:56.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004819)  
Because our home province not only receives runoff from the Sierra Madre.

**[05:50:09.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004820)  
But also the Agno River and the Zambales Mountain Range.

**[05:50:14.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004821)  
So, it is really a catch basin.

**[05:50:16.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004822)  
And unfortunately, Lahar had clogged our major thoroughfares.

**[05:50:22.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004823)  
And all I know, it needs a complex engineering solution.

**[05:50:25.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004824)  
Maybe requiring a new carriageway altogether.

**[05:50:31.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004825)  
And an elevated basis.

**[05:50:34.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004826)  
And we saw that in Typhoon Ondoy.

**[05:50:37.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004827)  
Where?

**[05:50:39.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004828)  
We requested the MRT to work 24 hours because it was the only way on the initial stage that we could connect because of the flooding.

**[05:50:53.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004829)  
That is a matter I undertook to raise as a major concern area on the national NDRRMC level.

**[05:51:04.718] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004830)  
And on the NEDA level.

**[05:51:07.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004831)  
I think it's high time.

**[05:51:08.958] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004832)  
Your Honor, that we have systems, engineered solutions for river systems, water systems, and for connectivity points.

**[05:51:22.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004833)  
It needs to be engineered and it should be science-based.

**[05:51:26.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004834)  
If not, it won't be sustainable, Your Honor.

**[05:51:29.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004835)  
Thank you.

**[05:51:30.020] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004836)  
That's right, Secretary.

**[05:51:32.798] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004837)  
Secretary, I know that during my time, I'm with JICA studies.

**[05:51:37.168] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004838)  
We're with Secretary Solidum.

**[05:51:38.878] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004839)  
When I was the Regional Director of Public Works dito sa NCR.

**[05:51:47.120] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004840)  
Gusto ko lang idagdag, Secretary, we have seen how the Philippine Air Force under the Lieutenant General Cordura

**[05:51:55.360] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004841)  
para yung pag-provide po nyo ng critical humanitarian assistance through sustained airlift operations.

**[05:52:04.638] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004842)  
May record po kasi ako dito yung June 12 to June 14, 2026.

**[05:52:08.958] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004843)  
Where Philippine Air Force delivered 1,200 DSWD family packs, ready-to-eat packs, jerry cans filled with clean water to barangay's lagimit,

**[05:52:20.480] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004844)  
and aligado in the isolated municipality of Glan, in the Sarangani Province, including with clean water in barangay Aligado.

**[05:52:34.558] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004845)  
Ang ating ding magigiting na sundalo ay nagsasagawa ng humanitarian assistance disaster response sa pamamagitan ng mga sandbagging,

**[05:52:44.607] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004846)  
ng tunituniladang buhangin sa mga apektadong bahagi ng ilog upang makatatag ang lugar at makatulong may iwasan karagdagang pinsaladulot ang habagat,

**[05:52:55.761] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004847)  
yung southwest monsoon rain sa lalawigan ng Tarlac, yung baya ng ating sekretary.

**[05:52:59.920] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004848)  
Maganda rin po ang nasabi ninyo kanina, yung system of pre-positioning your assets before it's a disaster strike na kanina,

**[05:53:05.440] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004849)  
rather than bringing them only after the calamity.

**[05:53:12.080] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004850)  
Kaya po, nonetheless, Secretary, congratulations po sa DND family for a job well done.

**[05:53:15.680] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004851)  
And for this, I strongly support the approval of the DND budget for the year 2027. Thank you, Mr. Chair, Madam Chair.

**[05:53:22.844] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004852)  
We thank the distinguished representative from Bulacan, a friend of longstanding, and although an adopted son of Bulacan,

**[05:53:34.690] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004853)  
[unclear] taga-Amianan [unclear] umay-ayos [unclear] po, [unclear] puso na po.

**[05:53:39.466] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004855)  
[unclear] po. Agyaman kami nga unay.

**[05:53:43.837] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004856)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[05:53:46.148] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004857)  
Thank you, Kong Tito. Meeting is suspended for a few minutes.

**[06:35:04.558] Rep. Bella Vanessa B. Suansing** (asr-004858)  
We would like to recognize Chairperson Mika Suansing.

**[06:35:08.958] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004858-split-2)  
Thank you very much, Vice Chair Bella Suansing.

**[06:35:34.718] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004862)  
hopefully, the—the—the DND family is happy with your budget sponsor. [unclear].

**[06:35:44.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004863)

**[06:35:49.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004864)  
until we defend the—the budget of the Department of National Defense. Um, Sec,

**[06:35:55.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004865)

**[06:36:25.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004870)

**[06:36:39.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004872)

**[06:36:45.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004873)
