**[06:36:53.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004874)  
Budget Amendments Review

**[06:36:56.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004875)  
Subcommittee. We will be deliberating on all of these requests po, uh, uh, openly, uh, line by line, um, in—in—in the

**[06:37:03.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004876)  
next few weeks. So, Sec, if I may ask po, um, if there are any priority projects or programs that were left

**[06:37:11.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004877)  
out from the NEP that you would like us to consider po for—for restoration in the HGAB.

**[06:37:17.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004878)  
Madam Chair, of immediate concern would be the capital outlays that, uh, were reduced from us because, as you know,

**[06:37:25.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004879)  
on the Department level, uh, also on the, uh, uh, AFP level, any item of, uh, equipment we receive needs the

**[06:37:36.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004880)  
appropriate capital outlay to support the infrastructure. On the DND, uh, we had to vacate

**[06:37:42.798] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004881)  
our building, which is 70 years old, and everybody was getting sick. So we, uh—the capital outlay was reduced,

**[06:37:50.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004882)  
and it will also impact the timeline for the

**[06:37:55.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004892)  
extension and the, uh, restoration of the legacy building, which we cannot touch

**[06:38:02.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004893)  
because of its, uh, historic, uh, value. Uh, and the rest of the needs will be, uh, communicated

**[06:38:11.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004894)  
to the, uh, distinguished chairperson and vice chairperson by, uh, Undersecretary [unclear].

**[06:38:20.482] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004895)  
Thank you very much po. If I may request po for a—for formal letter, um, coming from the Department of National Defense

**[06:38:27.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004896)  
um, so that I may submit that for the consideration of the Budget Amendments

**[06:38:33.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004897)  
Review Subcommittee. If possible po, Sec, if we could have the list prioritized already because the

**[06:38:39.600] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004898)  
worst that could happen is we’ll give additional funding to the Department of National Defense

**[06:38:43.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004899)  
and then the program will be wrong and we’ll be able to fund additional.

**[06:38:48.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004900)  
So,

**[06:38:48.558] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004901)  
if, um, sorry, Sec, but if it would be possible to receive that within the week, if—if—

**[06:38:55.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004902)  
if possible, because we’re doing—we’re convening the BARC, um, in a week or so, uh, para po maipasok

**[06:39:03.120] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004903)  
na po namin, um, list of institutional amendment requests for us to consider.

**[06:39:06.618] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-004904)  
Your Honor, within the week.

**[06:39:08.187] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004905)  
Thank you very much po.

**[06:39:11.039] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004906)  
Um, yeah, I believe after—after five—five hours of deliberations of the Department of National Defense,

**[06:39:18.718] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004907)  
um, on our part, uh, again, with all of our colleagues here, uh, I—I just want to say thank you po to—to DND

**[06:39:26.478] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004908)  
and all of our uniformed personnel, uh, for all of your hard work, all—everything that you’re doing for our

**[06:39:33.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004909)  
country. And on our part po, the best way that we could thank our military

**[06:39:38.638] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004910)  
and uniformed personnel is to be able to give you the resources, um, that you would need, um, to be

**[06:39:45.360] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004911)  
able to perform your mandate

**[06:39:47.600] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004912)  
and, um, to be able to, uh, operate day-to-day.

**[06:39:50.638] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004913)  
So, on the part of the Committee on Appropriations and of Congress, that as

**[06:39:55.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004914)  
much as we can po, given the limited fiscal space, uh, we will help restore whatever

**[06:40:00.958] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004915)  
we can, um, once we refine the final version of the, um, HGAB.

**[06:40:07.440] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004916)  
So, that’s it.

**[06:40:08.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004917)  
I just really wanted to come here and manifest my support, um, to the DND family and all of our

**[06:40:14.638] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004918)  
military and, uh—and uniformed personnel.

**[06:40:17.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004919)  
And from the bottom of my heart, thank you for everything you’re doing.

**[06:40:19.980] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004920)  
Thank you for all the good that you’re doing for our country and our countrymen.

**[06:40:23.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004921)  
Maraming, maraming salamat po.

**[06:40:26.318] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004922)

Um, that would be my manifestation po.

**[06:40:37.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-004927)  
Um, again, thank you very much po to the DND family.

Thank you, Chairperson Mika Suansing.
